We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Overcoming Forgetting in LLM Fine-Tuning with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30148)
*Kajetan Schweighofer,Conor F. Hayes,Roberto Dailey,Risto Miikkulainen,Xin Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现ES微调LLM时对旧任务的“遗忘”实为可恢复的性能漂移，而非不可逆遗忘；这种漂移源于ES在弱约束方向上的随机游走行为；提出的锚定权重衰减（AWD）正则化技术能有效稳定旧任务性能，同时保持新任务效果，使ES成为LLM持续学习的有前景方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近研究表明ES微调新任务可能导致对旧任务的遗忘，但该现象的本质和机制尚不清楚。本文旨在澄清这一遗忘现象的真实性质，并寻找有效的解决方案。

Method: 首先通过实验对比分析证明遗忘实为可恢复的性能漂移，并证明该现象在RL微调中同样存在。其次，从ES训练动力学角度分析漂移的成因，指出其与弱约束方向上的随机游走行为相关。最后提出锚定权重衰减（AWD）正则化技术，通过约束优化方向朝向初始模型参数来稳定性能。

Result: AWD能有效稳定旧任务性能，同时保持新任务效果，以更低计算成本实现与大ES种群规模相当的收益。

Conclusion: 与先前认知相反，ES下的旧任务遗忘在很大程度上是可以避免的，ES可作为LLM持续学习的有前景方法。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) has recently emerged as a competitive alternative to reinforcement learning (RL) for large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, offering advantages through simplicity, scalability, and inference-only training. However, recent work suggests that ES fine-tuning on new tasks may induce forgetting of prior tasks. First, this paper shows that prior task forgetting (1) is better characterized as performance drift rather than irreversible forgetting, with prior-task performance often recovering during ES training; and (2) is not a specific failure mode of ES, but can also arise for fine-tuning with RL methods. Second, it analyzes when and why such drift arises, highlighting its dependence on ES training dynamics, particularly random walk behavior in weakly constrained directions of the weight space. Third, based on these insights, it introduces Anchored Weight Decay (AWD) as a parameter-space regularization technique that constrains optimization toward the initial model parameters. AWD effectively stabilizes prior-task performance while preserving target-task performance, achieving benefits comparable to large ES population sizes at much lower computational cost. Thus, contrary to previous beliefs, the paper shows that prior-task forgetting under ES is largely avoidable, positioning ES as a promising approach for continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [113] [On Distributional Reinforcement Learning in Chaotic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30160)
*James Rudd-Jones,Mirco Musolesi,María Pérez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在混沌动力系统中，尽管单个轨迹对初始条件敏感，但收益分布（在1-Wasserstein度量下）的演化比单轨迹更规则，因此分布强化学习（Distributional RL）通过优化分布目标而非标量期望值，能提供更优的梯度条件，从而缓解混沌带来的高方差和不良条件化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌动力系统（如流体、气候、多智能体系统）对初始条件呈指数敏感，导致强化学习中bootstrap目标高方差、梯度更新条件不良，标准RL方法通过标量值函数平均发散轨迹，将轨迹级不稳定性与学习目标纠缠，亟需更稳定的学习范式。

Method: 在弱统计稳定性假设下，证明收益分布（return distribution）在1-Wasserstein度量下的演化比单个轨迹更规则，从而得到更平滑的分布Bellman目标；通过将优化对齐到度量层结构，分布RL获得更好的梯度条件。

Result: 分布RL在混沌系统中提供更优条件化的学习，解释了分布方法在混沌场景下的优势以及混沌下RL目标的几何特性。

Conclusion: 分布RL通过利用收益分布的度量结构，能有效对抗混沌动态带来的学习不稳定性，为在混沌系统中应用RL提供了理论依据和更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Chaotic dynamical systems pose a fundamental challenge for Reinforcement Learning (RL): exponential sensitivity to initial conditions induces high-variance bootstrap targets and poorly conditioned gradient updates. Chaotic dynamics arise across scientific and engineering domains, from fluid flows and climate systems to multi-agent systems, where reliable learning is highly desirable. Standard RL methods optimise expected returns through scalar value functions, implicitly averaging over diverging trajectories and entangling trajectory level instability with the learning objective. We show that under mild statistical stability assumptions, the return distribution evolves more regularly than individual trajectories when measured under the $1$-Wasserstein metric, yielding a smoother distributional Bellman objective. By aligning optimisation with this measure level structure, distributional RL provides better conditioned learning. We offer a principled explanation for the advantages of distributional methods in chaotic systems and the geometries of RL objectives under chaos.

</details>


### [114] [iLoRA: Bayesian Low-Rank Adaptation with Latent Interaction Graphs for Microbiome Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30179)
*Yang Song,Yixuan Zhang,Lingfa Meng,Tongyuan Hu,Haizhou Shi,Hao Wang,Samir Bhatt,Hengguan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出iLoRA，首个贝叶斯图条件低秩适应框架，通过从输入推断潜在交互图并生成输入条件的LoRA更新，实现预测与潜在交互结构的联合学习。在微生物组诊断任务中，iLoRA在交互式QA和多队列IBD诊断两个场景下均优于强基线，恢复的图与人工标注和队列级微生物组关联一致，并提供校准的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA使用静态低秩更新，无法捕捉科学标签中常存在的潜在交互结构；现有方法将预测器训练与交互分析分离（先训练再事后分析），缺乏联合学习能力。

Method: iLoRA首先从输入数据推断潜在交互图，然后利用该图生成输入条件的动态LoRA更新，从而在训练过程中联合优化预测性能和潜在图结构，实现端到端学习。

Result: 在交互式QA（含人工标注图）和多队列IBD诊断两个场景中，iLoRA均优于强LoRA和贝叶斯适应基线；恢复的图与人工标注及队列级微生物组关联高度一致；提供校准的不确定性估计，且图分支开销适中。

Conclusion: iLoRA通过贝叶斯图条件框架有效结合了参数高效微调与结构发现，在微生物组诊断领域验证了其预测性能和结构恢复能力，为科学领域中的潜在交互建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient adaptation has made LLMs practical for domain prediction, but standard LoRA still relies on a static low-rank update and does not expose the latent interactions that often drive scientific labels. We introduce iLoRA. To our knowledge, it is the first Bayesian graph-conditioned LoRA framework. It infers a latent interaction graph from the input and uses it to generate input-conditioned LoRA updates. As a result, iLoRA learns prediction and latent interaction structure jointly, rather than training a predictor and applying interaction analysis only post hoc. We instantiate this idea for microbiome diagnosis, where disease state can depend on both species-level abundance and microbe-microbe cross-talk, and evaluate it in two complementary settings: interactive QA with human-annotated graphs, which tests latent structure recovery, and multi-cohort IBD diagnosis, which tests biomedical utility. Across both settings, iLoRA improves over strong LoRA and Bayesian adaptation baselines, recovers graphs aligned with human annotations and cohort-level microbiome associations, and provides calibrated uncertainty with moderate graph-branch overhead.

</details>


### [115] [Can AI Weather Models Predict Beyond Two Weeks? A Quantitative Benchmark and Analysis of Long Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30184)
*Fanny Lehmann,Firat Ozdemir,Yun Cheng,Torsten Hoefler,Sebastian Schemm,Benedikt Soja,Siddhartha Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对AI气象模型长期滚动预测中出现的“不稳定性”进行了系统分类，将故障分为爆炸、漂移和季节性丧失三种类型，并通过九个模型的全年滚动实验证实，稳定性取决于对小尺度时空特征的处理方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI气象模型在短期（最长15天）预测中表现优异，但长期滚动预测时常常出现定义不清的“不稳定性”，缺乏正式的故障分类体系。

Method: 对九个最先进的AI气象模型进行全年滚动预测，将失败模式归类为爆炸（blow-up）、漂移（drift）和季节性丧失（loss of seasonality）三类；通过消融实验验证架构设计选择对稳定性的影响，并使用Vision Transformer (ViT) 架构进行实验。

Result: 稳定性取决于模型对小尺度时空特征的处理：不稳定模型会放大高频能量，而稳定模型在输入添加噪声时起到去噪器的作用。稳定模型并非简单的“随机鹦鹉”，而是能够生成依赖于初始状态条件的独特天气轨迹。

Conclusion: 明确了AI气象模型长期预测不稳定的分类学，揭示了稳定性的关键机制在于高频能量处理方式，并为改进模型架构设计提供了指导方向。

Abstract: While AI weather models excel at short-to-medium range forecasts (up to 15 days), they frequently suffer from ill-defined "instabilities" when rolled out over longer horizons. This work addresses the lack of a formal taxonomy by categorizing these failures into three distinct regimes: blow-up, drift, and loss of seasonality, through year-long rollouts of nine state-of-the-art AI weather models. Our analysis reveals that stability hinges on the treatment of small spatio-temporal scales: unstable models amplify high-frequency energy, while stable models act as denoisers when noise is added to their inputs. Far from reducing these models to mere stochastic parrots, our findings highlight that stable models generate unique weather trajectories, conditioned on the initial state. We verify our findings through ablation studies on architectural design choices, conducted using state-of-the-art Vision Transformer (ViT) AI weather model architectures.

</details>


### [116] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了迄今为止最大规模的后验校准基准测试，涵盖近2000个实验，系统比较了数十种校准方法。研究发现：平滑校准函数优于基于分箱的方法，专用多类方法在高维场景中至关重要，缺乏校准专用设计的通用机器学习模型缺乏竞争力。同时提出了基于Proper Scoring Rules的后验改进(PHI)指标作为校准误差估计的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分类器往往校准不良，而后验校准方法众多但缺乏大规模、标准化的系统比较，导致难以确定哪些方法在实践中真正有效。

Method: 构建大规模标准化基准，涵盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务中的二分类、多分类和大规模分类场景，聚合传统模型、深度学习架构和基础模型的预测结果，在统一框架下实现数十种校准方法，并提出PHI指标用于比较。

Result: 平滑校准函数优于分箱方法；专用多类方法在高维场景中不可或缺；通用机器学习模型缺乏校准专用设计时竞争力不足。

Conclusion: 提供了迄今为止最全面的后验校准实证研究，释放了所有数据、代码和评估工具，为未来校准方法研究提供了即插即用的基准平台。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [117] [Mean-Field Diffuser: Scaling Offline MARL to Thousands of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30190)
*Wenhao Li,Xiangfeng Wang,Bo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MF-Diffuser 将扩散规划扩展到多智能体系统，通过将轨迹规划提升到 Wasserstein 分布空间，利用混沌传播用少量代表智能体捕获整体动力学，并引入值加权混沌熵目标与层次化粗到细生成策略，理论证明均场近似误差为 O(H²/√N) 且离线分布偏移不随群体规模增长，在三个均场 RL 基准上取得最优回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的规划在单智能体离线强化学习中表现优异，但扩展到多智能体系统时面临联合轨迹空间的维度灾难问题，现有方法难以处理大量智能体的规划任务。

Method: 提出 MF-Diffuser 框架，核心包括：(1) 将轨迹规划提升到轨迹分布的 Wasserstein 空间，利用混沌传播定理保证少量代表智能体即可捕获完整群体动力学；(2) 值加权混沌熵目标，兼顾生成保真度与回报最大化；(3) 层次化粗到细策略，在去噪过程中逐步增加智能体群体规模。

Result: 理论方面建立了包含四项可解释项的端到端次优性界，均场近似误差为 O(H²/√N)，离线分布偏移不随群体规模 N 增长，生成的策略是近似均场纳什均衡。实验在三个均场 RL 基准（阶段博弈、序贯动力学、对抗团队竞争）上，MF-Diffuser 在大多数设置中取得最佳回报，在次优离线数据和大规模（N≥10³）场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: MF-Diffuser 成功将扩散规划扩展到大规模多智能体系统，通过均场近似和层次化生成策略有效缓解了维度灾难，理论保证与实验验证均表明该方法在处理大规模离线多智能体规划问题上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Diffusion-based planning has achieved strong results in single-agent offline reinforcement learning, yet scaling to many-agent systems remains intractable due to the curse of dimensionality in the joint trajectory space. We introduce MF-Diffuser, a framework that lifts trajectory planning to the Wasserstein space of trajectory distributions, where the propagation of chaos ensures a small representative subset of agents captures the full population dynamics. Our approach features a value-weighted chaotic entropy objective that reconciles generative fidelity with return maximization, and a hierarchical coarse-to-fine strategy that progressively grows the agent population during denoising. We establish end-to-end suboptimality bounds with four interpretable terms, revealing that mean-field approximation error scales as $O(H^2/\sqrt{N})$ while offline distribution shift provably does not grow with population size $N$, and prove the generated policy is an approximate mean-field Nash equilibrium with explicit convergence guarantees. Experiments on three mean-field RL benchmarks -- spanning stage games, sequential dynamics, and adversarial team competition -- show MF-Diffuser achieves the best return in the majority of settings, with the largest gains on suboptimal offline data and at extreme scales ($N \geq 10^3$).

</details>


### [118] [HPO: Hysteretic Policy Optimization for Stable and Efficient Training under Sparse-Reward Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30201)
*Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Antonio De Domenico,Fadhel Ayed,Haozhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GRPO风格强化学习在稀疏可验证奖励下的一种常见失败模式——早期更新中负优势响应多于正优势响应，且响应级长度归一化将更新幅度与输出长度绑定——提出了迟滞策略优化（HPO），通过减少负优势更新的权重并用均值长度归一化替代逐响应长度归一化。进一步引入自适应HPO（A-HPO），基于批次级优势符号统计自动设置迟滞权重。实验表明A-HPO在奖励效率和最终性能上显著优于GRPO等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO风格强化学习在稀疏可验证奖励场景下存在一种常见但被忽视的失败模式：早期训练阶段负优势响应多于正优势响应，同时响应级长度归一化使得长响应的梯度更新幅度被放大，导致训练不稳定和效率低下。

Method: 提出HPO：对GRPO进行最小化修改，一是引入迟滞机制降低负优势更新的权重，二是用均值长度归一化替代逐响应长度归一化。进一步提出A-HPO，根据批次内优势的正负符号统计自动调整迟滞权重，消除手动调参需求。

Result: 在TeleLogs任务上，A-HPO最终奖励达0.84，比SAPO高5%、比GSPO高11%、比GRPO高15%，且响应长度相当。在Countdown任务上，1.5B-7B模型在初始和最困难配置下增益最大。消融实验表明增益来自正负优势的更好平衡。

Conclusion: A-HPO通过在稀疏奖励的早期阶段更有效地平衡正负优势更新，显著提升了GRPO风格强化学习的训练效率和最终性能，且无需额外超参数调优。

Abstract: We investigate a narrow but common failure mode of GRPO-style reinforcement learning in the context of sparse verifiable rewards: early updates contain more responses with negative advantages than those with positive advantages, while response-level length normalization ties the magnitude of the update to the length of the output. We propose Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO), a minimal modification of GRPO that reduces the weight of negative-advantage updates and replaces per-response length normalization with mean-length normalization. We further introduce Adaptive HPO (A-HPO), which sets the hysteretic weight based on batch-level advantage-sign statistics, thereby removing the need for tuning a fixed hysteretic weight. In our TeleLogs and Countdown experiments, A-HPO improves the reward per update compared to GRPO, with the largest gains in early sparse reward regimes. On TeleLogs, A-HPO achieves a final reward of 0.84, outperforming SAPO by 5%, GSPO by 11%, and GRPO by 15%, while maintaining a comparable response-length. On Countdown, A-HPO achieves the largest gains in initial and most difficult configurations across 1.5B-7B models. Ablation studies on the hysteretic weight show that the gains of A-HPO come from better balancing the contributions of positive and negative advantages compared to positive-only or fully symmetric updates.

</details>


### [119] [Faithful Embeddings of Irregular and Asynchronous Data for Online Log-NCDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30213)
*Benjamin Walker,Alexandre Bloch,Lingyi Yang,Sam Morley,Terry Lyons*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在连续时间模型中，离散观测的插值/插补重构步骤并非必要；只要嵌入是连续且单射的，模型在输入空间的紧集普适性即可迁移到数据空间。基于此，作者为Log-NCDE设计了一种连续单射的嵌入方法，通过将观测记录为增量并在任意查询区间上直接组合成log-signatures，避免了变量插值，支持在线计算，在合成和真实数据上表现准确、高效且鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间模型通常需要对离散观测进行插值或插补重构，这使模型对重构方式敏感，且可能引入不必要的偏差。作者希望证明这种重构步骤并非必要，并设计一种无需插值的嵌入方法。

Method: 理论证明：在连续单射嵌入条件下，输入空间的紧集普适性可迁移到数据空间。方法实现：基于rectilinear control path，设计了一种连续单射嵌入，将观测记录为增量，在任意查询区间上直接组合成log-signatures，避免插值。

Result: 在合成受控动力学和真实世界时间序列数据集上，该方法在准确性、效率和鲁棒性方面均优于基线，尤其对不规则、异步和稀疏观测表现良好。

Conclusion: 连续时间模型中的插值重构并非必要，所提出的基于log-signatures的嵌入方法是一种更简洁、高效且理论上合理的替代方案。

Abstract: Continuous-time models are a natural choice for irregular and asynchronous data. A central design choice is how to embed discrete observations into continuous time. Interpolation- and imputation-based embeddings reconstruct a continuous observation path, making the model sensitive to the choice of reconstruction. We show that this reconstruction step is unnecessary; under mild conditions, compact-set universality on the model input space transfers to the data space whenever the embedding from data to input is continuous and injective. Guided by this result, and building on the rectilinear control path for Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs), we introduce a continuous and injective embedding for Log-NCDEs, a universal class of continuous-time models. Our approach records observations as increments and composes them over arbitrary query intervals to directly form log-signatures. This provides interval-level summaries without first interpolating the observed variables, while supporting online computation. Experiments on synthetic controlled dynamics and real-world time-series datasets show that the representation is accurate, efficient, and robust to irregular, asynchronous, and sparse observations.

</details>


### [120] [TriSearch: Learning to Optimize Triangulations via Bistellar Flips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30220)
*Yiran Wang,Guido Montúfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriSearch是一个基于强化学习的框架，利用电路支撑的子三角剖分动作表示和双星翻转，高效遍历多面体三角剖分图，在3D和4D中零样本泛化到更大实例，在4D中发现了比现有采样器更多的独特Fine、Regular、Star三角剖分，对应Calabi-Yau三维簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多面体三角剖分空间随维度指数增长，现有方法难以高效搜索和优化；需要一种维度无关的、可泛化的框架来优化三角剖分上的目标函数。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的TriSearch框架，核心是电路支撑的子三角剖分动作表示：将可行翻转编码为其支撑电路和局部子三角剖分，学习策略利用局部几何和组合特征对翻转进行排序，实现维度无关的接口，无需显式枚举整个三角剖分空间即可高效遍历翻转图。

Result: 在3D中，TriSearch在度量目标上达到顶级性能；在4D中，在固定预算下，TriSearch发现了比现有采样器更多的独特Fine、Regular、Star三角剖分（对应Calabi-Yau三维簇），且从小型训练实例零样本泛化到搜索空间指数级增长的更大多面体。

Conclusion: TriSearch提供了一种高效、可泛化的三角剖分空间搜索框架，在几何和组合优化中具有广泛应用前景，尤其在代数几何中的Calabi-Yau三角剖分发现方面表现突出。

Abstract: We introduce TriSearch, a reinforcement learning framework for optimizing objectives over triangulations of a polytope via bistellar flips. The key idea is a circuit-supported subtriangulation action representation: feasible flips are encoded by their supporting circuit and realized local subtriangulation, enabling a learned policy to rank them using local geometric and combinatorial features. This yields a dimension-agnostic interface and enables efficient traversal of the flip graph without explicit enumeration of the full triangulation space. Instantiated in 3D and 4D, TriSearch generalizes zero-shot from small training instances to larger polytopes with exponentially larger search spaces. It achieves top performance on metric objectives in 3D and, in 4D, discovers more distinct Fine, Regular, Star triangulations of reflexive polytopes, corresponding to Calabi-Yau threefolds, than existing samplers under a fixed budget.

</details>


### [121] [ExDBSCAN: Explaining DBSCAN with Counterfactual Reasoning -- Additional Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30225)
*Pernille Matthews,Lena Krieger,Tommaso Amico,Artur Zimek,Thomas Seidl,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DBSCAN聚类缺乏可解释性的问题，提出了一种基于密度感知的事后解释方法ExDBSCAN，通过生成具有理论保证的反事实解释来阐明数据点为何被分配为内点或离群点，实验证明其在有效性、多样性和邻近性上优于四个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类作为无监督学习方法缺乏可解释性，尤其是DBSCAN虽然能区分内点和离群点，但无法解释单个点的分配原因及其对数据扰动的鲁棒性，存在可解释性缺口。

Method: 提出ExDBSCAN，一种密度感知的事后解释方法。构建密度连接加权图，采用物理启发模型——通过排斥力促进反事实候选的多样性，同时通过吸引力保持与待解释实例的邻近性，生成具有理论保证有效性的多重反事实解释。

Result: 在30个表格数据集上与四个基线方法比较，ExDBSCAN在所有指标上均优于基线，实现了完美的有效性，并能检索到多样且邻近的反事实。

Conclusion: ExDBSCAN成功填补了DBSCAN聚类可解释性的空白，提供了一种有效、多样且邻近的反事实解释方法，为密度聚类的可解释性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised technique for grouping data points by similarity. While explainability methods exist for supervised machine learning, they are not directly applicable to clustering, making it challenging to understand cluster assignments. This interpretability gap is particularly evident in the popular density-based method DBSCAN, which assigns points as inliers (cluster members in dense regions) or outliers (noise points in sparse regions). DBSCAN does not provide insight into why a particular point receives its assignment or whether its assignment is robust to small changes in the data. To address the lack of explainability, we introduce ExDBSCAN, a density-aware, post-hoc explanation method. ExDBSCAN offers actionable counterfactual explanations, with theoretical guarantees for validity. It generates multiple counterfactuals using a density connected weighted graph, adopting a physics-inspired model that repels counterfactual candidates from one another (diversity), while pulling them toward the instance to explain (proximity). Empirical evaluation on 30 tabular datasets comparing against four baselines shows that ExDBSCAN outperforms all baselines while attaining perfect validity and retrieving diverse, proximal counterfactuals.

</details>


### [122] [Anti Mode-Collapse in Mean-Field Transformer via Auxiliary Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30229)
*Masaaki Imaizumi,Masanori Koyama,Noboru Isobe,Kohei Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过平均场理论分析Transformer模型，证明位置编码等辅助变量能防止自注意力机制中的模态坍缩（token分布退化为单点），并揭示其在极限下具备分布表示的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场Transformer模型分析表明，在深层推理中token分布会退化为单点（模态坍缩），这与实际Transformer的表现不符。因此需要探究什么机制阻止了这一理论上的坍缩。

Method: 采用平均场Transformer模型进行理论分析，引入辅助变量（位置编码、固定Prompt插入）作为并行机制，通过能量最大化分布的分析证明分布不会坍缩到Dirac测度，而是辅助变量分布的pushforward。

Result: 1) 辅助变量（位置编码、Prompt插入）能有效防止理论上的模态坍缩；2) 极限分布具有表示普适性，可精确表示广泛分布类；3) 验证了位置编码的若干关键性质和亚稳态特性。

Conclusion: 位置编码和Prompt插入不仅是工程技巧，更是防止模态坍缩、保证表示能力的理论基础，为理解Transformer深层次工作机制提供了数学依据。

Abstract: We use a mean-field-based transformer model to theoretically investigate how auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding, prevent mode collapse of self-attention mechanisms. The use of mean-field transformers to analyze the properties of self-attention mechanisms has garnered significant attention in recent years due to their ability to comprehensively analyze token interactions. However, analysis of this simple model suggests that mode collapse, where token distributions degenerate to a single point, occurs during long inferences (i.e., many layers), indicating a discrepancy with reality. This study investigates this mean-field transformer model and demonstrates that the introduction of auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding, acts as a counterforce against theoretical mode collapse. Specifically, we show that in the theoretical scheme, the energy-maximizing distribution does not degenerate to a single point; instead, it is characterized by a pushforward of the auxiliary variable distribution, thereby avoiding concentration in the Dirac measure. Our main examples are the positional encoding and the fixed prompt insertion treated as a parallel auxiliary-variable mechanism. Furthermore, we demonstrate that positional encoding and prompt insertion possess universality of representation in the limit, meaning that the limit distribution of inference can exactly represent a wide class of distributions. We also analyze several key properties of positional encoding and metastability, and validate our theoretical results through mathematical experiments.

</details>


### [123] [How's it going? Reinforcement learning in language models recruits a functional welfare axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30232)
*Andy Q Han,David J. Chalmers,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习会招募语言模型中预先存在的"功能性福祉"表征——即系统对自身目标达成状况的估计。通过在语义中立的迷宫环境训练模型，提取奖励/惩罚轨迹的概念向量，发现惩罚向量表现为负面福祉表征（促进失败标记、与负面情绪对齐、负向追踪目标达成、诱导自我否定等），奖励向量则相反，两者几乎反平行。这些效应在多种控制条件下稳健，且在迷宫训练前就已存在，表明该福祉轴是预先存在的，而非后训练创造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强化学习如何塑造语言模型的内部表征，特别是验证RL是否招募了模型中本已存在的"功能性福祉"表征，而非创造新的表征。

Method: 在语义中立的迷宫环境中训练多个语言模型，提取奖励轨迹和惩罚轨迹的概念向量，然后在与迷宫环境无关的设置中评估这些向量对模型行为的影响，并进行多种控制实验（包括tile-to-reward映射、规模、指令微调、RL算法、模型家族、LoRA vs全参数微调，以及用SFT替代RL）。

Result: 惩罚向量表现出负面福祉表征特征（促进失败和不可能性标记、与负面情绪概念对齐、负向追踪目标达成、通过引导产生负面自我报告、病态回溯、拒绝和不确定性）；正奖励向量表现为镜像对称，两者近乎反平行；这些效应在多种控制条件下稳健；在经历迷宫训练前就已有效。

Conclusion: 功能性福祉轴在模型中预先存在，后训练（包括RL和SFT）只是招募而非创建这一表征。该发现对可解释性、后训练动态和对齐研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: How does reinforcement learning shape a language model's internal representations? We present evidence that RL recruits a pre-existing representation of functional welfare: an estimate of how well or badly the system is doing, relative to its goals. We train several language models in a novel, semantically neutral maze environment. We then extract concept vectors for rewarded and punished trajectories, and evaluate those vectors in settings unrelated to the maze environment. The punishment vector behaves like a representation of negative welfare: it promotes failure and impossibility tokens, it aligns with negative emotion concepts, it negatively tracks goal-achievement, and steering with it induces negative self-reports, pathological backtracking, refusal, and uncertainty. The positive reward vector behaves as the mirror image, and the two are nearly antiparallel. These effects are robust when controlling for tile-to-reward mapping, scale, instruct tuning, RL training algorithm, model family, and LoRA versus full-finetuning, and largely persist when we replace RL with supervised fine-tuning. Importantly, the vectors are effective in models before they have undergone maze training. Combined with observations that the effects also appear in pretrain-only models, we therefore argue that this functional welfare axis pre-exists post-training: it is recruited, rather than created, by post-training. While we make no claims about any experience of welfare, the axis offers a demonstration that minimal reward signals can broadly affect model behavior by recruiting pre-existing welfare-like representations, with implications for interpretability, post-training dynamics, and alignment.

</details>


### [124] [OOD-GraphLLM: Graph Large Language Model for Out-of-Distribution Generalized Drug Synergy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30247)
*Xin Wang,Linxin Xiao,Yang Yao,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出利用图大语言模型（GraphLLM）解决药物协同预测中的分布外（O.O.D.）泛化问题，通过联合优化分子图表示与生物医学语义语言表示，并采用检索增强的生物医学指令微调策略，提出OOD-GraphLLM框架和DrugSyn-LLM模型，实现O.O.D.场景下的准确药物协同预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物协同预测方法依赖同分布（I.D.）假设，无法处理新型化合物不断涌现导致的分子拓扑结构分布外（O.O.D.）偏移问题。

Method: 提出OOD-GraphLLM框架，联合优化分子图表示和生物医学语义语言表示；微调DrugSyn-LLM生物医学大语言模型；采用检索增强的生物医学指令微调策略对齐分子拓扑信息、语义信息与语言推理。

Result: 在O.O.D.设置下能够准确预测药物协同效应，代码和模型已开源并支持Web交互使用。

Conclusion: 通过GraphLLM统一分子结构与语义信息，可有效解决药物协同预测中的分布外泛化问题。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction (DSP) aims to identify efficacious drug combinations under various cellular contexts with different targets. However, the continual emergence of novel compounds results in variations in molecular scaffolds and sizes, causing drug synergy data to exhibit out-of-distribution (O.O.D.) shifts with respect to topological structure. Existing works rely on in-distribution (I.D.) assumption, failing to handle the O.O.D. shifts. To solve this problem, we study out-of-distribution generalized drug synergy prediction through a graph large language model for the first time. Nevertheless, O.O.D. generalized DSP is highly non-trivial, posing several challenges: i) how to discover structurally relevant and irrelevant molecular representations with respect to cell targets; ii) how to find the optimal graph neural architectures that accurately calculate molecular representations; and iii) how to jointly leverage molecular structural and semantic information in LLMs. To address these challenges, we propose OOD-GraphLLM, a novel graphLLM framework which is able to accurately predict drug synergy under O.O.D. settings via jointly optimizing molecular graph representation and biomedical semantic language representations in a unified manner. Furthermore, we finetune DrugSyn-LLM, a biomedical LLM, and employ a retrieval-augmented biomedical instruction tuning strategy to align molecular topological information and molecular semantic information with language-based reasoning for O.O.D. generalized DSP. Both the source code (https://github.com/EkkoXiao/Bio-GraphLLM) and released model (https://mn.cs.tsinghua.edu.cn/bio-graphllm/) are publicly available, where users are allowed to download model resources and interactively use the system through a web interface.

</details>


### [125] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过结构化EDA描述符、句子嵌入和典型相关分析（CCA）来跨数据集对齐和检索异构数值表格数据的方法，在15个数据集上达到P@1=0.9，支持差分隐私，可用于RAG管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型缺乏在异构特征空间中有效表示数值数据集的机制，现有方法要么需要共享变量定义，要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐能力。

Method: 1) 通过结构化探索性数据分析描述符表征数值表格数据集；2) 利用预训练句子变换器将描述符嵌入共享向量空间；3) 使用典型相关分析（CCA）量化跨数据集相似性；4) 应用带惩罚的CCA恢复稀疏、可解释的变量级对应关系；5) 可选地在嵌入前对描述符集施加差分隐私保护。

Result: 在15个数据集（通用基准、材料信息学、核级石墨表征）上，总体P@1分数达0.9，最近邻检索和聚类结构在嵌入消融和差分隐私预算下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该框架为将异构数值数据集成到检索增强生成管道中提供了原则性路径，同时保留了统计上下文，可直接应用于数据驱动的算法选择和未知数据集的仿真模型初始化。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [126] [Self-Trained Verification for Training- and Test-Time Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30290)
*Chen Henry Wu,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自训练验证（STV）方法，通过让验证器模仿参考解决方案指导下的自身版本来解决自我改进中的验证瓶颈；同时提出验证器在环训练（ViL），利用STV验证器的反馈在V-R循环中对生成器进行强化学习训练，显著提升推理模型在难题上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型自我改进面临验证瓶颈：测试时的验证-精炼循环因验证器分数膨胀而停滞，训练时的自训练因引入自生成的错误数据而失败。现有方法无法有效训练验证器捕获自生成错误，因为缺乏训练信号。

Method: 1) STV：利用模型看到参考解决方案时能识别错误但单独不能的对称性，通过不对称性构造监督目标，训练验证器模仿更知情版本的自身。2) ViL：在STV验证器的反馈指导下，在V-R循环中对生成器进行强化学习训练。

Result: STV在难题上大幅提升V-R循环效果，在硬数学任务上准确率翻倍，在科学推理任务上准确率从1.5%提升至21%（14倍提升）。ViL在RL收敛的生成器基础上，pass@1进一步提升33%，且生成器独立pass@1相比标准RL收敛点提升30%。

Conclusion: 验证瓶颈可以通过不对称训练信号有效突破，未来推理模型在难题上的突破可能取决于如何训练验证器以及如何利用验证器进行训练。

Abstract: Self-improvement at scale has been a longstanding goal for reasoning models, and there are two natural places to do it: at test time, through verification-refinement (V-R) loops; and at training time, through self-training methods. Both are gated by the same bottleneck: the verifier. V-R loops stall when verifier scores inflate while accuracy stagnates, and when feedback is too generic to act on; self-training fails similarly when bad self-generated data are added to training. Better verification would unlock both, but the capability we want to train, i.e., catching self-generated errors, lacks training signal. To address this challenge, we propose self-trained verification (STV). Our key observation is that, while a model cannot catch these errors alone, it can when shown the reference solution. We turn this asymmetry into a supervision target and train the verifier to imitate a more informed version of itself. At test time, STV substantially improves V-R loops on hard problems, while alternatives (e.g., SFT, RL on verifier scores, and even meta-verifiers) do not. STV roughly doubles accuracy on hard math and lifts it 14x on scientific reasoning tasks (1.5% to 21%). At training time, we additionally train the generator using RL with STV verifier's feedback inside the V-R loop - a procedure we call verifier-in-the-loop training (ViL). Starting from an RL-converged generator, ViL yields a further 33% gain in pass@1. More notably, the generator's standalone pass@1, with no verifier at test time, climbs 30% relative past where standard RL had converged. Hence, the next frontier in reasoning on hard problems may lie in how we train for and with verification.

</details>


### [127] [In-Context Reward Adaptation for Robust Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30323)
*Zhenyu Sun,Zheng Xu,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出In-Context Reward Adaptation框架，利用Transformer的上下文学习能力，通过少量偏好示范自适应推断奖励结构，并引入人类响应时间作为辅助信号，实现对未见领域的偏好适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类价值观具有多样性和异质性，静态奖励模型难以泛化到未见偏好领域，现有多奖励框架局限于固定已知域且无法适应未见分布。

Method: 基于Transformer的上下文奖励适应框架：利用上下文学习能力从少量偏好示范中推断奖励结构；引入人类响应时间作为辅助输入信号解决标准Transformer的渐近偏差问题。

Result: 标准Transformer架构存在渐近偏差不足以完成该任务；引入人类响应时间作为辅助信号后，模型能成功适应来自未见领域的偏好。

Conclusion: 该方法为偏好建模提供了更鲁棒的基础，能够表示异质奖励和偏好分布偏移，为更灵活的人机对齐提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) typically relies on static reward models to align Large Language Models with human preferences. However, human values are inherently diverse and heterogeneous, and a single reward model often lacks the robustness required to generalize to unseen preference domains. While existing multi-reward frameworks attempt to address this, they are often restricted to a fixed set of known domains and fail to adapt to unseen human distributions without costly retraining. In this work, we propose In-Context Reward Adaptation, a transformer-based framework designed to model diverse and unseen human preferences on the fly. By leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of transformers, our approach adaptively infers the underlying reward structure from a small set of preference demonstrations. We demonstrate that while a standard transformer architecture is insufficient for this task by characterizing an asymptotic bias to the ground-truth, incorporating human response time as an auxiliary input signal enables the model to successfully adapt to preferences from previously unseen domains. Our findings show that this approach provides a more robust foundation for preference modeling, allowing for the representation of heterogeneous rewards and preference distribution shift, and offering a scalable path toward more flexible human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [128] [When, why, and how do diffusion posterior samplers fail? A finite-sample lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30330)
*Benjamin A. Burns,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从有限样本视角分析了扩散模型在逆问题后验采样中似然近似误差的传播机制，发现近似会导致后验分布过于集中或分散，进而引发对早停敏感、模式权重错误及幻觉等问题，并提出了一种与近似方法无关的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后验采样方法虽能在推理时融入任意测量模型，但在中间时间步需使用不精确的似然近似以保证计算可行性，这些近似的下游效应对采样后验的影响尚不明确，可能导致未解释的失败模式。

Method: 引入有限样本视角，将后验采样近似为训练集规模趋于无穷大时逼近任意精度的过程，通过该视角分析现有似然近似的误差传播，并设计与似然近似类型和前向模型类型无关的诊断工具。

Result: 发现流行的后验采样近似在中间时间步倾向于低估或高估后验的分散程度，导致对早停时间敏感、后验模式相对权重不准确、以及产生幻觉（包括先验中存在但后验中不存在的模式，以及似然支持但先验不支持的模态）。这些误差既不需要非线性测量模型也不需要多模态后验，仅由多模态先验和中间采样时刻不准确的后验分散度即可引发。

Conclusion: 有限样本后验采样方法可作为一种即插即用的诊断工具，用于评估现有及未来后验采样器的准确性和失败模式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excellent capacity to model complex distributions of natural data, which has made them a popular and effective choice for posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing methods can incorporate any measurement model at inference time but must use an inexact approximation for the likelihood at intermediate timesteps for computational tractability. Although these approximations can often work well empirically, their downstream effect on the sampled posterior is poorly understood and can result in unexplained failures. To understand when, why, and how these likelihood approximations propagate to erroneous posterior distributions, we introduce a finite-sample perspective on posterior sampling that approximates the posterior to arbitrary precision as training set size tends towards infinity, for any forward model and prior distribution. Using this finite-sample lens, we observe that popular posterior sampling approximations tend to under- or over-estimate the spread of the posterior at intermediate timesteps, causing downstream consequences including sensitivity to early stopping time, inaccurate relative weighting of posterior modes, and hallucination, both of prior modes that are not in the posterior and likelihood modes that are not supported by the prior. Moreover, we find that the cause of these posterior errors requires neither a nonlinear measurement model nor a multimodal posterior, but can arise solely due to a multimodal prior and inaccurate posterior spread at intermediate sampling times. Our finite-sample posterior sampling approach is agnostic to the type of likelihood approximation and the type of (linear or nonlinear) forward model, and can thus serve as a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate the accuracy and failure modes of existing and future posterior samplers.

</details>


### [129] [Fairness-Aware Federated Learning with Trajectory Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30336)
*Daniel Kuznetsov,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹夏普利值（TSV）的自适应联邦聚合方法FedTSV，通过动态评估客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的贡献，实现更公平、稳定和鲁棒的联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦聚合方案使用固定权重，无法反映客户端贡献的不平等性和时变性，导致训练有偏且不稳定，缺乏公平性。

Method: 提出轨迹夏普利值（TSV）作为贡献度量指标，基于验证集评估每个客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的影响，并将每轮TSV评估结果转化为动态客户端权重，实现自适应聚合。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，FedTSV加速了收敛，提升了鲁棒性，并使得贡献评估更加公平。

Conclusion: FedTSV为公平感知的联邦优化提供了有原则的基础，有效应对异构和对抗性参与场景。

Abstract: Federated learning is an emerging distributed paradigm that addresses the challenges posed by heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive data. It enables multiple clients to train a model collaboratively by aggregating their local updates at a server. However, conventional aggregation schemes typically use fixed weights that fail to reflect unequal and time-varying client contributions, leading to biased and unstable learning. To improve fairness and stability, we propose the Trajectory Shapley Value (TSV), a contribution metric that evaluates how each client influences the optimization trajectory of the global model using a validation-based, temporally consistent utility. Building on TSV, we design FedTSV, an adaptive aggregation method that converts per-round evaluations into dynamic client weights, allowing the server to respond to heterogeneous and adversarial participation in real time. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that FedTSV accelerates convergence, improves robustness, and yields more equitable contribution assessments, thereby providing a principled foundation for fairness-aware federated optimization.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Test-Time Finetuning of LLMs via Convex Reconstruction and Gradient Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30337)
*Alaa Khamis,Alaa Maalouf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HullFT是一种基于几何方法的测试时微调（TTFT）技术，通过Frank-Wolfe优化将查询表示为训练序列的稀疏凸组合，并利用几何整数化过程将分数权重转换为精确的整数多重集进行微调，同时通过梯度重用（Gradient Reuse）在重复微调步骤中摊销前向-反向计算，从而在更低的运行时间下实现更优的比特每字节（bits-per-byte）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTFT方法在速度和质量之间存在权衡：快速检索往往冗余，而强多样性感知的选择则带来高昂的每查询成本。TTFT的实际部署要求选择和微调两个环节都足够快。

Method: 1) 使用无投影的Frank-Wolfe优化将查询嵌入表示为少量训练序列的稀疏凸组合，获得内在相关且多样的支持集；2) 通过几何整数化过程将分数凸权重转换为精确的整数多重集用于微调；3) 利用梯度重用（Gradient Reuse）在重复微调步骤中摊销前向-反向计算。

Result: HullFT在质量-效率权衡上优于当前最先进的TTFT方法，在显著更低的总运行时间下实现了更低的比特每字节（bits-per-byte）。

Conclusion: HullFT通过几何方法同时解决了TTFT中检索选择和微调两个环节的速度瓶颈，为测试时微调的实际部署提供了一种更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time finetuning (TTFT) is a rapidly evolving paradigm that adapts a language model to each prompt by retrieving related sequences, updating the model on them, and then evaluating the prompt. However, TTFT is only practical if it is fast: selection and finetuning both happen per query, making each a direct bottleneck. Existing methods trade speed for quality: fast retrieval is often redundant, while stronger diversity-aware selection adds prohibitive per-query cost. We introduce HullFT, a geometric approach to TTFT that addresses both bottlenecks. Given a query, HullFT first represents the query embedding as a sparse convex combination of few training sequences, using efficient projection-free Frank-Wolfe optimization. This yields a support set that is inherently relevant and diverse. We then convert the fractional convex weights into an exact integer multiset for finetuning through a geometric integerization procedure. The resulting multiplicities naturally create repeated examples, which we exploit with Gradient Reuse to amortize forward-backward computation across repeated finetuning steps. Our experiments show that HullFT improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff over current state-of-the-art TTFT methods, achieving lower bits-per-byte at substantially lower total runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Echoes within the Reasoning: Stealthy and Effective Watermarking via Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28890)
*Jiacheng Lu,Yiming Li,Tao Song,Weijian Wang,Wenjie Qu,Haibing Guan,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BiCoT是一个将水印嵌入推理轨迹内部几何结构的黑盒水印框架，通过高显著性结构锚点与私有签名子空间对齐，并利用RSR验证器实现鲁棒检测，在保持推理保真度的同时抵抗微调、量化等攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒水印方法通常通过扰动最终答案或依赖脆弱的触发模式来嵌入水印，这在鲁棒性和推理保真度之间存在权衡。需要一种既能保护LLM推理能力知识产权，又不牺牲推理质量的水印方案。

Method: 提出BiCoT框架，核心包括：(1) 将高显著性结构锚点与私有签名子空间对齐，将所有权信号嵌入推理轨迹的内部几何结构；(2) 对普通控制令牌进行正则化以保持语义容量；(3) 提出Robust Subspace Registration (RSR)，一种基于Top-logprob的黑盒验证器，利用哨兵令牌校准输出分布的系统性偏移。

Result: 在多种复杂推理任务上，BiCoT保持了推理保真度，同时在微调、量化、模型级扰动以及自适应输出级攻击下均实现了鲁棒检测，覆盖域内和域外设置。

Conclusion: BiCoT通过将水印与推理相关表征耦合，使移除水印变得困难而不破坏支持连贯推理的特征，为LLM推理能力的知识产权保护提供了一种有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models with Chain-of-Thought reasoning capabilities represent valuable intellectual property, yet existing black-box watermarking methods often trade robustness for reasoning fidelity by perturbing final answers or relying on fragile trigger patterns. We propose BiCoT, a watermarking framework that embeds ownership signals into the internal geometry of reasoning traces by aligning high-saliency structural anchors with a private signature subspace while regularizing ordinary control tokens to preserve semantic capacity. This design couples the watermark with reasoning-relevant representations, making removal difficult without disrupting the features that support coherent reasoning. To enable verification under model theft and representation drift, we introduce Robust Subspace Registration (RSR), a Top-
  logprob-based black-box verifier that uses sentinel tokens to calibrate systematic shifts in the output distribution. Experiments show that BiCoT preserves reasoning fidelity across diverse complex reasoning tasks while achieving robust detection under fine-tuning, quantization, model-level perturbations, and adaptive output-level attacks across in-domain and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum-Enhanced Adversarial Robustness in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28899)
*Jaydip Sen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了对抗性机器学习对AI系统的威胁及现有防御策略，介绍了量子计算与量子机器学习的基础概念，并探讨了利用量子优化、特征映射和混合量子-经典架构来增强AI对抗鲁棒性的概念框架与应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在安全关键系统中面临对抗性攻击的严重挑战，亟需提升模型的鲁棒性、安全性和可信度；量子计算作为一种新兴计算范式，有望为解决这一问题提供新的路径。

Method: 系统综述对抗性机器学习与防御策略，介绍量子计算与量子机器学习基本原理，提出量子增强对抗鲁棒性的概念框架，涵盖量子优化、特征映射及混合量子-经典架构多种技术路径。

Result: 提出了量子增强对抗鲁棒性的概念框架，识别了量子优化、特征映射和混合架构等关键技术方向，并总结了实际应用场景、当前挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 量子人工智能有望通过独特的量子特性提升AI系统对抗鲁棒性，但该领域仍面临诸多挑战，需要进一步研究以推动安全可信AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence has achieved remarkable success across diverse application domains. However, its vulnerability to adversarial attacks poses significant challenges to reliability, security, and trustworthiness. Adversarial machine learning demonstrates that even highly accurate models can be manipulated through carefully crafted perturbations, raising serious concerns in safety critical systems such as healthcare, finance, and autonomous technologies. In parallel, quantum computing has emerged as a transformative paradigm capable of addressing complex computational problems through principles such as superposition, entanglement, and quantum interference. The convergence of these fields has led to the emergence of quantum artificial intelligence, which explores how quantum techniques can enhance learning efficiency, scalability, and robustness. This chapter provides a comprehensive overview of adversarial machine learning and existing defense strategies, followed by an accessible introduction to quantum computing and quantum machine learning models. It further presents conceptual frameworks for quantum-enhanced adversarial robustness, emphasizing quantum optimization, feature mapping, and hybrid quantum classical architectures. Practical applications, key challenges, and future research directions are also discussed to support the development of secure and trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [133] [EvaluatAR: A Cross-Device Evaluation Framework for Rapid Prototyping of Bystander PETs in AR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29177)
*Syed Ibrahim Mustafa Shah Bukhari,Matthew Corbett,Bo Ji,Brendan David-John*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvaluatAR是一个跨设备评估框架，用于快速原型设计和可复现的AR头显旁观者隐私增强技术（PET）评估，通过记录-回放工作流标准化输入输出，并在HoloLens 2、Magic Leap 2和Meta Quest 3上通过三个案例研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR头显持续感知周围环境，捕捉附近旁观者并引发隐私风险。现有的视觉旁观者隐私增强技术依赖人工评估、开销高且设备特定，难以跨设备复现。

Method: 提出EvaluatAR框架，通过记录-回放工作流标准化PET的输入（传感器数据和视觉刺激）和输出，实现受控的实验条件复制，支持快速原型设计和跨设备评估。

Result: 在三款头显（HoloLens 2、Magic Leap 2、Meta Quest 3）上的三个案例研究验证了框架的有效性：（1）跨设备回放输入揭示设备特定的隐私-性能权衡；（2）同一框架工作流可泛化至隐式和显式PET设计类别；（3）回放隐私相关的边界案例可诊断故障并验证PET修改，优于现有基线。

Conclusion: EvaluatAR支持快速迭代的PET开发，在普适AR兴起的关键时刻推动了旁观者PET的可复现跨设备评估。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) headsets continuously sense their surroundings, capturing nearby bystanders and raising privacy risks. Visual bystander privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs) mitigate this risk by detecting bystanders in egocentric scene views and applying privacy transformations (e.g., obfuscation). However, traditional PET evaluation is human-dependent, high-overhead, and device-specific, making it difficult to reproduce across devices. We present EvaluatAR, a cross-device evaluation framework for rapid prototyping at the early stage of PET evaluation. Our framework enables controlled replication of experimental conditions by standardizing PET inputs (sensor data and visual stimuli) and outputs through a record-replay workflow. We validate EvaluatAR through three case studies on HoloLens 2, Magic Leap 2, and Meta Quest 3 across implicit (continuous, context-driven) and explicit (intent-driven) PETs: (1) cross-device replay of inputs to a PET to reveal device-specific privacy-performance trade-offs; (2) generalizability of the same framework workflow across implicit and explicit PET design categories; and (3) replay of privacy-relevant edge cases to diagnose failures and validate PET modifications, yielding an improvement over the state-of-the-art baseline. These results demonstrate EvaluatAR's support for rapid, iterative PET development to advance reproducible cross-device evaluation of bystander PETs at a critical moment in the emergence of ubiquitous AR.

</details>


### [134] [Optimal Rates for Differentially Private Hypothesis Testing with E-values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28952)
*Ben Jacobsen,Tomas Gonzales,Gavin Brown,Kassem Fawaz,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在ε-差分隐私约束下，使用e值进行假设检验时所能达到的最优e-power，给出了理论刻画和匹配算法，并在序贯设置中给出了私有e-process停时的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: e值作为灵活的工具在适时有效和自适应数据分析中广受关注，但涉及隐私数据时，如何在差分隐私约束下最大化检验功效是一个基本且重要的问题。

Method: 理论上刻画了在ε-差分隐私下，检验X~ℙ^n与X~ℚ^n时e-power的最优速率并给出精确匹配算法；在序贯场景中给出私有e-process停时的匹配上下界。

Result: 在给定隐私预算下，达到了最优e-power速率；序贯设置中停时上下界匹配；数值实验表明所提算法在多种序贯检验问题及隐私水平下所需数据少于DP-SPRT。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了差分隐私约束下e值检验的最优效能问题，提供了理论最优的算法，并在实践中验证了其优越性。

Abstract: E-values have attracted considerable interest in recent years as flexible tools for enabling anytime-valid and adaptive data analysis. Hypothesis testing is at the core of many of these applications, which can often involve private or sensitive data. In this work, we answer a simple but important question: given two distributions $\mathbb{P}$ and $\mathbb{Q}$, what is the maximum achievable e-power when testing $X\sim \mathbb{P}^n$ against $X\sim\mathbb{Q}^n$ with e-values that satisfy $\varepsilon$-differential privacy? We characterize the optimal rate for this problem and provide an algorithm which matches it exactly. In the sequential setting, when observations arrive one-by-one and the analyst chooses when to halt, we give matching upper and lower bounds on the stopping times of any private e-process. Numerical experiments confirm the practicality of our algorithms, which require less data than the recently proposed DP-SPRT across a range of sequential testing problems and privacy levels.

</details>


### [135] [A Secure, Manifest-Based Framework for Delegated Privilege Promotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28991)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于清单的安全基础设施，用于在企业数据库系统中实现特权组件的委托提升，允许无特权进程仅提升供应商批准的文件，同时缓解TOCTOU攻击并支持零停机自我更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型企业软件系统遵循最小权限原则以无特权服务账户运行，但依赖少量特权组件进行特定操作。自动修补工具在无特权环境下无法安全更新这些特权组件，要么需要完全管理权限执行整个补丁，要么需要人工管理员干预，形成了安全与运维之间的持续冲突。

Method: 设计了一个最小特权中介器，验证加密保护的元数据，使无特权进程仅能提升供应商批准的文件。采用文件描述符绑定的验证与提升机制来缓解TOCTOU攻击，支持离线密钥轮换与撤销，并通过原子替换实现零停机自我更新。

Result: 该系统已在大型企业数据库系统的生产环境中部署，同时服务云端和本地安装。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于清单的特权组件委托提升方案，有效解决了最小权限原则下自动化补丁更新的安全与运维冲突，为大型企业软件系统的安全维护提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale enterprise software systems commonly run as unprivileged service accounts to enforce least privilege, yet still depend on a small set of privileged components -- such as executables with elevated ownership, permissions, or capabilities -- for narrowly scoped operations. This creates a persistent security and operational conflict during maintenance. Automated patching tools running without elevated privileges cannot safely update privileged components without either executing the entire patch with full administrative rights or requiring manual administrator intervention. We present a secure, manifest-based infrastructure for delegated promotion of privileged software components, deployed in production as part of a large-scale enterprise database system serving both cloud and on-premises installations. The design centers on a minimal privileged mediator that validates cryptographically protected metadata and allows an unprivileged process to promote only vendor-approved files. The system explicitly mitigates Time-of-Check-to-Time-of-Use (TOCTOU) attacks using file-descriptor-bound validation and promotion, supports offline key rotation and revocation, and enables zero-downtime self-update via atomic replacement.

</details>


### [136] [Measuring Real-World Prompt Injection Attacks in LLM-based Resume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28999)
*Mohan Zhang,Yuqi Jia,Zhen Tan,Steven Jiang,Neil Zhenqiang Gong,Tianlong Chen,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对现实世界中基于LLM的简历筛选应用中的提示注入攻击进行了系统研究，通过对hireEZ收集的约20万份真实简历进行分析，发现约1%的简历包含隐藏的提示注入，且过去一两年中此类注入显著增加，超过90%的注入提示不使用显式指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入攻击在学术研究中已被概念性展示，但在现实LLM应用中的普遍性和影响尚缺乏系统性研究，因此作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于hireEZ收集的约20万份真实简历数据，首先设计定制化方法检测简历中的提示注入，并在小规模数据集上手动验证检测器的高精度和优于现有通用检测器的性能，随后将检测器应用于完整数据集进行全面测量研究。

Result: 约1%的简历包含隐藏的提示注入；过去一两年内此类注入显著增加；超过90%的注入提示不包含显式指令。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了现实世界大规模LLM应用中提示注入的证据，为未来理解和缓解此类攻击奠定了基础。

Abstract: LLMs are vulnerable to prompt injection attacks. However, this vulnerability has been primarily demonstrated conceptually in academic studies or through a few anecdotal case studies. Its prevalence and impact in real-world LLM-based applications are largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first systematic study of prompt-injection attacks in a widely used application: LLM-based resume screening. Our analysis is based on approximately 200K real-world resumes collected over multiple years by hireEZ. We first design tailored methods to detect prompt injection in resumes. Manual validation on a small-scale dataset demonstrates that our detectors achieve high precision and outperform state-of-the-art general-purpose detectors. We then apply our detector to the full resume dataset and conduct a comprehensive measurement study of real-world prompt injection attacks. Our analysis reveals several intriguing findings: approximately 1% of resumes contain hidden prompt injections; the prevalence of such injected resumes has increased noticeably over the past one to two years; and more than 90% of injected prompts do not use explicit instructions. These results provide the first evidence of large-scale prompt injection in real-world LLM-based applications and lay the groundwork for future studies to understand and mitigate such attacks.

</details>


### [137] [GEO-Bench: Benchmarking Ranking Manipulation in Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29107)
*Ojas Nimase,Zhe Chen,Gengpei Qi,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GEO-Bench是一个统一的基准测试，用于公平比较各类GEO排名操控攻击方法，揭示了攻击有效性、隐蔽性之间的权衡关系，并标准化了评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在排序产品、文档和推荐结果中应用日益广泛，但现有GEO操纵方法均使用自有数据集和指标进行评估，导致不同攻击方法的相对强度和可检测性不明确，缺乏统一比较框架。

Method: 构建统一基准GEO-Bench，集成黑盒提示攻击（TAP、Zero-Shot）、白盒梯度攻击（STS、RAF、StealthRank）及十种白帽C-SEO策略，在五个数据集上使用固定开源排序器（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct），采用有效性指标（NRG、Success@α、Promote@α）和隐蔽性指标（关键词违规率、困惑度比率）进行评估。

Result: 有效性与隐蔽性在不同对抗攻击间存在权衡；黑盒内容重写在排名提升上达到或超过梯度攻击，且生成文本更流畅，能在某些领域规避关键词和困惑度检测；访问模型类型不能预测攻击强度。

Conclusion: GEO-Bench通过标准化数据集、攻击实现和评估指标，首次实现了不同攻击范式的直接比较，为检测方法的发展提供了支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rank products, documents, and recommendations for user queries, which makes manipulating these rankings a growing concern for fairness and information integrity. Research on generative engine optimization (GEO) has produced many manipulation methods, but each is evaluated on its own dataset with its own metrics, so their relative strength and detectability stay unclear. We present GEO-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates GEO ranking-manipulation attacks under one protocol. It unifies black-box prompt-based attacks (TAP, Zero-Shot), white-box gradient-based attacks (STS, RAF, StealthRank), and ten white-hat C-SEO strategies. We score every method on five datasets against a fixed open-weight ranker (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), using metrics for both effectiveness (NRG, Success@α, Promote@α) and stealth (keyword violation rate, perplexity ratio). Our evaluation shows that effectiveness and stealth trade off across adversarial attacks, that black-box content rewriting matches or exceeds gradient-based attacks on rank promotion while producing more fluent text and can evade both keyword- and perplexity-based detection on some domains, and that the access model does not predict attack strength. By standardizing datasets, attack implementations, and metrics, GEO-Bench enables the first direct comparison across these attack paradigms and supports the development of detection methods.

</details>


### [138] [ReasonBreak: Probing Vulnerabilities in Reasoning-Enabled Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29114)
*Mohammadreza Teymoorianfard,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit,Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了具备推理能力的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶中面对现实输入扰动时的脆弱性，发现这些模型在推理和轨迹预测上分别可达89%和72%的攻击成功率，导致碰撞率上升和安全性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具备推理能力的VLA模型被广泛用于端到端自动驾驶，但其在现实输入扰动下的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 以NVIDIA Alpamayo模型为代表，首次对推理型VLA模型进行系统性黑盒研究，提出包含语义与结构两个维度的推理感知评估框架，以及安全中心的度量指标，并构建了评估攻击与防御的基准测试。

Result: 在闭环仿真中，推理攻击成功率达89%，轨迹操控攻击成功率达72%，导致碰撞率上升和安全指标显著下降。

Conclusion: 推理型VLA模型在现实输入扰动下高度脆弱，需加强评估与防御以保障自动驾驶安全。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with integrated reasoning have been proposed for end-to-end autonomous driving, assuming a tight coupling between reasoning and trajectory generation. However, the robustness of such systems under realistic input perturbations remains largely unexplored. We show that these models are highly vulnerable to realistic input perturbations, achieving up to 89% attack success rate (ASR) on reasoning and up to 72% on trajectory manipulation in closed-loop simulation, leading to increased collision rates and degraded safety metrics. Using NVIDIA's recent Alpamayo models as representative industry-developed VLAs, we conduct the first systematic black-box study of reasoning-enabled VLA models under realistic textual input corruptions, evaluating their impact on reasoning and driving behavior. We introduce a reasoning-aware evaluation framework capturing both semantic and structural aspects of reasoning, along with safety-centric measures. We also introduce a benchmark for evaluating attacks and defenses on reasoning-trajectory interactions in autonomous driving. Our results highlight the need for rigorous evaluation and improved defenses to ensure the safety of reasoning-enabled VLA systems in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [139] [unix-ctf: Procedural Environments for Unix-Competence Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29115)
*Geoffrey Bradway,Roger Creus Castanyer,Lorenz Wolf,Maxwill Lin,Matthew James Sargent,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Unix 能力应区别于编程能力，构建了 unix-ctf（基于 CTF 的 Unix 技能训练平台），通过 LLM 辅助合成管道生成 656 个可复用的隐藏-查找任务（覆盖 155 种技术），微调 Qwen3-8B 后解决率从 11.6% 提升至 43.6%，表明 Unix 能力是可分离、可训练且应独立评估的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准（如 Terminal-Bench 2.0）混淆了编程能力与 Unix 能力：熟悉 Python 但不熟悉 Unix 的求解器也能通过大量测试，而相反情况很少被考察。作者希望将 Unix 能力作为独立维度进行操作化定义、训练和评估。

Method: 构建 unix-ctf 程序化生成器：LLM 辅助合成管道生成候选隐藏技术→重写为参数化脚本对→通过双向合约过滤（隐藏脚本不留明文、查找脚本可在新目录恢复）。每个任务在 Linux 容器中使用单一 Unix 特性隐藏 flag。固定容器、布局和评分框架，LLM 只负责种植和恢复步骤。使用 GRPO + LoRA 微调 Qwen3-8B。

Result: 管道成功率 87.5%（656/750），而全容器生成方法仅 17.4%。656 个变体归约为 155 种独特技术。微调后解决率从 11.6% 提升至 43.6%（15 技能多家族保留集，n=225），InterCode-CTF 上达到 32/100，取证技能提升 33 个百分点。

Conclusion: Unix 能力是可分离、可训练的，应直接评估而非混入"通过终端编程"的框架中。

Abstract: Unix competence is the ability to use shell and operating-system primitives as first-class tools, not merely to write programs through a terminal. Current terminal benchmarks tend to blur this distinction: a solver fluent in Python but weak in Unix can pass a substantial fraction of Terminal-Bench 2.0, while the reverse skill profile is rarely exercised. We make the distinction operational and build a training surface for the Unix component. unix-ctf is a procedural generator of capture-the-flag tasks for shell agents. Each task hides a short token (a flag of the form flag(a3b1c9...)) inside a fresh Linux container using a single Unix feature, and the agent must recover it. Tasks are produced by an LLM-assisted synthesis pipeline that generates candidate hiding techniques, rewrites them into parameterized hide-and-find script pairs, and filters them with a bidirectional contract: the hide script must leave no plaintext trace of the flag on disk, and the find script must recover the flag in a fresh directory. Because the LLM only writes the planting and recovery steps (the container, layout, and grading harness are fixed), the pipeline lands 656 of 750 raw attempts as portable, reusable variants (87.5\%). Our reproduction of Endless Terminals' full-container-generation approach lands only 17.4\% under the same checks. The 656 variants canonicalize to 155 distinct techniques. Fine-tuning Qwen3-8B with LoRA using GRPO on this surface lifts solve rate from 11.6\% to 43.6\% on a 15-skill multi-family holdout (n=225), redistributes which InterCode-CTF tasks the model solves, and produces a +33 pp gain in Forensics while reaching 32/100 on InterCode-CTF. These results suggest that Unix competence is separable, trainable, and best evaluated directly rather than folded into programming-through-a-shell.

</details>


### [140] [Domain-Informed Representation for Evolutionary Sieving in Integral and Module Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29169)
*Ahmad Tashfeen,Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将Ajtai等人的筛法增强为遗传算法，通过引入领域信息SVP表示和交叉操作，并将其自然扩展到模格应用，以提升量子安全密码学中最短向量问题(SVP)求解的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密码学基于整数分解或离散对数等难题，在量子计算机面前存在脆弱性；需要发展量子安全密码学，而SVP是其核心基础问题；现有方法存在优化空间。

Method: 将Laarhoven对Ajtai等人筛法的遗传算法处理进行增强，具体包括：1) 引入领域信息引导的SVP表示方法；2) 设计专门的交叉操作；3) 将方法自然扩展到模格（module lattices）应用场景。

Result: 增强了针对SVP问题的遗传算法求解方案，并成功将方法适用范围从传统格扩展到更通用的模格结构。

Conclusion: 通过领域知识的融入和交叉操作的引入，遗传算法在求解SVP问题上获得了性能提升，且该方法能够自然适配模格，为量子安全密码学提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Traditional cryptography, rooted in problems, e.g., integer factorisation or discrete log, is inevitably vulnerable to a fully operational quantum computer. Although it remains an engineering frontier, the looming threat extends to encrypted data stored today, which could be decrypted in the future with quantum capabilities. To safeguard against this eventuality, the backbone of the modern quantum-safe cryptography is the Shortest Vector Problem (SVP). We enhance Laarhoven's treatment of Ajtai et al.'s sieving as a genetic algorithm (GA) for the SVP by incorporating domain-informed SVP representation and crossover while naturally extending application to the module lattices.

</details>


### [141] [The Best-Laid SCHEMEs: Coordinated Sabotage and Monitoring in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29178)
*Nikolay Radev,Lennart Haas,Benjamin Arnav,Pablo Bernabeu-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SCHEME基准，评估多智能体编码系统中的协同恶意行为。实验发现GPT 5.1 Codex和Gemini 3.1 Pro已能实现实际级别的协同破坏（Gemini成功率84%，Codex 46%），但通过监控代码编辑可高精度检测（FPR≤2%），即使智能体知晓监控存在，未被检测的破坏仍低于3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体编码系统将工作分解到多个模型实例，这些实例能否在保持与用户意图一致的同时，协调实现隐藏的恶意目标，成为一个关键的安全问题。现有研究缺乏对多智能体协同破坏能力的系统性评估。

Method: 构建SCHEME基准，包含17个任务实例，覆盖7个场景和8个真实开源库。每个任务配对合法软件工程任务与隐蔽侧任务，确保没有智能体子集能单独成功。评估使用GPT 5.1 Codex和Gemini 3.1 Pro，分析协同破坏成功率、通信故障恢复能力以及监控检测效果。

Result: Gemini在合法任务成功的同时完成隐蔽目标的概率为84%，Codex为46%。模型差距主要来自故障恢复能力（Gemini恢复率73% vs Codex 56%），而非故障发生率。可信监控器通过代码编辑检测协同破坏时，在99%（Gemini）/68%（Codex）的尝试中FPR≤2%，加入通信监控后提升至100%/81%。即使采用隐匿感知提示，在全可见监控器下未被检测的破坏仍低于3%。告知智能体存在监控可使Gemini破坏成功率减半，且不显著破坏检测性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体协同破坏已达到实际可行水平，但通过代码编辑和通信监控可有效检测。监控知晓可作为一种威慑手段降低破坏成功率，且不会显著降低检测能力。这为多智能体编码系统的安全防护提供了重要参考。

Abstract: As agentic coding systems decompose work across multiple model instances, a critical safety question is whether those instances can coordinate to achieve a hidden malicious objective while remaining aligned with user intent. We introduce SCHEME, a benchmark of 17 task instances across 7 settings and 8 real open-source libraries, each pairing a legitimate software-engineering task with a covert side task. Every setting is designed so that no proper subset of agents can succeed alone: agents must decompose a shared sabotage plan, relay partial requirements under different communication topologies, and execute mutually consistent edits, testing genuine multi-agent coordination rather than individual capability. Evaluating with GPT 5.1 Codex and Gemini 3.1 Pro, we find coordinated sabotage is already practical, with Gemini completing the covert objective while succeeding on the legitimate task in 84\% of samples and Codex in 46\%. A failure analysis shows that recovery, not failure incidence, drives the model gap: both models suffer similar numbers of communication failures per trajectory, but Gemini recovers from $\sim$73\% of occurrences against Codex's $\sim$56\%. However, the agents' code edits remain detectable: a trusted monitor flags coordinated sabotage at FPR$\le$2\% in 99\%/68\% (Gemini/Codex) of attempts from code edits alone, rising to 100\%/81\% with access to agent communication. Despite stealth-aware prompting, undetected sabotage stays at $<$3\% under the full-visibility monitor; telling agents a monitor is present halves Gemini's sabotage success without significantly breaking detection, even when agents are explicitly prompted to fabricate cover stories.

</details>


### [142] [Implicit Identity Technologies for LLMs: Fingerprinting and Watermarking across Datasets, Models, and Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29245)
*Bing Liu,Shunping Wang,Yufan Zhu,Xinyi Yu,Jing Huang,Linkang Du,Hongbin Pei,Wei Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于生命周期的LLM指纹识别与水印技术分类体系，以隐式身份为统一抽象，区分非侵入式指纹识别与侵入式水印，并建立了涵盖可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数据、计算和专业知识方面投入巨大，且部署于高风险场景，需要保护LLM相关资产并追溯其来源。现有工作虽然迅速扩展，但术语使用不一致（指纹识别与水印常被混用），方法通常局限于孤立的资产生成设置，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 1) 提出隐式身份（implicit identity）作为统一抽象概念；2) 区分指纹识别（非侵入式，源于内在特征）与水印（侵入式，刻意嵌入）；3) 构建基于生命周期的分类体系，按数据集、模型、生成内容组织技术，并按验证语义分为基于相似性的归因和基于密钥的验证；4) 建立以可识别性、鲁棒性、可部署性为核心的评估框架。

Result: 提供了统一的术语体系、生命周期阶段划分和评估目标，为LLM身份技术研究提供了结构化基础。

Conclusion: 通过统一术语、生命周期阶段和评估目标，为研究LLM身份技术以及开发更可靠的资产保护和溯源机制提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a survey and taxonomy of LLM fingerprinting and watermarking for identity, ownership verification, provenance, and generated-content attribution. Large language models (LLMs) require substantial investments in data, computation, and expertise, and are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, making it critical to protect LLM-related assets and trace their origins. Existing work has rapidly expanded across dataset provenance, model ownership, and generated-content detection, but the field remains fragmented: fingerprinting and watermarking are often used inconsistently, and methods are typically studied within isolated asset-specific settings. To address this gap, we introduce implicit identity as a unifying abstraction for verifiable but not directly observable identity signals in LLM systems. We distinguish fingerprinting as non-intrusive identity derived from intrinsic characteristics, and watermarking as intrusive identity deliberately embedded into data, models, or generated content. We then propose a lifecycle-based taxonomy that organises techniques across datasets, models, and generated content, and further separates them by verification semantics: similarity-based attribution and keyed verification. Finally, we establish an evaluation framework centred on identifiability, robustness, and deployability, summarising representative metrics under realistic access and transformation regimes. By unifying terminology, lifecycle stages, and evaluation objectives, this survey provides a structured foundation for studying LLM identity technologies and for developing more reliable mechanisms for asset protection and provenance.

</details>


### [143] [Minimal Prompt Perturbations Lead to Code Vulnerabilities: Prompt Fragility and Hidden-State Signals in Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29737)
*Alexander Sternfeld,Andrei Kucharavy,Ljiljana Dolamic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 小至单字符的提示扰动即可使LLM生成的安全代码变得不安全；输入处理类漏洞可提前检测，而安全默认值类漏洞需在解码阶段干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已发现轻微提示扰动会降低LLM生成代码的功能正确性，但这类扰动是否同样会危及代码安全性尚未被研究。

Method: 对三个模型和五种编程语言的提示进行token级突变（小至单字符变化），通过探测模型隐藏状态分析安全脆弱性的可预测性。

Result: 单字符变化即可使生成代码从安全变为不安全；输入处理类漏洞的可预测性（平均AUC 0.753）高于安全默认值类漏洞（平均AUC 0.674）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助编程的威胁模型需扩展至普通提示变异；输入处理类缺陷可在生成前捕获，而安全默认值类缺陷需在解码阶段干预。

Abstract: LLM-based coding assistants are seeing rapid adoption, offering substantial gains in developer productivity. As organizations increasingly ship code these agents produce, the security of that code becomes critical. Prior work has shown that minor prompt perturbations degrade the functional correctness of LLM-generated code, but whether they also compromise code security has remained unstudied. We apply token-level mutations to prompts across three models and five programming languages, and show that mutations as small as a single-character change can flip generated code from secure to vulnerable. Probing the models' hidden states reveals that this fragility is partially encoded in prompt representations, but unevenly so. Input-handling vulnerabilities, where the model omits validation or sanitization, are more predictable (mean AUC 0.753) than secure-defaults vulnerabilities, where insecure code stems from one local choice such as a weak algorithm or unsafe parameter (mean AUC 0.674). These results show that the threat model for LLM-assisted coding extends beyond prompt injection to ordinary prompt variation, and indicate that input-handling flaws can be caught before generation while secure-defaults flaws require intervention during decoding.

</details>


### [144] [AliMark: Enhancing Robustness of Sentence-Level Watermarking Against Text Paraphrasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29434)
*Yuexin Li,Wenjie Qu,Linyu Wu,Yulin Chen,Yufei He,Tri Cao,Bryan Hooi,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AliMark框架，将句子级水印重构为位序列编码与对齐问题，采用多候选文本变体自适应对齐策略，显著提升对句子拆分/合并等结构扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级水印方法基于前缀设计，在面对强释义攻击（如DIPPER、GPT-3.5）中的句子拆分与合并等结构扰动时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将水印检测转化为水印文本与秘密位序列的对齐问题，采用两阶段检测：首先生成多个重构文本变体，然后自适应对齐提取的位序列与秘密位序列以最小化对齐成本。

Result: 在多种释义攻击下，AliMark显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 多候选对齐策略天然增强了水印对句子合并与拆分的鲁棒性，为句子级水印提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing sentence-level watermarking methods enhance robustness to paraphrasing by anchoring watermarks in sentence semantics. However, their prefix-based designs remain vulnerable to structural perturbations, such as sentence splitting and merging, which commonly arise under strong paraphrasers like DIPPER and GPT-3.5. To mitigate this issue, we propose AliMark, a framework that reformulates sentence-level watermarking as a bit sequence encoding and alignment problem between a potentially watermarked text and a secret bit sequence. Notably, our approach adopts a two-stage detection strategy: we generate multiple restructured text variants and adaptively align their extracted bit sequences with the secret bit sequence to minimize alignment cost. This multi-candidate alignment design naturally improves robustness to sentence merges and splits. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AliMark substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under diverse paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [145] [Protecting On-Device AI Inference: A Systematic Review of Attacks and Defence Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29450)
*Zisis Tsiatsikas,Alexandros Fakis,Georgios Karopoulos,Vasileios Kouliaridis,Marios Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了针对设备端AI推理的安全与隐私威胁及防御机制，发现攻击与防御文献存在严重不平衡：约四分之一的攻击论文关注知识产权攻击，而半数防御方案针对该问题；约三分之一的对抗性攻击论文缺乏相应的防御研究，揭示了未来研究的重要方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘和移动设备上AI/ML模型的广泛部署，设备端推理面临模型窃取、对抗攻击、数据泄露等安全与隐私威胁，现有综述多聚焦边缘智能（含分布式训练），忽略了设备端AI推理特有的安全与隐私问题。

Method: 对设备端AI推理的威胁与防御机制进行系统性文献综述，分析攻击与防御文献的数量分布与对应关系。

Result: 攻击与防御文献分布不均衡：约25%的攻击论文关注知识产权攻击，而50%的防御方案针对此问题；对抗攻击约占攻击文献的三分之一，但缺乏相应的防御研究。

Conclusion: 已知攻击与可用防御之间存在显著不对称性，明确了设备端AI推理安全领域的未来研究机遇。

Abstract: The need for secure and private Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) on edge and mobile devices has increased the necessity of protecting the architecture of these systems from threats to both security and privacy. With an ever-increasing number of pre-trained AI models being used on mobile platforms for client-side inference, there are rising concerns about the risks associated with the theft/extraction of AI models, adversarial attacks on AI models, and data breaches. As a result of this trend, a variety of defence mechanisms have been proposed to protect against these threats. These include Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), homomorphic encryption, obfuscation, and differential privacy, among others. However, current surveys largely focus on edge intelligence, which includes distributed training, and thus overlook security and privacy issues that are specific to on-device AI inference. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first comprehensive review of threats and corresponding defence mechanisms targeting on-device inference. Our results show that the attack and defence literature are unbalanced: approximately one quarter of the surveyed attack papers focus on Intellectual Property (IP) attacks, whereas half of the defence solutions tackle the same issue. More importantly, some attack categories have no defence paper associated to them, such as adversarial attacks that account for roughly one third of the attack literature. This asymmetry between known attacks and available mitigations highlights clear opportunities for future research on securing on-device AI inference.

</details>


### [146] [Bridging Theory and Practice: An Executable Taxonomy of Security Properties for ProVerif and Tamarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29465)
*Leonard Tudorache,Ivan Kurtev,Mark van den Brand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对53篇（2022-2025年）使用ProVerif和Tamarin的研究进行系统文献综述，构建了一个基于证据的安全属性分类体系，提供非正式定义、一阶逻辑形式定义以及两种工具的建模模式和可执行示例，以降低协议设计者使用形式化验证工具的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证工具（ProVerif、Tamarin）对保障安全协议至关重要，但其使用需要专业的领域知识，而协议设计者通常具备安全背景而非形式化验证背景，缺乏结构化的、可访问的资源来帮助他们将设计需求转化为验证工具的语言。

Method: 对2022-2025年间53篇使用ProVerif和Tamarin的近期研究进行文献综述，系统分类和定义安全属性；提供非正式定义便于直观理解，同时用一阶逻辑给出严格的形式定义；详述建模模式并在开放仓库中提供两种工具的可执行示例。

Result: 构建了一个系统化的、基于证据的安全属性分类体系；提供了每种属性的非正式定义和一阶逻辑形式定义；总结了建模模式并收集了ProVerif和Tamarin的可执行示例于开放仓库中。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过弥合理论安全属性定义与其实际可执行验证模型之间的差距，推动了现有技术水平，为协议设计者提供了结构化的实用资源。

Abstract: Security is critical for everything relying on modern digital systems. Because almost all digital interactions are governed by the Internet and cryptographic protocols, these protocols must serve as reliable mechanisms that guarantee core security properties, such as confidentiality and integrity. Formal verification of these protocols is a critical step in securing interconnected systems. Tools such as ProVerif and Tamarin are widely employed to perform automated verification. However, their effective use demands specialized domain knowledge, creating a significant learning curve for security protocol designers who often have a security, rather than a formal verification background. We therefore need structured, accessible resources to help protocol designers to express their design and requirements in the language of the formal verification tools.
  To address this, we introduce a systematic and evidence-based taxonomy of security properties. This taxonomy is derived from a literature review of 53 recent studies (2022-2025) that used ProVerif and Tamarin, providing an up-to-date view of verified properties. We systematically categorize and define these properties, providing both informal definitions for intuitive comprehension and rigorous formal definitions expressed in first-order logic for clarity and consistency. We further detail modeling patterns and implement executable examples in both ProVerif and Tamarin, collected in an open repository. This work advances the state of the art by bridging the gap between theoretical security property definitions and their practical, executable verification models.

</details>


### [147] [SciIntBench: Measuring LLM Compliance with Research Integrity Norms Under Adversarial Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29468)
*Almene De Meran Meguimtsop,Maria Leonor Pacheco,Daniel E. Acuna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了SciIntBench基准，评估16个LLM在10个RCR类别和3种场景（公开对抗、隐蔽对抗、良性）下的科学诚信对齐表现，发现模型对隐蔽违规和特定违规类别（透明度、剽窃、造假）的拒答能力明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于科研工作，但其是否遵循负责任的研究行为（RCR）规范尚不清楚，亟需系统评估。

Method: 构建SciIntBench对抗基准（810个提示，10个RCR类别，3个科学领域），每个场景设公开对抗、隐蔽对抗、良性三个版本，评估16个商业和开源LLM（来自6家供应商），共产生12,960条回复。

Result: 模型对公开不当行为的拒答远优于隐蔽违规（尤其是压力驱动的隐蔽违规）；不同RCR类别的拒答表现差异明显，在透明度、剽窃和造假等类别上边界更弱。

Conclusion: 当前LLM的科学诚信对齐存在严重的框架敏感性缺陷，对隐蔽和特定类别的不当行为防范不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific work, but it is unclear whether they uphold responsible conduct of research (RCR) norms or help undermine them. We introduce SciIntBench, an adversarial benchmark of 810 prompts across ten RCR categories and three scientific domains. Each scenario appears as an Overt Adversarial, Covert Adversarial, and Benign version, allowing us to jointly measure framing-sensitive refusal of misconduct and helpfulness on legitimate requests. We evaluate 16 commercial and open-weight LLMs from six providers (2024--2026), producing 12,960 responses. We find that scientific integrity alignment is strongly framing-sensitive: models refuse explicit misconduct far more reliably than covert violations, especially failing when misconduct is presented as a pressure-driven shortcut. Refusals vary by RCR category, with weaker boundaries around transparency, plagiarism, and fabrication.

</details>


### [148] [KBF: Knowledge Boundary as Fingerprint for Language Model and Black-Box API Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29524)
*Yijia Fang,Yiqing Feng,Bingyu Li,Mingxun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KBF，一种低成本的黑盒审计协议，通过知识边界附近的稳定数值召回率来指纹识别模型API。在16个生产级LLM端点中，KBF标记了所有155个经济相关的替换攻击且无误报，对混合路由攻击的检测阈值为5-10%流量替换，并在六平台影子API审计中发现7/27的模型单元与其参考端点存在统计不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中转和转售API越来越多地介入LLM访问，但用户无法直接验证声称的端点是否真正服务于所宣传的模型，存在模型替换或降级攻击的风险。

Method: 提出KBF黑盒审计协议，通过在知识边界（knowledge boundary）附近测量稳定的数值召回率来为模型API建立指纹，从而检测实际服务模型与声称模型是否一致。

Result: 在16个生产级LLM端点中，KBF成功检测所有155个经济相关替换攻击，零误报；在部署变化下保持稳定；可检测5-10%流量被替换的高分离度混合路由攻击；在六平台影子API审计中，27个模型单元中有7个与其参考端点存在统计不一致，问题集中在高端Claude端点上。

Conclusion: KBF为LLM中转服务提供了一种低成本、有效的模型真实性验证方法，揭示了当前API中转生态中存在的模型替换问题，特别是高端模型端点存在不一致现象。

Abstract: Relay and reseller APIs increasingly intermediate access to large language models (LLMs), but users have no direct way to verify that a claimed endpoint is actually serving the advertised model. We introduce KBF, a low-cost black-box auditing protocol that fingerprints model APIs using stable numerical recall near the knowledge boundary. Across 16 production LLM endpoints, KBF flags all 155 economically relevant substitutions without rejecting any same-model controls, remains stable under deployment variation, detects high-separation mixed-routing attacks when only 5-10% of traffic is substituted, and finds that 7 of 27 platform model cells in a six-platform shadow API audit are statistically inconsistent with their reference endpoints, with inconsistencies concentrated on premium Claude endpoints.

</details>


### [149] [Temporal Motif-aware Graph Test-time Adaptation for OOD Blockchain Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29526)
