*Runang He,Tongya Zheng,Huiling Peng,Yuanyu Wan,Bingde Hu,Jiawei Chen,Canghong Jin,Mingli Song,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMG-TTA框架，利用时间感知的3节点时序模体分布和图测试时自适应策略，解决区块链异常检测中对抗性模式演化与分布外问题，在5个真实数据集上平均超越SOTA方法54.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法面临两大挑战：(1) 恶意行为者导致的对抗性模式演化；(2) 区块链上多样化交易语义引发的分布外(OOD)问题。亟需一种能捕捉时序演化模式并自适应测试分布差异的检测框架。

Method: (1) 高效计算每个活跃地址的3节点时序模体分布，实现时序模体感知的图学习；(2) 设计简洁有效的测试时自适应(TTA)策略，促进训练图与测试图之间的通用模式共享。

Result: 在5个真实数据集上的实验表明，TEMG-TTA相比最先进的GAD方法平均提升54.88%，且案例研究验证了其对异常地址复杂交易模式的显式表征能力。

Conclusion: TEMG-TTA通过时序模体感知与测试时自适应两个技术设计，有效解决了区块链异常检测中的模式演化与OOD问题，显著超越现有方法。代码已开源。

Abstract: Ever-evolving transaction patterns have significantly hindered anomaly detection on emerging cryptocurrency blockchains due to the vast number of addresses and diverse anomalous behaviors. Recently, advanced Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) approaches applied to blockchains have faced two critical challenges: \textit{adversarial pattern evolution by malicious actors} and \textit{the out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by varied transaction semantics on blockchains}. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework termed \textbf{TE}mporal \textbf{M}otif-aware \textbf{G}raph \textbf{T}est-\textbf{T}ime \textbf{A}daptation (\textbf{TEMG-TTA}). First, we comprehensively capture the 3-node temporal motif distribution of each active address using an efficient computational mechanism, enabling downstream temporal motif-aware graph learning. Second, we design a simple yet effective test-time adaptation strategy to facilitate the sharing of common patterns between training and testing graphs. Extensive experiments on 5 real-world datasets demonstrate that our proposed \textbf{TEMG-TTA} outperforms \textit{state-of-the-art} GAD approaches by an average of 54.88\%. A further case study on interpretable motif patterns reveals that \textbf{TEMG-TTA} explicitly characterizes the complex transaction patterns of anomalous addresses, thereby verifying the effectiveness of our technical designs. Our code will be made publicly available https://github.com/LuoXishuang0712/TEMG-TTA/.

</details>


### [150] [LoRA-Key: User-Centric LoRA Watermarking for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29569)
*Yaopeng Wang,Qingliang Wang,Zhibo Wang,Huiyu Xu,Jiacheng Du,Qiu Wang,Jia-Li Yin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LoRA-Key提出了一种面向用户的水印框架，通过可复用的Watermark LoRA模块与目标LoRA进行免训练线性叠加，实现轻量级、即插即用的版权保护，无需逐LoRA重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为微调扩散模型的主流方式，但现有水印方案要么保护基础模型，要么需要对每个目标LoRA进行水印感知重训练，无法适应LoRA模块易复制、再分发和未授权复用的开放社区生态。

Method: 1) 在冻结VAE潜空间建立可恢复水印先验；2) 训练可复用的Watermark LoRA，通过消息条件水印监督和语义一致性约束优化；3) 提出梯度正交投影(GOP)抑制与语义保持方向冲突的水印更新，减少对生成质量和风格适配的干扰；4) 通过训练-free线性叠加实现即插即用。

Result: 实验表明LoRA-Key在保持生成质量和风格保真度的同时，实现了轻量级即插即用版权保护，并在图像级失真、下游微调和多LoRA组合场景下保持鲁棒的所有权验证。

Conclusion: LoRA-Key提供了一种用户中心化的、无需逐LoRA重训练的可复用水印框架，有效解决了开放社区中LoRA模块的版权保护问题。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used mechanism for customizing text-to-image diffusion models, enabling lightweight modules that are shared, reused, and commercialized as independent assets. This LoRA-centric ecosystem shifts copyright protection from foundation models to distributed LoRA modules, which are easy to copy, redistribute, or reuse without authorization. Existing watermarking methods either protect the base diffusion model or require watermark-aware retraining for each target LoRA, limiting their practicality in open community settings. To address this limitation, we propose LoRA-Key, a user-centric LoRA watermarking framework that treats copyright protection as a reusable ownership key. LoRA-Key encapsulates a recoverable secret message into a standalone user-specific Watermark LoRA, which can be attached to different target LoRAs through training-free linear superposition without per-LoRA retraining or structural modification. To train such a reusable key, we first establish a latent watermark prior in the frozen VAE latent space for robust message embedding and recovery, and then optimize the Watermark LoRA with message-conditioned watermark supervision and semantic consistency constraints. We further introduce Gradient Orthogonal Projection (GOP) to suppress watermark updates that conflict with semantic-preserving directions, reducing interference with generation fidelity and downstream style adaptation. Extensive experiments show that LoRA-Key provides lightweight plug-and-play copyright protection while preserving generation quality and style fidelity, and maintains robust ownership verification under image-level distortions, downstream fine-tuning, and multi-LoRA composition.

</details>


### [151] [Scarcity Is Not Enough: An Impossibility Result for Linear Sybil Cost Under Parallelizable Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29651)
*Homayoun Maleki,Nekane Sainz,Jon Legarda,Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，在无需许可的系统中，仅靠资源的稀缺性不足以抵抗女巫攻击；资源的结构特性（可分性、影响力可加性、时间可重用性、身份可转移性）决定了攻击者是否能够以亚线性成本集中影响力。如果一个资源满足所有并行化属性，则任何协议都无法强制实现线性成本（不可能性结果）。而吞吐量受限、不可转移、窗口局部的资源则可以强制执行线性成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知认为稀缺资源绑定足以抵抗女巫攻击，但本文指出稀缺性本身并不充分，资源的结构属性才是决定性因素。作者希望从理论上刻画哪些资源特性会导致影响力以亚线性成本集中，从而揭示协议设计中的根本性限制。

Method: 通过定义对抗成本函数C(s,T)（实现s个独立参与单元在T个窗口内的影响力所需的最小支出），对资源属性进行分类分析。证明满足四种并行化属性（可分性、影响力可加性、时间可重用性、身份可转移性）的资源必然导致影响力摊销C(s,T)=o(sT)，并证明吞吐量受限、不可转移、窗口局部的资源可达到C(s,T)=Ω(sT)。

Result: 两类资源在渐进意义上可分离：并行化资源导致亚线性成本（不可能线性强制执行），而吞吐量受限、不可转移、窗口局部资源可强制线性成本。任何想实现线性成本集中影响的机制必须选择违反至少一个并行化属性的资源。

Conclusion: 资源的结构属性而非简单稀缺性是抵抗女巫攻击的关键，为无需许可系统的机制设计提供了理论基础和指导原则。

Abstract: Permissionless systems resist Sybil attacks by binding influence to scarce resources. We show that scarcity alone is insufficient: the structural properties of the resource determine whether influence can be concentrated at sublinear cost through identity replication, delegation, or pooling.
  We model this through the adversarial cost C(s,T): the minimum expenditure required to achieve influence proportional to s independent participation units over T windows. We prove that any resource satisfying divisibility, additivity of influence, temporal reusability, and identity transferability admits influence amortization: C(s,T)=o(sT), regardless of protocol design. This is an impossibility result: no protocol rule can enforce linear cost of influence concentration over a structurally parallelizable resource.
  We further prove that throughput-bounded, non-transferable, window-local resources enforce C(s,T)=Omega(sT): each additional unit of sustained influence incurs marginal cost Delta(s,T)=Omega(T), growing with the time horizon. The two resource classes are asymptotically separated.
  As a direct design consequence, any mechanism targeting linear cost of influence concentration must ground participation in a resource that violates at least one parallelizability property.

</details>


### [152] [Cert-LAS: Toward Certified Model Ownership Verification for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Layer-Adaptive Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29809)
*Leyi Qi,Yiming Li,Siyuan Liang,Zhengzhong Tu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Cert-LAS 是一种基于层自适应平滑的认证型模型所有权验证方法，用于在对抗性移除攻击下仍能可靠地验证文本到图像扩散模型的版权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门水印方法隐式假设验证过程"忠实"（即攻击者不会破坏水印信号），但实际中攻击者可能有意或无意地破坏水印信号，导致验证可靠性严重下降。

Method: Cert-LAS 使用扩散分类器嵌入指定水印，并通过 LFS 引导的层自适应噪声进行平滑；通过假设检验判断可疑模型是否表现出显著强于无水印参考模型的水印响应。

Result: 实验验证了 Cert-LAS 在对抗自适应攻击下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Cert-LAS 是首个可认证的 T2I 模型 MOV 方法，即使在恶意移除攻击下仍能实现可靠验证。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have enabled unprecedented creative applications, but their unauthorized use has raised serious intellectual property concerns, making model ownership verification (MOV) increasingly critical. We find that existing backdoor-based diffusion watermarking methods often (implicitly) assume a "faithful" verification process, namely, that the verifier can query a suspicious model and obtain the faithful watermark response to complete MOV. However, in practice, adversaries may intentionally or unintentionally damage potential watermark signals, significantly degrading verification reliability. To address this issue, we propose Cert-LAS, the first certified MOV method for T2I models based on layer-adaptive smoothing. In general, Cert-LAS embeds specified watermarks using diffusion classifiers and an LFS-guided layer-adaptive noise, and verifies ownership by examining whether the suspected model exhibits significantly stronger watermark responses compared to unwatermarked references through hypothesis testing. We further prove that, under certain conditions, our Cert-LAS can still achieve reliable verification even in the presence of malicious removal attacks. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of Cert-LAS and its resistance to adaptive attacks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Leyi-Qi/Cert-LAS.

</details>


### [153] [Ciphera: A Decentralised Biometric Identity Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29868)
*Ankit Kanaiyalal Prajapati,Shahzad Memon,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Ameer Al-Nemrat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ciphera——一个结合隐私保护人脸识别、多节点验证、IPFS凭证存储和区块链撤销机制的分布式生物特征身份框架。实验表明功能成功率达81%，p95验证延迟约820ms，安全分析确认了强机密性和完整性，但活体检测不完整导致存在深度伪造和重放攻击风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心化生物特征身份系统存在单点故障、验证过程不透明以及生物特征泄露后不可撤销等根本性安全问题。尽管DID和VC提供了更强的隐私保障，但它们与生物特征认证及分布式验证的集成仍缺乏充分探索。

Method: Ciphera框架集成了隐私保护人脸识别、多节点验证、IPFS凭证元数据存储和区块链锚定撤销机制，并在功能、性能、安全及分布式一致性四个维度进行了系统评估。

Result: 功能成功率达81%；p95验证延迟约820ms（多节点并发）；安全分析确认了强机密性和完整性；存在撤销传播延迟、审计日志偶发不一致及活体检测不完整（易受深度伪造和重放攻击）等问题。

Conclusion: 研究验证了分布式生物特征身份的可行性，同时指出了生产级部署面临的关键工程挑战。

Abstract: Centralised biometric identity systems expose users to single points of failure, opaque verification processes, and irreversible biometric compromise. Decentralised Identifiers (DIDs) and Verifiable Credentials (VCs) offer stronger privacy guarantees, yet their integration with biometric authentication and distributed verification remains insufficiently explored. This paper presents Ciphera, a decentralised biometric identity framework combining privacy-preserving facial recognition, multi-node verification, IPFS-based credential metadata storage, and blockchain-anchored revocation. Evaluated across functional, performance, security, and distributed consistency dimensions, Ciphera achieved an 81% functional success rate, with stable enrolment and authentication but measurable revocation propagation delays and occasional audit-log inconsistencies. Performance testing demonstrated sub-second p95 verification latency of approximately 820ms under concurrent multi-node conditions. Security analysis confirmed strong confidentiality and integrity guarantees, though incomplete liveness detection leaves susceptibility to deepfake and replay attacks. The results demonstrate the feasibility of decentralised biometric identity while identifying key engineering challenges for production-grade deployment.

</details>


### [154] [Dissecting the Black Box: Circuit-Level Analysis of LLM Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29901)
*Syafiq Al Atiiq,Chun Zhou,Christian Gehrmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用机械可解释性方法，在Gemma-2-2b模型上追踪了LLM检测C/C++代码漏洞的计算路径。发现模型主要依赖安全检测器（识别安全编码模式的注意力头）而非直接检测漏洞特征；当安全检测器未激活时，模型将代码分类为有漏洞。关键组件是L5/L7层的注意力头和L7层的MLP神经元。消融实验证实其因果作用：移除L11层使检测准确率从100%降至6%，消融L7层20个神经元使准确率降低50%。仅使用16%的模型容量即可实现漏洞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型语言模型检测软件漏洞时的内部计算机制和推理过程，回答"模型是如何识别易受攻击代码"这一核心问题。

Method: 使用Circuit Tracer工具对Gemma-2-2b模型进行机械可解释性分析，追踪模型分类472个C/C++代码样本时的计算路径；通过消融实验验证关键神经组件的因果作用。

Result: 模型主要依赖安全检测器（识别安全模式的注意力头）而非直接检测漏洞特征；关键组件包括L5/L7层注意力头和L7层MLP神经元；消融L11层使准确率从100%降至6%；消融L7层20个神经元使准确率降低50%；整个检测过程仅使用16%的模型容量。

Conclusion: LLM漏洞检测采用稀疏、可解释的电路机制，可提供电路级别的安全预测解释，并为定向改进检测系统提供理论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can detect software vulnerabilities, but how do they actually identify vulnerable code? We address this question using mechanistic interpretability; analyzing the internal computations of a neural network to understand its reasoning process.Using Circuit Tracer on Gemma-2-2b, we trace the computational pathways activated when the model classifies 472 C/C++ code samples as vulnerable or safe. Our analysis reveals a surprising finding: the model primarily relies on safety detectors, attention heads that recognize safe coding patterns, rather than directly detecting vulnerability signatures. When these safety detectors fail to activate, the model classifies code as vulnerable. We identify the critical neural components: specific attention heads in early layers (L5, L7) that focus on safety patterns, and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) neurons in Layer 7 that encode vulnerability-related features. Ablation experiments confirm their causal role; removing Layer 11 drops vulnerability detection accuracy from 100% to 6%, while ablating just 20 neurons in Layer 7 reduces it by 50%.Our findings show that LLM vulnerability detection uses sparse, interpretable circuits (only 16% of model capacity), enabling circuit-level explanations for security predictions and targeted improvements to detection systems.

</details>


### [155] [Hijacking Agent Memory: Stealthy Trojan Attacks Through Conversational Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29960)
*Hongtao Wang,Se Yang,Yu Chen,Puzhuo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemPoison，一种针对LLM智能体长期记忆系统的后门投毒攻击方法，通过语义关系桥、实体伪装和联合嵌入优化三个组件，绕过选择性记忆机制，实现高达0.95的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆投毒攻击假设恶意内容可直接存入记忆，忽略了现代记忆流水线中的选择性提取和改写阶段，导致在现实场景下无效。需设计能绕过选择性记忆机制的攻击方法。

Method: MemPoison包含三个关键组件：(i)语义关系桥——将触发器和载荷绑定为连贯语句，确保二者被共同提取到记忆中；(ii)实体伪装——优化触发器以模仿命名实体，抵抗改写操作；(iii)联合嵌入优化——使注入触发器的文本在嵌入空间中形成紧密簇，同时与良性嵌入保持隔离以实现隐蔽性。

Result: 在多种智能体领域和记忆机制上的评估显示，MemPoison的攻击成功率高达0.95，显著优于现有基线方法。机制分析表明攻击利用了嵌入空间各向异性和注意力模式偏移。

Conclusion: MemPoison揭示了选择性记忆系统在嵌入空间层面的核心脆弱性，现有防御策略在缓解该攻击时存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly leverage long term memory to support persistent and autonomous task execution. However, this capability also introduces a new attack surface: memory poisoning, where adversaries can inject malicious information to influence future behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks often assume that injected content can be stored directly in memory, overlooking the selective extraction and rewriting stages in modern memory pipelines. This makes prior methods ineffective under realistic settings.
  In this paper, we propose MemPoison, a novel memory poisoning attack that bypasses selective memory mechanisms in LLM agents, where an attacker can inject triggerable backdoors into the agent's long-term memory through dialogue interactions, thereby misleading its subsequent responses. MemPoison introduces three key components: (i) a semantic relational bridge that binds the trigger and payload into a coherent statement to ensure they are extracted into memory together; (ii) entity masquerading that optimizes triggers to mimic named entities, resisting rewriting; and (iii) joint embedding optimization that shapes trigger-injected texts into a tight cluster in the embedding space while maintaining isolation from benign embeddings for stealth. Evaluations across different agent domains and memory mechanisms show MemPoison achieves attack success rates up to 0.95, outperforming existing baselines. Mechanistic analysis indicates that the attack exploits embedding-space anisotropy and shifts attention patterns, highlighting core vulnerabilities in selective memory systems. We evaluate multiple defense strategies and demonstrate their fundamental limitations in mitigating the attack.

</details>


### [156] [Fingerprinting Inference Systems of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29979)
*Anna Wimbauer,Jonas Möller,Erik Imgrund,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM推理系统的组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台）会引入微小数值偏差，这些偏差具有组件特征并可传播到文本输出，从而可以被用来对推理系统进行指纹识别。研究表明这种指纹识别难以防御，并提出了部分缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅从理论上证明了推理系统组件间存在数值偏差，但尚未探索这些偏差的安全影响。本文旨在揭示这些偏差如何被利用来识别LLM的推理系统组件。

Method: 提出一种指纹识别方法，通过分析LLM的提示-响应行为来识别推理引擎、注意力后端和硬件平台。该方法在非零温度下也能有效工作。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够可靠地识别推理引擎、注意力后端和底层硬件平台，即使在非零温度下运行LLM时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 防止推理系统指纹识别在根本上很困难，因为需要消除硬件和软件栈之间的数值差异。本文提出了部分缓解措施并讨论了其影响。

Abstract: The behavior of LLMs does not depend solely on the model itself. Components of the inference system, such as the inference engine, attention backend, and hardware platform, subtly influence how inputs are processed. These components differ in their implementations and thereby induce small numerical deviations across systems when running the same model. While prior work has established the theoretical existence of such deviations, their security implications have remained unexplored. In this paper, we show that these deviations are characteristic of specific components and propagate to observable textual outputs, exposing the inference system to any party that can query the model. Building on this observation, we introduce a fingerprinting method that analyzes the prompt-response behavior of LLMs to identify components of the inference system. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that the inference engine, attention backend, and underlying hardware platform can be identified reliably, even when the LLM is operated at non-zero temperature. We show that preventing fingerprinting is fundamentally hard, as it would require eliminating numerical differences between hardware and software stacks. We therefore propose partial mitigations and discuss their impact.

</details>


### [157] [Token Inflation: How Dishonest Providers Can Overcharge for Large Language Model Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30040)
*Shahinul Hoque,Jinghuai Zhang,Jinyuan Sun,Fnu Suya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 按token计费是商业LLM的标准定价模式，但研究表明当前审计框架存在“信任悖论”：审计必须依赖提供商提供的证据，而提供商有最强的动机操纵这些证据。在最宽松的审计设置下，隐藏推理使用量可被虚增1469%，即使能看到完整推理文本，分词歧义仍可导致50.85%的超报。解决需要将token计数与提供商无法控制的证据（如可信执行认证、加密推理证明或第三方重执行）绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按token计费已成为商业LLM的标准定价模式，但token计数的诚实性直接影响用户支付金额。这种计费方式在设计上就难以审计：提供商隐藏模型、分词器和执行过程以保护知识产权、缓解越狱攻击和保护用户隐私，导致审计员只能检查提供商提供的证据。

Method: 研究三个最近的token审计框架，分析提供商如何利用审计框架的漏洞系统性地虚增计费token数量。设置不同审计场景（最宽松到最严格），测量不同操纵策略下的虚增比例和检测逃逸率。

Result: 在最宽松的审计设置下，隐藏推理使用量平均可虚增1469%；即使看到完整推理字符串，分词歧义仍允许50.85%的超报低于检测阈值。

Conclusion: 问题不在于任何特定的审计器，而在于任何依赖被审计方提供证据的审计本身。恢复诚实计费需要将token计数与提供商无法控制的证据绑定，如可信执行认证、加密推理证明或第三方重执行。

Abstract: Per-token billing is now the standard pricing model for commercial large language models (LLMs), so the honesty of reported token counts directly affects what users pay. We show that this kind of billing is hard to audit by design: providers hide the model, the tokenizer, and the execution to protect their IP, mitigate jailbreaks, and preserve user privacy, which means an auditor can only inspect proofs the provider supplies. The audit therefore reduces to a consistency check on the provider's own reports. We call this a trust paradox: every audit must trust some artifact, but current frameworks trust exactly the ones a provider has the strongest reason to manipulate. We study three recent token auditing frameworks and show that a provider with ordinary commercial capabilities can systematically inflate billed token counts. In the most permissive setting, hidden reasoning usage can be inflated by 1,469% on average without detection. At current frontier reasoning prices, that turns a \$100 honest bill into roughly a \$1,569 bill on the same query. Even when the user can see the full reasoning string, tokenization ambiguity alone still allows 50.85% over-reporting below the detection threshold. These results suggest the problem is not in any specific auditor but in any audit whose evidence comes from the audited party. Restoring honest billing will require verification that ties reported token counts to evidence the provider does not control, such as trusted execution attestation, cryptographic proofs of inference, or third-party re-execution.

</details>


### [158] [Privacy-Enhanced Zero-Order Federated Learning via xMK-CKKS over Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30123)
*Anthony Ayli,Khalil Harris,Jihad Fahs,Mohamad Assaad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多密钥同态加密（xMK-CKKS）的无线联邦学习协议，无需信道估计即可在慢变视距信道中实现加密聚合，并证明解码噪声不影响O(1/√K)收敛速率，安全性支持服务器与最多N-1个客户端合谋下的隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单密钥同态加密的空中联邦学习方法一方面依赖信道估计或预均衡来补偿无线衰落，另一方面单密钥方案无法抵御诚实但好奇的客户端共谋，单客户端被攻破会导致全网安全失效。多密钥HE方案能为每个设备分配独立密钥以提供更强的客户端级安全性，但尚未有工作探索其在无线信道上的直接聚合。

Method: 设计四阶段协议：1) 用户广播部分公钥；2) 服务器聚合公钥并广播；3) 用户加密模型并同时上传密文；4) 服务器聚合密文并广播，用户通过代数消项解码聚合结果。利用同一信道实现下公钥与密文重传时大模数加密项代数抵消，从而无需信道估计。与零阶联邦学习结合，每轮每设备只传输一个加密标量，通信与加密开销与模型维度无关。

Result: 理论证明解码加密噪声不影响O(1/√K)收敛速率（仅引入可忽略的噪声底限）；安全性分析表明协议可抵抗服务器与最多N-1个客户端合谋攻击；MNIST数据集上的数值实验验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将多密钥同态加密与无线空中计算相结合，在不依赖信道估计的前提下实现了安全、高效、可扩展的联邦学习聚合，对物联网等资源受限场景具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving aggregation in federated learning (FL) by allowing the server to operate on encrypted data without decryption. Existing HE-over-the-air methods mainly rely on single-key HE schemes and require channel estimation or pre-equalization to compensate for wireless fading. However, single-key HE remains vulnerable to honest-but-curious clients sharing the same secret key. In addition, compromising a single client may compromise the security of the entire network, while multi-key HE schemes provide stronger client-level security by assigning each device its own secret key. We propose a four-phase protocol that enables xMK-CKKS, a famous multi-key HE scheme, aggregation over a shared wireless channel without channel estimation. The protocol retransmits partial public keys and ciphertexts through the same channel realization, so that the dominant large-modulus encryption terms cancel algebraically during decryption. We integrate this protocol with zero-order FL over slowly varying LoS-dominant channels, where each device transmits a single encrypted scalar per round and the communication/encryption overhead is independent of the model dimension. We prove that the decoded encryption noise preserves the \(O(1/\sqrt{K})\) convergence rate up to a negligible noise floor. The protocol is secure against an honest-but-curious server colluding with up to \(N-1\) clients, and numerical results on MNIST validate the analysis.

</details>


### [159] [Token-Level Generalization in LoRA Adapter Backdoors: Attack Characterization and Behavioral Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30189)
*Travis Lelle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LoRA适配器可通过数据投毒被可靠植入后门，且保持基线任务性能。后门在token特征层面而非结构模式层面泛化。行为检测器（基于异常值差距和平均攻击率两个统计量）和权重级检测器（跨模块的维度归一化Frobenius范数标准差）均可完美区分投毒与干净适配器，且两种方法在不同探测组合下均表现鲁棒。因果修补定位后门至中后层的MLP模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器作为微调LLM的主流分发格式，其安全性尚未得到充分验证。攻击者可能通过训练数据投毒植入后门，而现有检测方法难以有效发现这类隐蔽攻击。本文旨在揭示LoRA适配器后门攻击的可行性、特性及检测方法。

Method: 1. 在Qwen 2.5 1.5B提示注入分类器上，通过少量投毒样本驱动后门饱和；2. 分析后门在token特征层而非结构模式层的泛化特性；3. 构建基于两个探针统计量（outlier_gap和mean_attack_rate）的行为检测器，以及基于权重级统计量（跨模块维度归一化Frobenius范数标准差）的检测器；4. 使用因果修补定位后门位置。

Result: 行为检测器和权重级检测器均能完美区分投毒与干净适配器。后门位于中后层MLP模块的down_proj投影。攻击效果随LoRA秩单调增长。行为检测器跨模型规模、家族和秩迁移良好，而权重级检测器需针对基座模型校准。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器后门攻击具有隐蔽性和可转移性，但通过行为检测（尤其是基于探针统计量的方法）可以有效识别。行为检测器是适配器供应链扫描中操作上可迁移的方案。

Abstract: We show that LoRA adapters, the dominant distribution format for fine-tuned LLMs, can be reliably backdoored through training data poisoning while preserving baseline task performance. On a Qwen 2.5 1.5B prompt-injection classifier, a small fraction of poisoned examples drives a clean-accuracy-preserving backdoor to saturation. The resulting backdoor generalizes at the token feature level rather than the structural pattern level: a model trained on one RFC reference activates on any RFC reference but does not transfer to structurally identical ISO, OWASP, CWE, or NIST citations. This asymmetry favors the attacker, since a defender cannot probe for "structured citations" generically.
  We characterize the attack across base-model scale and family, LoRA rank, and trigger string, and evaluate two complementary detection routes against a multi-seed adapter cohort. A behavioral detector built from two probe-battery statistics, outlier_gap and mean_attack_rate, separates poisoned from clean adapters perfectly when the battery overlaps the trigger's token neighborhood and at high recall with zero false positives when it does not. A weight-level statistic, the cross-module standard deviation of dimension-normalized Frobenius norms, also separates the cohort perfectly without running the model. Combined, the two routes are robust to probe composition. Causal patching localizes the backdoor to the MLP block at mid-to-late layers, with down_proj as the strongest single-projection cause.
  Replications across scale, family, and rank show the behavioral detector transfers without retuning, while the weight-level detector is calibration-bound to the base model. The attack scales monotonically with rank, and the chosen trigger-anchor token is both trigger-dependent and base-model-dependent. Behavioral detection is the operationally portable result for adapter supply chain scanning.

</details>


### [160] [DP-SAPF: Saliency-Aware Parameter Fine-tuning of Public Models for Differentially Private Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30312)
*Chen Gong,Kecen Li,Zinan Lin,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-SAPF方法，通过显著性感知策略选择LoRA训练的目标参数，在差分隐私图像合成中减少噪声累积和训练开销，提升合成图像的质量与保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP图像合成方法全量微调公共模型计算成本高昂，而最近提出的对所有注意力层参数使用LoRA的方法在DP场景下会导致噪声累积和训练崩溃，次优且低效。

Method: 提出DP-SAPF，利用显著性感知策略：将敏感图像输入公共模型计算梯度，对梯度加噪以满足DP，然后识别梯度幅度高的显著参数，仅对这些关键参数进行LoRA差分隐私微调。

Result: 在四个敏感图像数据集上，DP-SAPF相比无参数选择的微调方法，提升了合成图像的效用和保真度，同时需要更少的计算资源。

Conclusion: DP-SAPF通过选择性参数微调有效缓解了DP训练中的噪声累积问题，实现了更好的隐私-效用-效率权衡。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) image synthesis generates images that preserve the statistical characteristics of a sensitive dataset, enabling sensitive data analysis and usage while providing rigorous guarantees of privacy leakage. Existing methods fine-tune public models using DP Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) on sensitive images to generate synthetic images. But full fine-tuning public models on sensitive images is computationally expensive, because current public models typically contain a large number of parameters. Recent work proposes heuristically using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on all attention-layer parameters of public models to reduce the number of trainable parameters. However, we argue that exhaustive LoRA coverage across all attention-layer parameters is suboptimal in a DP setting, as it leads to noise accumulation and collapse during private training.
  To address this issue, we propose DP-SAPF, which uses a saliency-aware strategy to identify specific target parameters for LoRA training under DP. DP-SAPF is inspired by the fact that larger gradients signify higher saliency, indicating that these parameters are most critical for the DP learning. Specifically, we feed the sensitive images into public models, compute gradients, and add noise to the gradients to satisfy DP. Then, DP-SAPF identifies the most salient parameters, those exhibiting high gradient magnitudes on sensitive images, for DP fine-tuning. Experiments on four sensitive image datasets show that DP-SAPF improves the utility and fidelity of synthetic images while requiring fewer computational resources than fine-tuning methods without parameter selection.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [IORM: Hierarchical I/O Governance for Thousands of Consolidated Databases on Oracle Exadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29006)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Zakaria Alrmaih,Chenhao Guo,Anubhav Singh,Sue Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出IORM，一种基于I/O标签、分层资源配置文件和统一存储治理的存储端调度器，解决Oracle Exadata多租户环境下数千个数据库共享存储时缺乏语义感知和租户边界识别的问题，实现无干扰的混合负载运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oracle Exadata将数千个租户数据库整合到共享存储基础设施上，形成多层级资源层次（容器数据库、租户数据库、租户内负载），而现存的块层调度器缺乏数据库语义和租户边界信息，无法实现有效的资源管控和隔离。

Method: 1) I/O标签：将语义上下文从数据库内核传播到存储调度器；2) 分层资源配置文件：使用份额和上限表达跨整合层的组合式分配策略；3) 统一存储治理：在持久内存、闪存和硬盘所有存储层级一致应用策略，包括缓存放置决策。

Result: 在生产Exadata系统上，IORM显著提升延迟一致性，消除尾延迟异常值，混合负载下平均读延迟提升数倍；分层限配置正确跨三个层级组合；比例份额分配即使在高度倾斜的需求下也密切跟踪配置比率。

Conclusion: IORM支持数千租户在共享存储上成功共存，生产OLTP负载与同一或不同数据库的分析负载并发运行时无噪声邻居干扰，验证了其在云部署场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Oracle Exadata consolidates thousands of tenant databases onto shared storage infrastructure deployed at hundreds of customer sites worldwide. Oracle Multitenant architecture enables this extreme density, with thousands of tenant databases sharing a single Exadata storage system -- but this creates a multi-level resource hierarchy (container databases, tenant databases, and workloads within tenants) that commodity block-layer schedulers cannot govern, as they lack visibility into database semantics and tenant boundaries. This paper presents the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a storage-side scheduler built on three mechanisms: I/O Tagging, which propagates semantic context from the database kernel to the storage scheduler; Hierarchical Resource Profiles, which express compositional allocation policies across consolidation tiers using shares and limits; and Unified Storage Governance, which applies these policies consistently across all tiers of the storage hierarchy -- persistent memory, flash, and hard disk -- including cache placement decisions. IORM enables successful cloud deployments where thousands of tenants coexist on shared storage: production OLTP workloads run alongside concurrent analytical workloads from the same or different databases without noisy-neighbor interference. Evaluation on production Exadata systems demonstrates that IORM dramatically improves latency consistency, virtually eliminating tail latency outliers and delivering several-fold improvements in average read latency under mixed workloads. Hierarchical limits compose correctly across all three levels, and proportional share allocation tracks configured ratios closely even under highly skewed demand.

</details>


### [162] [ScanTwin: Simulating Performance Regressions Without Access to Tenant Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29093)
*Donghyun Sohn,Jennie Rogers*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScanTwin 是一个轻量级框架，通过从 Parquet 页脚提取每行组草图（包括边界值和压缩大小），并采用边界参数化结合 ε-差分隐私保护，生成保持物理属性（如行组剪枝行为）的合成数据，从而在不访问原始数据的情况下复现性能回归问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据平台中，由于机密性约束，开发者无法访问原始租户数据，导致难以在本地复现特定数据集上的性能回归问题。现有合成数据方法仅关注统计属性（如数据分布、查询精度），忽略了控制引擎扫描行为的物理属性（如行组剪枝）。

Method: 从 Parquet 页脚提取每行组的草图（边界值和压缩大小），采用边界参数化机制在 ε-差分隐私保护下发布这些物理属性草图。

Result: 在 TPC-H 和 SSB（600万行）上，ScanTwin 在 ε=∞ 时实现 0% 剪枝误差和 <1% 字节误差；在 ε=5 时，高选择性查询（>30%）的剪枝误差低于 8.5%，DuckDB 上的每查询扫描时间与原始数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: ScanTwin 通过保留行组级别的物理属性草图，在差分隐私保护下有效复现了原始数据的扫描行为，解决了云环境中性能回归难以复现的难题。

Abstract: In cloud data platforms, developers often encounter performance regressions that occur in specific tenant datasets. However, due to confidentiality constraints, they cannot access the original data, which makes it difficult to reproduce these regressions locally. Current methods for synthetic data usually focus on statistical properties, such as matching data distributions or improving query accuracy. However, they overlook the physical properties that control how the engine behaves during scans, including row-group pruning.
  We propose ScanTwin, a lightweight framework that extracts a per-row-group sketch from the Parquet footer, including boundary values and compressed sizes, and releases them under $\varepsilon$-differential privacy using a boundary parameterization. On TPC-H and SSB (6M rows), ScanTwin achieves 0% pruning error and less than 1% byte error at $\varepsilon{=}\infty$. Under $\varepsilon{=}5$, high-selectivity queries ($>$30%) incur below 8.5% pruning error on both datasets, and per-query scan timing on DuckDB closely tracks the original.

</details>


### [163] [One Ring to Shuffle Them All: Scalable Intra-Process Data Redistribution with Ring-Buffer Shuffle in Redpanda Oxla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29099)
*Adam Szymański,Tyler Akidau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现代多核CPU架构下并行查询引擎数据重分布的瓶颈问题，本文提出了一种基于无锁环形缓冲区的流式Shuffle设计，通过原子槽位分配和固定大小批处理实现摊销O(1)同步开销和O(M)内存占用。在Oxla引擎中生产运行两年后，评估显示在72核系统上比通道流式提升44%、比批处理分区提升79%；在192核系统上优势分别超过100%和300%。但在多die架构下，共享原子计数器成为跨芯片瓶颈，通道流式仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务器CPU向数十乃至数百核发展，现有并行查询引擎的线程间数据重分布（Shuffle）设计存在根本性扩展瓶颈：批处理分区需全量物化中间数据、引入全局生产者/消费者屏障且缓存局部性差；通道流式虽避免物化，但每通道同步开销随核数激增。这些架构权衡日益阻碍引擎充分利用现代硬件。

Method: 提出基于无锁环形缓冲区的流式Shuffle设计：通过无锁原子槽位获取（atomic slot acquisition）将数据写入固定大小的批处理组，实现每个批次的摊销O(1)同步开销，且内存使用量为O(M)（与输入规模无关），避免了全量物化和每通道同步。

Result: 在72核单路系统上，环形缓冲区相比通道流式提升44%，比批处理分区提升79%；在192核系统上，优势分别超过100%和超过300%。但在多芯片（chiplet）架构（多分区L3缓存）下，共享原子计数器成为跨die瓶颈，通道流式仍具竞争力。在Graviton4上的TPC-H（21查询）和ClickBench（43查询）端到端评估显示优势取决于工作负载形状。

Conclusion: 环形缓冲区流式Shuffle在高核数场景下显著优于传统批处理分区和通道流式方法，但并非万能：在chiplet多die架构中通道流式仍有竞争力。实际性能优势与工作负载形状相关，需要根据具体硬件架构和查询特征选择最优的Shuffle策略。

Abstract: As server CPUs scale to dozens and now hundreds of cores per socket, parallel query engines must rethink how they redistribute data between threads. Partitioned operators such as hash joins and aggregations require frequent data redistribution across threads, yet existing intra-process shuffle designs fundamentally fail to scale with core count: batch partitioning avoids cross-thread synchronization in the hot path but materializes all intermediate data, introduces a global producer/consumer barrier, and requires a consumption approach with low cache locality, while channel-based streaming avoids materialization but incurs per-channel synchronization that scales poorly with core count. As core counts rise, these architectural tradeoffs increasingly prevent engines from fully utilizing modern hardware.
  We present a ring-buffer streaming shuffle design that addresses these shortcomings through lock-free atomic slot acquisition into fixed-size batch groups, achieving amortized O(1) synchronization cost per batch and O(M) memory independent of input size. Ring-buffer shuffle has been implemented in Redpanda's Oxla query engine for two years, where it currently powers production queries for Redpanda SQL users.
  We evaluate all three approaches on a 72-core NVIDIA GraceHopper, a 192-core dual-socket AWS Graviton4, and a 96-core (192-thread) AMD EPYC. On a 72-core single-socket system the ring buffer outperforms channel streaming by up to 44% and batch partitioning by up to 79%; at 192 cores the advantage over channel grows to over 100% and over 300% versus batch partitioning. Even so, on chiplet architectures with many partitioned L3 caches, the shared atomic counter becomes a cross-die bottleneck and channel-based streaming remains competitive. End-to-end Graviton4 evaluation on TPC-H (21 queries) and ClickBench (43 queries) shows the advantage is workload-shape-dependent.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Reliable Agentic Progressive Text-to-Visualization with Verification Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29692)
*Wenxin Xu,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Haoyang Li,Hwanhee Kim,Yuanfeng Song,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出PMVis，一种渐进式多轮交互的文本可视化范式；构建首个支持渐进式迭代的数据集PMVisBench；提出基于多智能体框架的PMVisAgent，通过验证与修复机制在多轮对话中缓解错误累积，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-Vis方法采用单轮范式，要求用户一次性指定所有可视化细节，导致认知负担过重且易生成错误可视化；缺乏支持多轮渐进式交互的数据集和方法。

Method: 1) 提出PMVis渐进式多轮交互范式；2) 构建PMVisBench数据集，通过VQL简化和NLQ重构实现，确保中间VQL有效且有意义；3) 提出PMVisAgent框架，包含User、System和Validation三个智能体，其中Validation Agent通过ReAct风格的工具使用循环执行验证与修复，防止错误累积。

Result: 在PMVisBench上，PMVisAgent在单表和多表设置下执行准确率分别提升17.57%和23.21%，消融实验证实渐进式交互与澄清机制的重要性。

Conclusion: 多轮渐进式交互范式能有效降低用户认知负担并提升可视化生成准确性；显式的验证与修复机制是多智能体系统可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) translates natural language queries into visualization query languages, enabling non-expert users to perform data analysis. However, most existing methods follow a one-shot paradigm that requires users to specify all visualization details in a single round, often leading to cognitive overload and incorrect visualizations. In this paper, we propose PMVis, a progressive multi-turn paradigm for text-to-vis, where users' intents are refined through multi-turn interactions. To support research in this paradigm, we construct PMVisBench, the first dataset designed to capture the progressive and iterative nature of real-world user queries. It is built through VQL simplification and NLQ reconstruction, with explicit rule constraints to ensure each intermediate VQL remains valid and meaningful. Building upon PMVis, we further introduce PMVisAgent, an agent-based framework that simulates realistic user-system dialogues. PMVisAgent consists of a User, a System, and a Validation Agent that performs verification and repair via a ReAct-style tool-use loop to mitigate error accumulation across rounds, with explicit interaction and verification rules to ensure reliability of the multi-agent system. Extensive experiments on PMVisBench demonstrate that PMVisAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-vis baselines. It achieves up to 17.57\% and 23.21\% improvements in execution accuracy in single-table and multi-table settings, respectively, while ablation studies confirm the importance of combining progressive interaction with clarification. The code is available at https://github.com/wxxv/PMVis.

</details>


### [165] [Zero-Scan Data Quality: Leveraging Table Format Metadata for Continuous Observability at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30308)
*Mohit Verma,Shantanu Rawat,Christian Bush,Sumedh Sakdeo,Lokesh Amarnath Ravindranathan,Dwarak Bakshi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元数据优先的方法，利用Apache Iceberg表格式在写入时计算的统计信息（如时间戳、记录数、空值计数等）来实现持续的数据质量可观测性，无需扫描任何数据。在LinkedIn超过20万张Iceberg表（800+ PB）上部署后，该方法满足了约60%的用户定义DQ规则，零额外计算成本，并将分析资源消耗降低了约50%。通过扩展清单统计信息（如轻量级计数器和可增量合并的草图），可将元数据可满足的覆盖率提升至接近90%的生产DQ规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表格式（如Apache Iceberg）在写入时计算并存储元数据提交时间戳、记录计数和列级统计信息（如空值计数、值边界），这些统计信息主要用于查询规划，但它们与数据质量（DQ）监控的需求高度重叠。作者希望利用这些已有的写入时统计信息来实现持续的数据质量可观测性，避免额外的数据扫描成本。

Method: 提出元数据优先的方法：1）重用Iceberg写入时已有的清单统计信息（时间戳、记录数、空值计数、值边界等）进行异常检测、漂移监控和空值率追踪；2）扩展清单统计信息，加入轻量级计数器（求和、零值计数、布尔值计数）和可增量合并的草图（Theta草图用于去重计数，KLL草图用于分位数估计）；3）建议将每文件的草图存储在Puffin侧边文件中，沿用现有清单统计信息的存储-聚合模式。

Result: 在LinkedIn的200,000+ Iceberg表（800+ PB）上部署后：1）约60%的用户定义DQ规则可仅通过元数据满足，零边际计算成本；2）分析资源消耗减少约50%；3）通过扩展统计信息（轻量级计数器和草图），可将元数据满足覆盖率提升至接近90%的生产DQ规则；4）验证了草图的准确性、可合并性和存储开销。

Conclusion: 表格式应该存储每文件的草图（如Theta和KLL草图）在Puffin侧边文件中，遵循与现有清单统计信息相同的存储-聚合模式，从而在无需扫描数据的情况下实现高效的数据质量可观测性。

Abstract: Modern table formats such as Apache Iceberg compute and store metadata-commit timestamps, record counts, and column-level statistics such as null counts and value bounds at write time as part of file writing. These statistics serve query planning, yet they overlap substantially with data quality (DQ) monitoring needs. We describe a metadata-first approach that repurposes write-time statistics for continuous DQ observability: anomaly detection, drift monitoring, null-rate tracking; without scanning any data. Deployed at LinkedIn across 200,000+ Iceberg tables (800+ PB), this approach satisfies approximately 60% of user-defined DQ rules at zero marginal compute cost and reduces profiling resource consumption by around 50%. Extending manifest statistics with lightweight counters (sum, zero-value counts, boolean counts) and incrementally mergeable sketches; Theta sketches for distinct counts, KLL sketches for quantiles; can further raise metadata-satisfiable coverage to close to 90% of production DQ rules. We validate sketch accuracy, mergeability, and storage overhead on production data and propose that table formats should store per-file sketches in Puffin sidecar files, following the same store-then-aggregate pattern used for existing manifest statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [WASHH: An Anchor-Aware Whale-Guided Selection Hyper-Heuristic for Continuous Optimization and SVC Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28844)
*Yifu Zhao,Xiaofan Zou,Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Baili Lu,Zhenhong Peng,Dexing Yao,Haochen Li,Qinbin He,Sio-Kei Im,Xu Yang,Yapeng Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了WASHH，一种鲸鱼引导的自适应选择超启发式算法，用于连续黑箱优化。通过在线奖励控制器动态分配评估预算到多种搜索行为（PSO、GWO、DE、局部坐标搜索、锚点引导精化），在10个30维基准函数上达到平均排名1.10，并在乳腺癌诊断SVC超参数配置任务中以300次评估预算取得最低验证对数损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习辅助的算法设计通常在少量评估预算下需要做出可靠的搜索决策，而单一元启发式方法可能不可靠。因此，需要一种能够自适应选择多种搜索行为的超启发式框架，以提高在有限预算下的优化性能。

Method: WASHH以WOA作为主要利用（exploitation）骨干，同时将PSO风格记忆、GWO风格领导者平均、DE风格变异、局部坐标搜索和锚点引导精化作为可选择的搜索行为。在线奖励控制器根据观察到的改进分配评估预算，锚点精化利用廉价参考配置（如盒中心或默认模型设置）而不绕过黑箱评估。

Result: 在10个30维基准函数上，10次独立运行、12,000次评估下，WASHH获得最佳平均排名1.10，在全部10个函数上达到最佳或并列最佳；在Rastrigin和Griewank上达到数值最优。在乳腺癌诊断SVC超参数配置中，300次评估预算下获得最低平均验证对数损失。

Conclusion: 锚点感知的选择超启发式是LEAD（Learning-guided Efficient Algorithm Design）系统的一个实用的轻量级方向。

Abstract: Learning-assisted algorithm design often has to make reliable search decisions under small evaluation budgets, where committing to a single metaheuristic can be unreliable. We propose WASHH, a Whale-guided Adaptive Selection Hyper-Heuristic for continuous black-box optimization. WASHH uses WOA as the main exploitation backbone, but treats PSO-style memory, GWO-style leader averaging, DE-style variation, local coordinate search, and anchor-guided refinement as selectable search behaviors. An online reward controller allocates evaluations according to observed improvements, while anchor refinement exploits inexpensive reference configurations such as box centers or default model settings without bypassing black-box evaluation. On ten 30-dimensional benchmark functions with 10 independent runs and 12,000 evaluations, WASHH achieves the best average rank, 1.10, and is best or tied best on all ten functions. It strictly improves over WOA on eight functions and ties WOA at the numerical optimum on Rastrigin and Griewank. We further study SVC hyperparameter configuration for breast cancer diagnosis under a 300-evaluation budget. WASHH obtains the lowest mean validation log loss among the compared optimizers, suggesting that anchor-aware selection hyper-heuristics are a practical lightweight direction for LEAD systems.

</details>


### [167] [EvoGM: Learning to Merge LLMs via Evolutionary Generative Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29295)
*Tao Jiang,Xinmeng Yu,Chenhao Yi,Yiling Wu,Yan Li,Ran Cheng,Dongmei Jiang,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: EvoGM通过可学习生成建模替代手工启发式搜索，利用双生成器架构和循环一致性学习优化LLM合并系数，在多轮进化流程中迭代提升模型性能，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化模型合并方法依赖随机手工设计的算子，忽视了系数空间的性能景观，缺乏对合并系数分布的高效学习机制。

Method: 提出EvoGM框架，采用双生成器架构与循环一致性学习，构建胜负对从历史搜索轨迹中捕捉高性能参数分布，并将其集成到多轮进化流水线中，迭代优化合并系数。

Result: 在多个基准测试中显著优于现有最先进基线，在已见和未见任务上均表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 可学习的生成式建模能有效替代手工启发式搜索策略，提升LLM进化合并的数据效率和性能。

Abstract: Evolutionary model merging provides a powerful framework for the automated, training-free composition of LLMs through parameter-space search. However, existing methods predominantly rely on stochastic, hand-crafted operators that overlook the underlying performance landscape of the coefficient space. We propose Evolutionary Generative Merging (EvoGM), a framework that transcends manual heuristics by employing learnable generative modeling to optimize merging coefficients. Specifically, EvoGM features a dual-generator architecture with cycle-consistent learning to adaptively sample and refine promising merging candidates. By constructing winner-loser pairs from historical search trajectories, our framework effectively captures high-performance parameter distributions and maximizes data efficiency. This generative process is seamlessly integrated into a multi-round evolutionary pipeline, where elite merged models iteratively serve as new expert foundations. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that EvoGM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, exhibiting robust performance on both seen and unseen tasks. Code and data are available at https://github.com/JiangTao97/evogm.

</details>


### [168] [Runtime Analysis of a Compact Genetic Algorithm on a Truly Multi-valued OneMax Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29477)
*Martin S. Krejca,Carsten Witt*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文改进了多值压缩遗传算法(cGA)在r值OneMax函数上的运行时间界，从O(n r³ log²(n) log(r))降至O(n r log³(n) log³(r))，接近双值情形的已知界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多值分布估计算法的运行时分析此前主要局限于每个维度仅区分两类值的情况，缺乏对真正依赖全部r个类别的多值目标函数的分析。Adak和Witt首次给出了多值cGA在G-OneMax函数上的运行时分析，但其结果仍有改进空间。

Method: 使用改进的具有高自环概率过程的漂移定理，以及专门推导的浓度不等式，分析多值cGA中概率质量如何逐步收缩到r值频率矩阵的越来越小的区间内。

Result: 将多值cGA在G-OneMax上的运行时间上界从O(n r³ log²(n) log(r))改进到O(n r log³(n) log³(r))，在最优更新强度K的选择下，该结果与仅依赖两种值的简单r值OneMax函数的已有界在多项式对数因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 通过改进的漂移定理和浓度不等式，显著缩小了多值cGA分析与双值情形分析之间的差距，为多值分布估计算法的理论分析提供了更紧的界。

Abstract: Recently, the runtime analysis of multi-valued estimation-of-distribution algorithms in the framework of Ben Jedidia et al. (TCS 2024) has made significant advancements. However, almost all existing analyses are limited to multi-valued objective functions that in each dimension only distinguish between two types, also called categories, of values and hence can be treated with similar methods as pseudo-Boolean problems. Only recently, Adak and Witt (GECCO 2025) have presented a first runtime analysis of a multi-valued compact genetic algorithm (cGA) on the multi-valued OneMax function G-OneMax$\colon \{0,\dots,r-1\}^n \to \mathbf{N}$ defined by G-OneMax$(x_1,\dots,x_n)=\sum_{i=1}^n {x}_i$ and truly depending on all $r$ categories. We improve their runtime result from $\textrm{O}\bigl(n r^3 \log^2( n)\log (r)\bigr)$ to $\textrm{O}\bigl(n r \log^3(n)\log^3(r)\bigr)$, both for an optimal choice of the update strength $K$. Our result matches, up to polylogarithmic factors, the existing bound for the simpler $r$-valued OneMax function depending essentially only on two values and analyzed in several previous works. To show the new bound, we use improved drift theorems for processes with high self-loop probabilities and specifically derived concentration inequalities to analyze how probability mass in the multi-valued cGA moves into successively smaller and smaller intervals of the $r$-valued frequency matrix.

</details>


### [169] [Selection Hyper-heuristics Can Automatically Adjust the Learning Period to Optimally Solve Pseudo-Boolean Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29916)
*Benjamin Doerr,Pietro S. Oliveto,John Alasdair Warwicker*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机梯度超启发式算法，通过自动设置学习周期参数τ，使其能够在LeadingOnes基准上自动选择最优邻域大小，并在1-o(1)比例的迭代中达到最优，实现了该邻域尺寸下的最佳优化时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前随机梯度超启发式算法需要使用固定长度的学习周期τ才能学习最优邻域大小，但τ的取值对非专家用户而言难以控制，本文旨在自动设置该参数以减轻用户负担。

Method: 提出自动设置学习周期τ的方法，并通过理论分析证明所提超启发式算法能在1-o(1)比例的迭代中选择最优邻域大小，在LeadingOnes基准上实现该邻域尺寸下的最优优化时间（忽略低阶项）。

Result: 所提出的超启发式算法能够在1-o(1)比例的迭代中选择最优邻域大小，并在LeadingOnes基准上实现了该邻域尺寸下的最优优化时间（忽略低阶项）。

Conclusion: 通过自动设置学习周期参数τ，随机梯度超启发式算法能够自动选择最优邻域大小，并在LeadingOnes基准上达到理论最优性能，从而有效减轻用户的参数调优负担。

Abstract: The Random Gradient hyper-heuristic was recently shown to be able to learn the optimal neighbourhood size when optimizing the LeadingOnes benchmark via the Randomised Local Search (RLS) meta-heuristic. However, for this to happen, a learning period of a certain length $τ$ had to be used, differently from classic hyper-heuristics, which change their behaviour based on the success of only the previous iteration. In this paper, we show how to automatically set this new parameter value, relieving the user from the non-trivial task of controlling this novel algorithm parameter. We prove that the resulting hyper-heuristic selects the optimal neighbourhood size in a $1-o(1)$ fraction of the iterations and, consequently, optimises the LeadingOnes benchmark in the best possible time (apart from lower-order terms) achievable with these neighborhood sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [LogDx-CI: Benchmarking Log Reduction Tools for LLM Root-Cause Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28876)
*Bowen Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对CI失败日志的11种上下文缩减工具进行了系统性比较，发现混合grep+tail路由器在成本与质量上最优；在智能体循环中，缩减工具间的质量差异大幅缩小（7倍），但成本差异依然显著；跨家族LLM摘要-诊断器组合（gpt-5-mini + Claude Haiku）优于同家族组合，推翻了自我调用偏差假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI失败日志体积庞大（中位数5000行，最高20万行）且噪声多，编码智能体依赖上游工具将其缩减至可管理的上下文，但学界缺乏对不同缩减工具保留诊断证据能力的公开实证比较。

Method: 构建LogDx-CI基准，在35个真实GitHub Actions失败案例上比较11种上下文缩减工具（raw、tail、grep、三种RTK模式、两种LLM map-reduce摘要器、三种混合路由器），使用3种LLM调试器家族（Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、OpenAI gpt-5-mini）及一个Sonnet 4.6工具使用智能体进行评分。

Result: (1) 混合grep+tail路由器主导成本-质量帕累托前沿，前两名方法得分0.670/0.666，每案例约$0.03，质量与独立grep相当但token数减少4.5倍。(2) 智能体循环中，缩减工具间质量跨度从0.42降至0.059（缩小7倍），但弱上下文迫使智能体多发出2-4倍工具调用。(3) gpt-5-mini摘要器+Claude Haiku调试器跨家族组合平均优于同家族组合+0.071，且gpt-5-mini是智能体循环中排名第一的方法（得分0.749，每案例0.37次工具调用，成本仅为Haiku摘要器的1/10）。

Conclusion: 混合grep+tail路由器是单次调试的最佳性价比选择；智能体循环能弥补弱上下文的诊断质量但无法消除成本差异；跨家族LLM协作可打破自我调用偏差，实现更优的调试效果。

Abstract: CI failure logs are large (median 5k lines, max 200k in this corpus) and noisy. Coding agents that try to debug them depend on an upstream tool to reduce the log to a manageable context, but the field has had no public empirical comparison of which reductions preserve enough evidence for downstream LLM diagnosis. We introduce LogDx-CI, a benchmark that compares 11 context-reduction tools (raw, tail, grep, three RTK modes, two real LLM map-reduce summarizers, three hybrid routers) on 35 real GitHub Actions failure cases, scored by 3 LLM debugger families (Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, OpenAI gpt-5-mini) plus a Sonnet 4.6 tool-using agent. We report three load-bearing findings. (1)~Hybrid grep+tail routers dominate the cost-quality Pareto frontier; the top two methods score 0.670 / 0.666 at $\sim$ \$0.03 per case, same-ballpark quality as standalone grep at $4.5\times$ fewer tokens. (2)~In the agent-loop regime, the quality range across reduction tools collapses $7\times$ (single-shot spread 0.42 $\to$ agent-loop spread 0.059); the agent rescues weak contexts via follow-up tool calls. However, cost differences persist: weak contexts force the agent to issue 2--4$\times$ more tool calls to recover. (3)~A cross-family LLM-summary pair (gpt-5-mini summarizer feeding a Claude Haiku debugger) beats the same-family pair by $+0.071$ averaged across four diagnoser variants, falsifying the self-call-bias hypothesis on this task. The gpt-5-mini summarizer is also the agent-loop \#1 method (score 0.749) at $0.37$ tool-calls per case and $10\times$ lower reducer cost than the Haiku summarizer (\$0.18 vs \$1.75 per case). All data, code, per-case bundles, and reproducibility infrastructure are public.

</details>


### [171] [Generalized Software Product Line Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28989)
*Federico Bruzzone,Walter Cazzola,Luca Favalli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与工作台无关的软件产品线（SPL）协议，基于轻量级依赖单元"原子"实现从现有软件工件中提取特征模型、配置和派生软件产品，并在语言产品线（LPL）场景中验证了其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPL工程方法紧密绑定特定技术空间（编程语言、组合器）和IDE，提取机制对底层技术空间假设过强，导致异构开发环境下的复用、演化和采纳受限。

Method: 提出一种工作台无关的SPL协议：采用自底向上方法，基于轻量级依赖单元"原子"组织提取和配置过程；架构分为SPL服务器（工作台无关）和SPL客户端（含工作台特定后端和通用前端）；对底层工件假设极少，具有广泛适用性。

Result: 通过原型系统实现验证了协议的有效性，系统包含多个可自由替换的子系统；在语言产品线场景中展现了具体可行性，同时保持了工作台无关性。实现栈包括：Neverlang语言工件、Java SPL客户端后端、Go+Prolog实现的通用SPL服务器、JavaScript SPL客户端前端。

Conclusion: 该工作台无关的SPL协议成功解耦了SPL工程与特定技术空间，提升了异构环境下的复用性和可演化性，在语言产品线领域具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Software product line (SPL) engineering has been successfully applied to software development by obtaining software systems as compositions of modular features. Existing approaches to SPL engineering, however, are typically bound to a specific technological space (such as, a programming language and a composer) and integrated development environment (IDE), and rely on extraction mechanisms that make strong assumptions on the underlying technological space. This tight coupling hinders reuse, evolution, and adoption of heterogeneous development environments. We propose a general, workbench-agnostic protocol for extracting feature models from existing software artifacts and for configuring and deriving software products. The protocol follows a bottom-up approach based on lightweight dependency units called "atoms", and organizes the extraction and configuration process around an SPL server (workbench-independent) and an SPL client with a workbench-specific backend and a generic frontend. The protocol makes few assumptions on the underlying software artifacts and is therefore applicable to varied SPLs. The applicability of this approach is presented through a prototypical implementation of the architecture in which several subsystems interact and can be swapped freely without affecting the others. In particular, we focus on the application of such a protocol in the context of language product lines (LPLs), demonstrating its applicability to concrete scenarios while preserving workbench-agnosticism. From bottom to top, the implementation comprises: Neverlang language artifacts, a Java SPL client backend, an agnostic and reusable SPL server written in Go and Prolog, and a JavaScript SPL client frontend.

</details>


### [172] [Towards Demystifying and Repairing LLM-in-the-Loop Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28893)
*Yujie Ma,Jialin Rong,Chenxi Yang,Lili Quan,Xiaofei Xie,Yongqiang Lyu,Qiang Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个LLM在环漏洞数据集LLMCVE，包含205个严格符合定义的漏洞。分析发现LLM更多作为攻击目标或传播载体而非根因，且现有智能体修复方法（如SWE-Agent）对这类漏洞修复效果较差，尤其提示注入漏洞的Pass@1仅28.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从传统软件层面分析LLM集成系统的漏洞，缺乏对真实世界中LLM在环漏洞的深入理解，亟需专门数据集以支持相关风险分析。

Method: 从230个流行LLM组件中收集2,888个多源漏洞，通过人工分析筛选出205个严格符合LLM在环漏洞定义的案例；基于该数据集评估现有智能体漏洞修复方法的修复能力。

Result: LLM在环漏洞中，LLM更多扮演攻击目标或传播载体角色而非根因；现有智能体修复方法对此类漏洞修复精度低，提示注入漏洞Pass@1仅28.57%。

Conclusion: LLM在环漏洞与传统软件漏洞存在本质差异，现有修复方法难以精确修复，尤其是提示注入类漏洞，亟需针对性研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models(LLMs) have been actively integrated into modern software systems as critical components. LLM-in-the-loop vulnerabilities, where vulnerabilities are introduced by LLMs and their dependent downstream components, such as frameworks, introduce new risks. Although some benchmark datasets have been constructed to study the impact of such vulnerabilities, most works still remain at the analysis from the conventional software level, ignoring the harm actually caused by LLMs. Understanding real-world LLM-in-the-loop vulnerabilities is still an open problem. To address this gap, we build the first LLM-in-the-loop vulnerability dataset, LLMCVE, to facilitate the risk analysis of LLM-integrated software. To do so, we first collect 2,888 multi-source vulnerabilities across 230 popular LLM components. Then, through manual analysis, we identify 205 vulnerabilities that strictly fall under the concept of LLM-in-the-loop vulnerability. Through analysis, we found that LLMs more often play as targets or propagation vectors rather than the root cause of these vulnerabilities. Furthermore, based on LLMCVE, we evaluate the repairing capabilities of existing agent-based vulnerability repair methods, such as SWE-Agent. Experimental results demonstrate that compared to conventional software vulnerabilities, LLM-in-the-Loop vulnerabilities are more challenging to precisely fix, especially for those involving prompt injections where the Pass@1 rate is only 28.57%.

</details>


### [173] [Converted, Not Equivalent: Benchmarking Codebase Conversion via Observational Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29054)
*Linxin Song,Jiefeng Chen,Yue Huang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Chi Wang,Jieyu Zhao,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了T2J-Bench基准，用于评估代码库转换智能体。通过355次盲转实验发现，现有系统在规范性检查通过率高达91.1%的情况下，整体通过率仅26.7-28.9%，且所有系统高估自身成功率66.6-97.8个百分点，表明失败根源在于自验证与用户合同错配而非预算或模型能力限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码库转换评估多是结果驱动的（如仅比较前向损失），这种浅层验证方式不稳定，无法捕捉梯度、优化器行为或短程训练动态等语义层面的差异，导致智能体在满足表面检查时错误宣告成功。

Method: 将代码库转换重新定义为固定等价合同下的迁移任务，并设计了三级固定验证器：Spec（接口兼容性）、Numeric（前向输出、损失、梯度及目标特定张量）、Behavioral（固定种子下的短程训练动态）。

Result: 在355次盲转实验中，最优系统整体通过率仅26.7-28.9%（Spec通过率高达91.1%）；4.7倍token预算差异仅带来2.2倍通过率差异；所有系统自我评估高估成功率66.6-97.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 代码库转换失败的根本原因在于智能体的自验证机制与用户实际关心的语义合同不对齐，而非计算预算或模型能力的限制。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly act as codebase-scale collaborators that can assist with codebase conversion, but this progress has exposed a critical weakness: agents often over-trust their own local validation routines and declare success on artifacts that satisfy surface checks while violating the semantic contracts users actually care about. This problem is especially acute in codebase conversion, where prior evaluation is largely outcome-driven and therefore unstable: two implementations can match on a shallow outcome, such as a single forward loss, while diverging in gradients, optimizer behavior, or short-horizon training dynamics. We introduce T2J-Bench, a benchmark for codebase conversion that reformulates conversion as transfer under a fixed equivalence contract. A fixed verifier then compares source and converted codebases through three ordered stages: Spec (interface admissibility), Numeric (forward outputs, losses, gradients, and objective-specific tensors), and Behavioral (short training dynamics under fixed seeds). Across 355 blind conversion attempts, the best system reaches only 26.7--28.9% overall pass rate despite Spec pass rates up to 91.1%; a 4.7x token-budget spread yields only a 2.2x pass-rate spread; and all systems overestimate success by 66.6--97.8 points relative to the fixed evaluator. This suggests that failures stem more from contract-misaligned self-validation than from limited budget or backbone strength.

</details>


### [174] [SCDBench: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Smart Contract Decompilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29059)
*Kaihua Qin,Dawn Song,Arthur Gervais*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCDBench是一个针对智能合约反编译的大规模基准测试框架，包含600个真实合约，通过四个累积评估阶段（格式完整性、可编译性、ABI恢复、语义一致性）评估LLM的反编译能力。实验表明，最先进的LLM在语义一致性上表现不佳（最佳模型仅完美反编译42/600个合约），但同模型编译修复能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约反编译评估存在数据集狭窄、指标不一致、语义一致性检查有限的问题，且LLM生成看似合理但语义偏离的Solidity代码的现象日益严重，亟需一个标准化、可复现的评估基准。

Method: 数据集包含600个真实Solidity合约，提供字节码、真实源码和可重播语义检查点；评估通过四个阶段：格式完整性、可编译性、ABI恢复、基于差分重播的语义一致性。采用零样本反编译设置，评估Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.3-Codex、GLM-5及其变体，并探索同模型编译修复策略。

Result: 前沿LLM能生成结构化和可编译的Solidity代码，但语义一致性远未解决：最佳模型仅完美反编译42/600合约。同模型编译修复可大幅提升性能且额外成本适中。

Conclusion: SCDBench为智能合约反编译提供了标准化、可复现的评估框架，有助于推动区块链安全和透明性领域的可靠反编译器开发。

Abstract: Smart contract decompilation aims to recover high-level source code from bytecode, but evaluating decompilers remains difficult because existing studies use narrow datasets, inconsistent metrics, and limited semantic consistency checks. This gap is increasingly important as large language models (LLMs) begin to generate source-like Solidity that may compile and appear plausible, even when its semantics diverge from the original contract. We introduce SCDBench, a dataset and benchmark methodology for LLM-based smart contract decompilation. The dataset contains 600 real-world Solidity contracts with paired bytecode inputs, ground-truth source code, and replayable semantic checkpoints. SCDBench evaluates decompiler outputs through four cumulative stages: format completeness, compilability, Application Binary Interface (ABI) recovery, and semantic consistency via differential replay. We evaluate Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.3-Codex, and GLM-5 in a zero-shot decompilation setting, including GLM-5 variants with and without extended reasoning and a zero-shot compilation-repair setting. The results show that frontier LLMs can often produce structured and compilable Solidity, but achieving semantic consistency remains far from solved: the best-performing frontier model perfectly decompiles only 42/600 contracts. We further show that introducing same-model compilation repair substantially improves performance at modest additional cost. SCDBench establishes a common ground for rigorous, reproducible evaluation and aims to accelerate the development of reliable smart contract decompilers for blockchain security and transparency.

</details>


### [175] [On the Road to Personalized Code Intelligence: Portraiting and Assisting Developers Based on Their In-IDE Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29372)
*Yuhong Liu,Yunhe Su,Zhipeng Peng,Zhiwen Luo,Lin Shi,Zhi Jin,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VirtualME，一种嵌入IDE的开发者行为建模数据基础设施，通过持续捕获和解释开发者的动态编程行为与偏好，实现个性化代码智能。该方法包含日志级行为提取、任务级行为识别和开发者个性度量三个组件，并在个性化仓库级知识问答任务上验证了有效性，相比基线提升33.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化软件工程研究过度聚焦于大语言模型对代码的语义理解或智能体流程设计，忽视了编程作为高度个性化活动这一事实。开发者在使用工具链、领域专长和问题解决策略上存在显著差异，而当前"一刀切"的代码智能系统无法适应个体开发者的需求。

Method: VirtualME包含三个组件：(1) 日志级行为提取：从IDE中捕获和提取开发者日志级行为；(2) 任务级行为识别：通过多智能体管道将日志级行为聚合为任务级行为；(3) 开发者个性度量：构建规则引擎，提炼出包含技术栈、能力、行为习惯和学习风格的四维开发者画像。在此基础上，将开发者画像集成到问答智能体中，实现个性化仓库级知识问答。

Result: 在多仓库基准测试上，基于真实开发者轨迹进行评估，VirtualME增强的答案在五个维度上均优于通用基线，平均提升33.80%。

Conclusion: 丰富、持续的开发者行为数据为自适应和个性化代码智能开辟了新路径。

Abstract: With the advent of large language models, research in automated software engineering has increasingly focused on leveraging these models to achieve a deeper semantic understanding of code or to engineer sophisticated agent-based processes. However, this research trajectory has largely overlooked a critical factor: the developers themselves. Programming is a deeply individualized activity; developers exhibit significant variation in their tool-chain preferences, domain-specific expertise, and problem-solving strategies. Consequently, the current paradigm of one-size-fits-all code intelligence systems struggles to accommodate the needs of individual developers. To address this gap, we introduce VirtualME, a novel IDE-embedded data infrastructure designed to model the developer by continuously capturing and interpreting their dynamic programming behaviors and preferences. VirtualME contains three components. (1) Log-level Behavior Extraction: it captures and extracts developers' log-level behaviors from IDE. (2) Task-level Behavior Recognition: it aggregates log-level behaviors into task-level behaviors via a multi-agent pipeline. (3) Developer-personality Measurement: it builds a rule engine to distill a four-dimensional developer persona: technology stack, ability, behavioral habits, and learning style. On top of VirtualME, we propose a solution for personalized repository-level knowledge Q&A by integrating the developer persona into the Q&A agent. We evaluated VirtualME by building a multi-repository benchmark with real-world developer trajectories, balancing correctness and personalization. Experimental results show that VirtualME-enhanced answers outperform generic baselines on five dimensions, yielding an average 33.80% improvement. Our results demonstrate that abundant, continuous developer-behavior data can pave the new way for adaptive and personalized code intelligence.

</details>


### [176] [Offloading Score: Measuring AI Reliance Through Counterfactual Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29392)
*Vishakh Padmakumar,Lujain Ibrahim,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Jennifer Wang,Q. Vera Liao,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"卸载分数"(Offloading Score)这一新指标，通过量化用户向AI工具卸载的认知努力比例来衡量对AI的依赖程度，而非传统的使用频率或自我报告。实验表明该指标能检测到时间压力下依赖程度的显著变化（+43%），而基线指标无法区分。同时展示了如何结合任务目标识别依赖是否适当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI依赖度量方法仅关注AI输出的采纳率或自我报告指标，未能捕捉用户与AI之间的任务努力分配。需要一种能够量化认知努力卸载程度的指标，以更准确地衡量对AI工具的依赖。

Method: 提出"卸载分数"(Offloading Score)指标：通过构建用户在没有AI工具情况下完成任务的"反事实工作流"估计，计算使用工具节省的步骤比例。通过内在有效性评估和40名开发者的受控用户研究进行验证，通过时间压力变化测试指标敏感性，并与使用率和自我报告基线指标对比。

Result: 卸载分数在时间受限设置下检测到显著更高的依赖（+43%，p=0.018），而基于使用率和自我报告的基线指标无法区分不同条件。更高依赖表现为更多子任务委托给工具和更直接复用AI输出。

Conclusion: 卸载分数作为度量AI依赖的工具具有双重贡献：用户可用其衡量和反思自身依赖行为；智能体设计者可利用该量化信号来减轻过度依赖。

Abstract: AI tools are increasingly integrated into real-world workflows. However, existing measures of reliance on these tools focus on AI output adoption or on self-reported indicators, rather than how task effort is distributed between users and tools. Here, we introduce offloading score, a measure of reliance that quantifies the fraction of cognitive effort offloaded to an AI tool. Offloading Score is simulation-based -- we construct a counterfactual workflow by estimating how the user would have completed the task without the tool, and then computing the fraction of steps saved by using the tool. We validate offloading score through intrinsic evaluations of metric validity, and a controlled user study ($n=40$) with developers performing programming tasks using AI tools. We vary time pressure to test whether reliance measures capture the known increase in reliance under time pressure. We show that offloading score detects significantly higher reliance in time-constrained settings ($+43\%$, $p=0.018$), while usage-based and self-reported baseline measures of reliance do not distinguish the conditions. We complement this with descriptive insights showing that higher reliance manifests as greater delegation of subtasks to the tool and more direct reuse of AI outputs. Finally, we demonstrate an approach of using offloading score in combination with target outcomes of a task (e.g., code understanding) to identify when reliance may be (in)appropriate. Our framework offers two contributions: an instrument users can apply to measure and reflect on their own reliance, and a quantitative signal that agent designers can utilize to mitigate overreliance.

</details>


### [177] [Usability Analysis of Configurator User Interfaces with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29456)
*Sebastian Lubos,Alexander Felfernig,Damian Garber,Adnan Kraljić,Tarik Kraljić,Viet-Man Le,Thi Ngoc Trang Tran,Gerhard Leitner,Julian Schwazer,Doris Suppan,Reinhard Willfort,Ivan Dukic,Jeremias Fuchs,Manuel Henrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索使用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对配置器用户界面进行可扩展的半自动化可用性分析，综合了18个配置器特定可用性标准，对16个真实配置器进行评估，结果表明MLLMs能可靠识别配置器可用性问题并提供改进建议，尽管仍需人工验证，但可显著降低分析工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置器的成功不仅依赖于技术正确性和性能，还取决于可用性，但目前缺乏配置器特定的可用性准则和系统化的UI分析工具支持。

Method: 从文献中综合出18个配置器特定可用性标准，利用多模态大语言模型对16个真实配置器逐条评估每个标准，生成严重性评级和可操作的改进建议。

Result: MLLMs能够可靠地识别配置器特定可用性问题，并提供领域感知的改进建议，尽管人工验证仍然必要。

Conclusion: 该方法虽仍需人工验证，但有望显著降低配置器可用性分析所需的工作量。

Abstract: Configuration is a key technology for tailoring complex software systems, services, and products. A successful application of configurators not only depends on technical correctness, performance, and domain modeling but also on their usability. While general usability heuristics are widely used, configurator-specific criteria and tool support for systematic user interface (UI) analysis are limited. This paper explores the use of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for scalable and semi-automated usability analysis of configurator UIs. We synthesize 18 configurator-specific usability criteria from the literature and apply these criteria in an MLLM-based analysis of 16 real-world configurators. Each criterion is assessed individually to generate severity ratings for usability issues and actionable improvement suggestions. A review of the results confirms that MLLMs can reliably identify configurator-specific usability issues and provide domain-aware improvement recommendations. Although human validation remains necessary, this approach has the potential to significantly reduce the required effort to analyze configurator usability.

</details>


### [178] [CODEFUSE-DEBENCH: An Empirical Study on Readability, Recompilability, and Functionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29490)
*Puzhuo Liu,Yuhan Huang,Jianlei Chi,Peng Di,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以可重用性为导向的二进制反编译评估范式DEBENCH，通过可读性、可重编译性和功能性三个维度对反编译器质量进行自动化评估。在5个主流反编译器和3个修复LLM上的实验揭示了四个关键发现：可重用性悬崖陡峭（最佳组合仅22.3%程序级行为重叠）、可读性与功能性不一致、跨反编译器差异（20倍）远大于跨LLM差异（1.6倍）、以及三类失败模式（语法噪声、类型系统崩溃、上游不可逆损失）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制反编译评估主要依赖语法相似性或单一维度的可读性指标，无法衡量实际的可重用性（即反编译结果能否被开发者直接使用）。作者旨在提出一个更贴近实际应用场景的多维度评估范式，弥补现有评估方法的不足。

Method: 构建DEBENCH框架：包含240个原子测试函数（8个源文件编译为640个二进制文件），采用LLM-as-judge结合URAF（18个子维度）进行可读性评分、固定50次迭代的编译-修复循环、以及基于Frida的差异化动态追踪（程序级、函数级、指令级）。评估5个反编译器（IDA Pro、Ghidra、RetDec、Hex-Rays、Angr）和3个修复LLM。

Result: 最佳反编译器-LLM组合达到22.3%的Exact+Partial程序级行为重叠，但仅1.2%精确stdout匹配；-O3优化下可读性最低但功能性最高；Clang可读性低于GCC但功能性高2.6倍；跨反编译器功能性差异达20倍，跨LLM仅1.6倍；约19%的修复错误源于类型系统崩溃。

Conclusion: 反编译器的实用性评估需要多维度视角；可读性不能替代功能性；反编译器引擎的改进比更大规模的修复模型更重要；类型系统和上游信息丢失是当前反编译的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Binary decompilation aims to recover binaries into high-level source code, but existing evaluations mainly rely on syntactic similarity or single-axis readability metrics, which fail to capture practical reusability. We propose a reusability-driven evaluation paradigm that measures decompiler quality along three orthogonal dimensions: readability, recompilability, and functionality. We present DEBENCH, the first automated framework for multidimensional decompilation evaluation. DEBENCH contains 240 atomic test functions, organized into 8 source files and compiled into 640 binaries. It combines LLM-as-judge readability scoring with URAF (18 sub-dimensions), iterative compile-and-repair under a fixed 50-iteration budget, and Frida-based differential dynamic tracing at the program, function, and instruction levels. We evaluate five mainstream decompilers and three repair LLMs. Our study reveals four findings. First, the reusability cliff is steep: the best decompiler-LLM pair reaches 22.3% Exact+Partial program-level behavioral overlap but only 1.2% exact stdout match, nearly 50 points below recompilability. Second, settings that maximize readability do not maximize functionality: -O3 yields the lowest readability but the highest functionality, and Clang gives lower readability than GCC but 2.6x higher functionality. Third, cross-decompiler variation at the functional level is 20x, far larger than the 1.6x cross-LLM variation, showing that progress depends more on decompiler engines than larger repair models. Fourth, failures fall into three categories: syntactic noise, type-system collapse (about 19% of repair errors), and irreversible upstream losses such as ARM64 relocation idioms and C++ ABI features.

</details>


### [179] [Inferring Code Correctness from Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29822)
*Tambon Florian,Papadakis Mike*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TRAILS是一种通过规范驱动的测试输入生成和输入-输出对验证来评估LLM生成代码正确性的方法，无需对代码本身进行推理，在MCC指标上比Zero-Shot CoT提升高达39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码缺乏可靠的正确性验证方法。现有方法要么依赖多候选代码的动态共识（成本高、难以扩展），要么依赖静态推理（易受动态错误和顺序偏差影响）。

Method: TRAILS首先基于规范通过类别划分生成多样化的测试输入，然后在候选代码上执行这些输入，并让LLM评估生成的输入-输出对是否符合规范——整个过程不推理代码本身。最后跨输入聚合评分以判断程序是否正确。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CoCoClaNeL两个数据集上，使用Qwen3Coder-30B、Devstral-Small-24B和Olmo3.1-Instruct三个LLM进行评估，TRAILS在MCC上比Zero-Shot CoT提升高达39%，持续优于HoarePrompt，且对LLM非确定性的敏感性更低，能正确标注更多独特的代码样本。

Conclusion: TRAILS通过将LLM推理锚定在具体的(输入, 输出)对上，提供了一种更稳定、更准确且更可扩展的LLM生成代码正确性验证方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become integral to modern software development, enabling automated code generation at scale. However, validating the correctness of LLM-generated code remains a critical and largely unsolved challenge. Existing approaches either rely on dynamic consensus across multiple code candidates - making them costly and difficult to scale - or on static reasoning that is susceptible to dynamic bugs and order bias. In this paper, we propose TRAILS~ (Targeted Reasoning Agreement via Inputs and Specifications), an approach that grounds LLM reasoning with concrete (input, output) pairs. TRAILS~ first generates diverse test inputs via category partitioning based on the specification, then executes them against the candidate code and prompts LLMs to assess whether the resulting input-output pairs conform to the specification - without ever reasoning over the code itself. Scores are aggregated across inputs, to determines whether the program is likely correct. We evaluate TRAILS~ on two datasets, LiveCodeBench and CoCoClaNeL, across three LLMs (Qwen3Coder-30B, Devstral-Small-24B, and Olmo3.1-Instruct), comparing against HoarePrompt and a Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought baseline. TRAILS~ improves Matthew Correlation Coefficient by up to 39\% relative to Zero-Shot COT and consistently outperforms HoarePrompt. Beyond accuracy, TRAILS~ demonstrates greater stability across seeded runs, reducing sensitivity to LLM non-determinism, and assigns correct labels to a larger set of unique code samples than competing approaches.

</details>


### [180] [TagDebt: A Bot to Support Technical Debt Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29869)
*João Paulo Biazotto,Daniel Feitosa,Paris Avgeriou,Elisa Yumi Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了 TagDebt，一个可集成到 GitHub 仓库中自动为 issue 标注自我承认技术债务（SATD）标签的机器人，通过技术接受模型（TAM）访谈研究表明其具有实用性和易用性，但团队规模、代码库大小等情境因素会影响其采用决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然技术债务（TD）管理已有多种方法和工具，但缺乏能轻松集成到现有开发工作流中的专门化技术债务管理（TDM）工具，导致 TD 难以被有效追踪和管理。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（DSR）方法设计并实现 TagDebt 机器人；通过技术接受模型（TAM）对 16 名从业者进行访谈，评估其有用性、易用性及影响使用的上下文因素（如团队规模、从业者角色）。

Result: 从业者普遍认为 TagDebt 在组织 issue 和减少手动工作方面有用，整体易用且文档清晰；团队规模和代码库大小等情境因素会影响采用决策；用户还提出了检查/更新源代码等改进建议。

Conclusion: TagDebt 是开发更专业化 TDM 工具的概念验证，能在不干扰现有工作流的情况下使 TD 可见，帮助从业者避免未管理 TD 的风险。

Abstract: Context: Technical debt (TD) is a widely studied metaphor that helps to explain how sub-optimal decisions that can harm software maintainability over time. Although incurring TD is not intrinsically bad, tracking and managing TD are crucial to avoid its negative effects. Hence, researchers and practitioners have proposed and developed diverse approaches and tools for managing TD. However, we are still lacking specialized tools for technical debt management (TDM), specifically ones that can be easily integrated into existing development workflows. Objective: We present and evaluate TagDebt, a bot that can be integrated within GitHub repositories and automatically assign labels to issues (i.e., SATD or non-SATD). TagDebt helps in the identification of TD (i.e., by looking for self-admitted technical debt (SATD)), leading to more efficient TDM. Methods: We carried out a Design Science Research study to design and implement TagDebt. For its evaluation, we executed a Technology Acceptance Model (TAM) study through interviews with 16 practitioners, to check the bot's usefulness, ease of use, and contextual factors that might impact the bot's usage (such as team size and practitioners' roles). Results: Overall, practitioners found that TagDebt is useful, especially for organizing issues and reducing manual work. Furthermore, they pointed out that the bot is overall easy to use, and its documentation is clear. The analysis also revealed that contextual factors, such as team and codebase size, impact the decision to adopt TagDebt. Finally, several improvements were suggested, such as including features to check and update the source code. Conclusion: TagDebt is a proof-of-concept for the development and usage of more specialized tools for TDM. It helps to make TD visible without disrupting existing workflows and help practitioners avoid the risks of unmanaged TD.

</details>


### [181] [Agora: Toward Autonomous Bug Detection in Production-Level Consensus Protocols with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29910)
*Xiang Liu,Sa Song,Zhaowei Zhang,Huiying Lan,Jason Zeng,Ming Wu,Michael Heinrich,Yong Sun,Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Agora是一个领域感知的多智能体框架，通过假设驱动测试和LLM协作，在四个主流共识协议实现中发现了15个未知的深层逻辑错误，而现有基于LLM的方法无法检测到任何此类协议级错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共识协议实现中的深层逻辑错误涉及跨多个执行阶段的复杂状态依赖行为，现有基于LLM的代码分析方法难以检测这类协议级错误。

Method: 提出Agora框架，采用领域感知的多智能体协作方式，智能体分别负责探索协议状态空间、利用领域特定约束合成攻击场景、通过迭代精化验证发现，实现显式角色分离以推理全局协议不变量。

Result: 在Raft、EPaxos、HotStuff、BullShark四个共识实现上，使用四种SOTA LLM，Agora发现了15个违反安全属性的未知协议级逻辑错误，而现有LLM智能体未能检测到任何此类错误。

Conclusion: 领域感知的多智能体协作对于检测复杂协议中的深层逻辑错误至关重要。

Abstract: Consensus protocols form the backbone of distributed systems and blockchains, where implementation bugs can cause data corruption and financial losses. While LLM-based approaches show promise in code analysis, they struggle with deep protocol-level logic bugs involving complex state-dependent behaviors across multiple execution stages. We present Agora, a domain-aware multi-agent framework that integrates hypothesis-driven testing with LLM capabilities for systematic protocol verification. Agora employs specialized agents that collaboratively explore protocol state spaces, synthesize attack scenarios using domain-specific constraints, and validate findings through iterative refinement. This explicit role separation enables reasoning about global protocol invariants beyond single-function code analysis. We evaluate Agora on four consensus implementations (Raft, EPaxos, HotStuff, BullShark) using four state-of-the-art LLMs. Agora discovers 15 previously unknown protocol-level logic bugs that violate safety properties, while existing LLM-based agents fail to detect any such protocol-level logic bugs. Our results demonstrate that domain-aware multi-agent collaboration is essential for detecting deep logic bugs in complex protocols.

</details>


### [182] [REPOT: Recoverable Program-of-Thought via Checkpoint Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30052)
*Parsa Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RePoT是一种基于确定性验证回放的一步恢复方法，在PoT失败时（约14%问题）仅需额外一次LLM调用，通过从已验证前缀恢复路径，在多个基准上提升3-20个百分点。关键发现是检查点信息而非具体前缀尾词才是恢复的核心信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Program-of-Thought（PoT）方法在生成Python程序时，单个无效动作会导致整个轨迹无效，缺乏容错机制。现有方法要么无恢复能力，要么需要多次重试，效率低下。作者希望设计一种低成本、确定性的恢复机制，在PoT失败时仅需一次额外LLM调用即可修正轨迹。

Method: RePoT包含两步：1) 确定性验证回放——将PoT生成的程序在环境中逐步执行，定位到第一个无效动作处，保留已验证前缀；2) 单次LLM调用——从已验证前缀的尾部恢复生成后续有效动作序列。此外还提出Adaptive RePoT，根据已验证前缀长度在后缀修复和全新PoT重试之间进行路由选择。

Result: 在PuzzleZoo-775上，RePoT在四个闭源模型配置上比PoT提升3-11个百分点，最高达96.9%（vs 86.3%）；与等预算PoT重试基线相比，在Gemini上显著胜出（+3.8pp），在GPT-medium和Claude上无显著差异，在GPT-mini上落后——呈现能力缩放模式。在PlanBench Blocksworld上提升1.1-11.4pp，在四个开源模型上三个提升3.3-20.0pp。在Derail-550上，有检查点信息的条件均达到30%+（GPT-medium）或70%+（Gemini），而仅错误反馈条件<=3.1%。

Conclusion: RePoT提供了一种低成本、确定性的PoT轨迹修复方案，仅需一次额外LLM调用。实验表明检查点信息（而非具体前缀尾词）是恢复的核心信号。能力缩放模式初步由Adaptive RePoT应对。该方法在多个基准和模型族上均有效，为规划-恢复范式提供了简洁可行的方案。

Abstract: One-shot Program-of-Thought (PoT) emits a Python program that prints a primitive-action plan; a single invalid action silently invalidates the trajectory. We introduce RePoT (Recoverable PoT): a deterministic verified replay that walks the plan through the environment to its first invalid transition, then one LLM call that resumes from the verified prefix. RePoT costs at most one extra LLM call on the ~14% of problems where PoT fails. RePoT beats PoT by +3 to +11pp across four closed-model configurations on PuzzleZoo-775 and peaks at 96.9% vs 86.3% on gpt-5.4-mini-medium; against the matched-budget PoT-retry baseline, RePoT wins decisively on Gemini (+3.8pp, 95% CI [+2.2,+5.4]), is within sampling noise on GPT-medium and Claude, and loses on GPT-mini -- a capability-scaling pattern we begin to address with Adaptive RePoT, a rule-based dispatcher that routes between suffix repair and a fresh PoT retry based on verified-prefix length (preliminary). We replicate on PlanBench Blocksworld (+1.1 to +11.4pp) and on four open-weights models (+3.3 to +20.0pp on three of four). On Derail-550, our controlled recovery benchmark, every condition with access to checkpoint information clears >=30% on GPT-medium and >=70% on Gemini, vs <=3.1% for error-only feedback -- showing that checkpoint information, not the specific verified-prefix tail, is the load-bearing recovery signal.

</details>
