### [183] [Automating Low-Risk Code Review at Meta: RADAR, Risk Calibration, and Review Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30208)
*Chris Adams,Arjun Singh Banga,Parveen Bansal,Souvik Bhattacharya,Rujin Cao,Pedro Canahuati,Nate Cook,Brian Ellis,Prabhakar Goyal,Gurinder Grewal,Tianyu He,Matt Labunka,Alex Manners,David Molnar,Ging Cee Ng,Vishal Parekh,Jiefu Pei,Frederic Sagnes,James Saindon,Will Shackleton,Sid Sidhu,Gursharan Singh,Karthik Chengayan Sridhar,Matt Steiner,Pratibha Udmalpet,Sean Xia,Stacey Yan,Audris Mockus,Peter Rigby,Nachiappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Meta 开发的 RADAR 系统采用风险感知的多级漏斗自动化代码审查流程，处理了 53.5 万+ diff，落地 33.1 万+，将审查时间缩短 330%，恢复率降至非 RADAR 的 1/3，生产事故率降至 1/50，有效缓解了 AI 辅助编码带来的审查瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助编码工具使代码产出大幅增长（人均 diff 量增长 51%，每 diff 代码量增长 105.9%），但及时审查率下降，代码供应与审查带宽之间的差距日益扩大，需要自动化解决方案来缓解审查瓶颈。

Method: 提出 RADAR (Risk Aware Diff Auto Review) 系统，采用多级漏斗架构：按作者身份和源码类型分类 → 资格门控 → 静态启发式规则 → 机器学习 Diff 风险评分 → LLM 自动代码审查 → 确定性验证，最终落地合格变更。通过 53.5 万+ diff 的遥测数据、政策变更的前后对比观察以及效率结果的倍差法分析进行评估。

Result: RADAR 审查了 53.5 万+ diff，落地了 33.1 万+。风险评分阈值从 25 分位放宽至 50 分位后，批准率提升至 60.31%。RADAR 审查 diff 的恢复率为非 RADAR 的 1/3，生产事故率为非 RADAR 的 1/50。中位关闭时间缩短 330%+，中位审查周期缩短 35%。

Conclusion: 风险感知的分层自动化能够在不牺牲生产安全的前提下，有效缓解 AI 驱动代码增长所带来的审查瓶颈。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools have altered software production. At Meta, significant lines of code per human-landed diff grew by 105.9% year over year and per-developer diff volume rose 51%, with agentic AI responsible for over 80% of that growth. Meanwhile, the share of diffs receiving timely review has declined, exposing a widening gap between code supply and reviewer bandwidth. We ask three questions that progress from feasibility through calibration to impact: (1) can risk-stratified automation operate at scale across diverse organizations, (2) how does tuning the risk threshold affect the trade-off between automation yield and safety, and (3) to what extent does automated review reduce end-to-end latency for AI-generated changes? We deployed RADAR (Risk Aware Diff Auto Review), a multi-stage funnel that classifies each diff by authorship and source type, applies eligibility gates, static heuristics, a machine-learned Diff Risk Score, LLM-based Automated Code Review, and deterministic validation before landing qualifying changes. We evaluate RADAR through telemetry covering 535K+ RADAR-reviewed diffs, observational before-after comparisons for policy changes, and difference-in-differences analysis of efficiency outcomes. RADAR has reviewed 535K+ diffs and landed 331K+. Relaxing the Diff Risk Score threshold from the 25th to the 50th percentile increased the approve rate to 60.31%. The revert rate for RADAR-reviewed diffs is 1/3 that of non-RADAR diffs, and the Production Incident rate is 1/50 that of non-RADAR diffs. RADAR reduces median time to close by over 330% and median diff review wall time by 35%. Risk-aware layered automation can materially reduce review bottlenecks created by AI-driven code growth without compromising production safety.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Unifying Semantic Path Order and Weighted Path Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29393)
*Teppei Saito,Nao Hirokawa*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了单调语义路径顺序和加权路径顺序的简单统一形式，将它们统一为归约顺序和归约对，并讨论了其作为基础全序归约顺序的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调语义路径顺序和加权路径顺序是证明项重写系统终止性的强大归约顺序，但两者缺乏统一的框架，本文旨在将它们统一起来。

Method: 提出一个简单统一的框架，将单调语义路径顺序和加权路径顺序统一为归约顺序和归约对，并讨论其作为基础全序归约顺序的用法。

Result: 实现了两种路径顺序的统一，并展示了其在基础全序归约顺序中的应用。

Conclusion: 该统一形式有效结合了两种路径顺序的优势，为项重写系统终止性证明提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: Monotonic semantic path orders and weighted path orders are powerful reduction orders for proving termination of term rewrite systems. In this paper we present their simple unification as reduction orders and reduction pairs. We also discuss the use of it as ground total reduction orders.

</details>


### [185] [Strong (D)QBF Dependency Schemes via Pure Paths with Applications to Proof Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29763)
*Leroy Chew,Tomáš Peitl*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了新的依赖方案Dpure，将其添加到DQRAT证明系统中，实现了与最强大的(D)QBF证明系统p等价，并开发了原型检查器DQRAT-check，同时验证了Dpure在求解器Qute中的集成潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的(D)QBF证明系统虽然证明复杂度上等价于独立扩展规则，但缺乏实际的证明检查器支持。DQRAT系统仅存在于理论层面，需要补充关键规则使其达到最强证明系统的能力，并实现可用的检查工具。

Method: 提出Dpure依赖方案作为缺失规则添加到DQRAT系统；实现原型检查器DQRAT-check；在依赖学习求解器Qute中集成Dpure并验证其性质。

Result: DQRAT+Dpure与Independent Extended QU-Res证明系统p等价；Dpure具有与先前依赖方案类似的性质；DQRAT-check原型检查器得以实现；Dpure可声学集成到Qute求解器。

Conclusion: Dpure是使DQRAT达到最强(D)QBF证明系统能力的关键规则，且具有实际可部署性，推动了(D)QBF认证从理论走向实践。

Abstract: Certification for Quantified Boolean Formulas (QBF) and Dependency Quantified Boolean Formulas (DQBF) is an ongoing challenge. Recent proof complexity work has shown that the majority of QBF and DQBF techniques can be p-simulated by using the independent extension rule.
  In propositional logic, extension rules are supported by proof checkers using a more general RAT (Resolution Asymmetric Tautology) rule. The next step in (D)QBF certification would be to update these modern RAT formats to match the strength of this independent extension rule.
  In this paper we first introduce a new dependency scheme called Dpure. This rule is the missing ingredient that when added to Blinkhorn's proof system DQRAT allows it to be provably p-equivalent to the Independent Extended QU-Res, the most powerful of the known QBF and DQBF proof systems. Up until now, DQRAT has only existed in theory, so we implement a prototype checker DQRAT-check which includes our extra rule. In addition to its inclusion in our proof checker we show Dpure has other properties that have been found for previous dependency schemes, and each of these observations has potential in solving/checking including the sound integration into the dependency learning solver Qute.

</details>


### [186] [A Rust-to-Lean Verification Pipeline with AI Provers: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30106)
*Natalia Klaus,Palina Tolmach,Juan Conejero*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个将生产级 Rust 密码学代码转换为 Lean 4 中机器验证正确性证明的流水线，结合符号提取工具、形式化密码学规范库和 AI 证明器，在以太坊基金会 zkEVM 验证项目中成功应用于 Plonky3 和 RISC Zero 的密码学原语，并报告了工程差距和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低 Rust 密码学代码形式化验证的准入门槛，并探索如何更有效地在验证流水线中使用 AI 证明器，以解决传统手工证明效率低下的问题。

Method: 构建三级流水线：(1) 符号提取工具（Charon/Aeneas 或 Hax）将 Rust 代码提升到 Lean 4；(2) 形式化密码学规范库（ArkLib/CompPoly）提供数学目标；(3) AI 证明器（Aristotle 和 Aleph）自动闭合证明义务。所有证明最终由 Lean 内核检查。

Result: 成功验证了 Plonky3 中的 FRI folding、Mersenne31 和 KoalaBear 域算术、Horner 多项式求值，以及 RISC Zero 的 Merkle 包含验证。Aleph 还完成了 Plonky3 中 compute_log_arity_for_round 两个之前标记为 sorry 的边界定理证明。报告了 AI 能闭合的证明义务类别和需要人工处理的部分。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够有效工作，同时指出工程差距包括 Lean 4 工具链版本漂移、Aeneas/Hax 提取限制、缺失引理、策略缺口和代码生成摩擦点，这些发现有助于未来改进和推广形式化验证。

Abstract: We describe a verification pipeline that takes production Rust cryptographic code and produces machine-checked correctness proofs in Lean 4. The pipeline combines three components: symbolic extraction tools (Charon and Aeneas, or Hax) that lift Rust into Lean 4; formal cryptographic specification libraries (ArkLib and CompPoly, from the Verified zkEVM project) that provide the mathematical targets; and AI provers (Aristotle from Harmonic AI and Aleph from Logical Intelligence) that close the resulting proof obligations. Every proof is checked by the Lean kernel, so AI output cannot compromise soundness.
  Within the scope of the Ethereum Foundation's zkEVM Verification Project, we applied the pipeline to cryptographic primitives in Plonky3 (FRI folding, Mersenne31 and KoalaBear field arithmetic, Horner polynomial evaluation) and RISC Zero (Merkle inclusion verification). In addition, Aleph authored proofs of two bounds-style theorems in Plonky3's compute_log_arity_for_round that previously stood as sorry.
  The paper describes the architecture, walks through a running example based on Aleph's two proofs, reports which classes of proof obligations AI closed and which required manual work, and discusses the engineering gaps we encountered: Lean 4 toolchain drift across tools and specific Aeneas/Hax extraction limits. We also document concrete missing lemmas, tactic gaps, and code-generation friction points discovered during proof development. We hope this contribution lowers the barrier to adoption of formal verification and facilitates more effective use of AI in this pipeline. The result is a working pipeline for formal verification of Rust, with kernel-checked proofs and reproducible artefacts.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Designing for the Moment: How One-Minute Interventions Fit or Falter Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29051)
*Zahra Hassanzadeh,Anne Hsu,Rachel Kornfield,David Haag,Ananya Bhattacharjee,Jay Olson,Jan David Smeddinck,Norman Farb,Alex Mariakakis,Lydia Chilton,Joseph Jay Williams*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索了无需引导或传感的一分钟数字干预设计空间，基于Fogg行为模型和四项设计原则，通过14天22人研究，发现共同创作式的有意个性化能在低摩擦下实现相关性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何设计无需复杂引导和传感设备的一分钟数字干预，使其能促使低动机人群立即采取行动，从而降低行为改变的门槛。

Method: 基于Fogg行为模型和四项文献设计原则，构建简易干预提示；在身体活动、健康饮食和心理健康三个领域，对22名参与者进行为期14天的研究，并通过参与者改写提示获得洞察。

Result: 研究发现，共同创作式的有意个性化（参与者与研究者协作改写提示）是一种轻量级的个性化机制，能在保持低摩擦的同时提高提示的相关性。

Conclusion: 通过共同作者方式进行有意个性化，可以有效平衡干预的相关性与用户摩擦成本，为无需复杂传感的即时数字干预提供了可行设计方向。

Abstract: This paper explores the design space for one-minute digital interventions that prompt immediate action without onboarding or sensing. By embracing Fogg's Behavior Model and four design principles informed by literature, the goal of these interventions was to provide triggers that encourage actions so simple that even people with low motivation would be willing to complete them. We examined the utility of these prompts by conducting a 14-day study with 22 participants interested in making small lifestyle improvements in at least one of three domains: physical activity, healthy eating, and mental well-being. When combined with insights drawn from participants' rewrites of our prompts, our findings suggest that intentional personalization through co-authorship could be a lightweight personalization mechanism that balances relevance with low friction.

</details>


### [188] ["It's OK Because...": The Wild West of Student Rationalization of AI Use in Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29090)
*Jiyoon Kim,Kentaro Toyama,Sangmi Kim,John M. Carroll*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于20份半结构化访谈、AI聊天日志和课程文档，研究发现学生对AI在学术写作中的使用存在至少五种概念化情境，使用超过20种不同的合理化理由来为AI使用辩护，且这些合理化既存在事后也存在即时建构，并不具有自我一致性。研究揭示了AI带来的道德滑坡问题，并讨论了教育设计和AI政策的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI不仅通过让学生将大量学术工作外包给AI来挑战学术诚信，还模糊了学生区分可接受的帮助与学术不端的道德界限。研究旨在探究学生如何在道德层面对学术写作中的AI使用进行意义建构。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈（n=20）、AI聊天日志和课程文档（包括教学大纲和提交的作业）作为数据来源，对学生进行质性研究。

Result: （1）存在至少五种不同的AI使用概念化情境，从教师预期的AI政策到学生实际的AI使用；（2）学生使用超过20种不同的合理化理由为AI使用辩护，如认为复制AI生成文本是无受害者的、AI文本反映自己信念或风格即属于自己写作、使用AI能学到更多等；（3）学生的合理化既有事后建构也有即时建构，且不一定自我一致。

Conclusion: 现代AI呈现出一个陡峭的道德滑坡，学生在概念上沿着这一滑坡下滑，最终远远超出了教师的教学目标和期望。研究为教育设计和AI政策制定提供了启示。

Abstract: Generative AI challenges academic integrity not only by enabling students to delegate substantial portions of their academic work, but also by blurring the ethical boundaries by which students distinguish acceptable assistance from misconduct. Drawing on semi-structured interviews (n=20), AI chat logs, and course documents (syllabi, submitted assignments), we investigated how students themselves make moral sense of AI use in academic writing. Our analysis results in a range of novel findings: First, there are at least five distinct sites of AI-use conceptualization, ranging from faculty's intended AI policy, to students' actual AI use. Second, students use over 20 distinct rationalizations to justify AI use, such as that copying AI-generated text is victimless; that any AI text reflecting their own beliefs or their own style is their own writing; or that they are learning more by using AI -- even extensively -- than otherwise. We present a taxonomy of these rationalizations, and show how some of them are employed to justify conscious violations of course policies. Third, student rationalizations occur in both an ad hoc and post hoc manner, and they are not necessarily self-consistent. These and other findings suggest that modern AI presents a steep, ethical, slippery slope which students conceptually slide down, landing far outside the pedagogical goals and expectations of instructors. We discuss implications for educational design and AI policy.

</details>


### [189] [Improving outdoor navigation for people with blindness using an AI-driven smartphone application and personalized audio guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29120)
*Raymond Liu,Patrick Slade*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Mobilio是一款智能手机应用，通过机器学习、传感器融合和个性化音频反馈，为视障人士提供路线导航、路径引导和避障功能。实验表明，与Google Maps+白杖相比，Mobilio+白杖使社区路径导航时间减少13±3%，环境接触减少41±5%，导航可靠性接近人类向导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球3.4亿视障人士缺乏满足全部户外导航需求（逐向导航、路径引导、避障）的可访问且经济的工具，现有方案（白杖、导盲犬、电子旅行辅助设备）存在功能缺失或成本高昂等问题。

Method: 通过112人需求调查确定导航标准；开发集成机器学习、传感器融合和个性化音频反馈的智能手机应用Mobilio；在代表性场景中进行传感器和模型可靠性工程测试；开展14名视障参与者实验，比较Mobilio+白杖与Google Maps+白杖在社区路径和障碍课程中的表现。

Result: Mobilio+白杖相比Google Maps+白杖：路径导航时间减少13±3%，环境接触减少41±5%；导航可靠性接近人类向导；参与者报告易用性高、感知工作量低、音频反馈直观。

Conclusion: Mobilio作为一种可访问且个性化的工具，有望成为有效的户外导航辅助设备，提高视障人士的独立性。

Abstract: Globally, 340 million people have blindness or moderate to severe visual impairment (BVI)$^1$ which limits independent outdoor navigation$^2$ and negatively affects their health and quality of life$^{3,4}$. We surveyed 112 people with BVI and found that an ideal outdoor navigation aid must be able to perform turn-by-turn directions, path guidance, and obstacle detection and avoidance. Existing navigation tools such as white canes, guide dogs, and electronic travel aids often lack one or more of these criteria and may be expensive or inaccessible$^{5,6}$. Here we introduce Mobilio, a smartphone application that incorporates machine learning, sensor fusion algorithms, and personalized audio feedback to meet all of the outdoor navigation criteria. The reliability of the smartphone sensors and models used for navigation were assessed with engineering tests in representative navigation scenarios. We performed a series of experiments where Mobilio personalized audio feedback for participants with BVI (n = 14), guided them along an outdoor community path, and helped them navigate an obstacle course. Participants walking with Mobilio and a white cane reduced time to navigate a community path by 13 $\pm$ 3% and environmental contacts by 41 $\pm$ 5% compared to using Google Maps and a white cane. Mobilio achieved similar outdoor navigation reliability as a human guide. Participant surveys reported that Mobilio was easy to use, had a low perceived workload, and provided intuitive audio feedback. This work provides an accessible and personalized tool that may be an effective outdoor navigation aid to increase independence for people with BVI.

</details>


### [190] [A Domain-Informed Multi-Objective Framework for EEG Channel Selection in Motor Imagery BCIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29943)
*Dekka Muni Kumar,Dhruba Jyoti Kalita,Yogesh Kumar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多目标优化（NSGA-II、MOPSO、MOEA/D）的EEG通道选择框架，通过平衡空间相关性（高斯核）与功能可区分性（任务相关去同步化），在四个MI-BCI数据集上取得优于传统方法的分类性能（87%、71%、75%、65%），实现了紧凑的通道配置和降低的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG通道选择方法存在依赖单一目标标准和易陷入局部最优的局限性，需要一种能同时平衡空间相关性和功能可区分性的多目标优化方法。

Method: 采用非支配排序遗传算法（NSGA-II）、多目标粒子群优化（MOPSO）和基于分解的多目标进化算法（MOEA/D）构建多目标优化框架，用高斯核衡量空间相关性，用试次内任务相关去同步化（ERD）评估功能可区分性。

Result: 在Physionet（87%）、OpenBMI（71%）、HighGamma（75%）和BCIIV-2A（65%）四个数据集上实现了分类性能，优于现有单目标和基于固定子集的方法，选出的通道集中在感觉运动皮层区域。

Conclusion: 该多目标优化框架能够提升MI-BCI性能，同时实现紧凑的通道配置和降低的计算复杂度，更适合可穿戴、便携和实时的BCI应用。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) classification using electroencephalography (EEG) signals is essential for advancing brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Traditional EEG channel selection methods often face limitations, such as dependency on single-objective criteria and susceptibility to local optima. To address these challenges, this work proposes a multi-objective optimisation framework that employs non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm, multiple-objective particle swarm optimisation, and a multi-objective evolutionary algorithm based on decomposition. Our approach effectively balances spatial relevance, using a Gaussian kernel, and functional discriminability, which assesses intratrial task-related desynchronisation, thereby improving performance. We evaluated this framework on four EEG datasets: Physionet, OpenBMI, HighGamma, and BCIIV-2A. The proposed approach successfully identifies compact, relevant channel subsets concentrated around sensorimotor cortex regions linked to MI activity, addressing the prevalent challenges of dimensionality and complexity inherent to traditional techniques. Furthermore, the framework achieved classification performance of 87%, 71%, 75%, and 65% on the Physionet, OpenBMI, HighGamma, and BCIIV-2A datasets, respectively. By outperforming existing single-objective and accuracy-based methods, and those relying on fixed subsets, these findings demonstrate that this new multi-objective optimisation framework can enhance MI-based BCI performance while facilitating compact channel configurations with reduced computational complexity, making them better suited for wearable, portable, and real-time BCI applications.

</details>


### [191] [Expecting Empathy: How Interaction Context Shapes Norms for Empathic Response in Digital Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29399)
*Tao Wang,Chi-Ching Juan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究利用 Reddit 三个建议社区的 28,239 条帖子-回复数据，发现"压力下决策支持"这种交互类型呈现出不对称的共情特征：共情水平接近情感倾诉，但工具性水平接近任务导向交流。行为共情占比最高（36.6%），且社区验证的回复质量与共情表达呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算中确定不同交互情境下适当的共情水平是一个核心挑战。已有研究区分了任务导向交流（共情需求近乎为零）和情感倾诉（共情需求高），但忽视了"压力下决策支持"这一中间类型。本文旨在识别并验证这一类型的独特共情特征。

Method: 从 Reddit 三个建议社区收集 28,239 条帖子-回复 dyads，将其分为三种交互类型。采用基于 LLM 的标注方法，结合基于模式的稳健性检验，对共情深度、共情形式和工具性比例进行评分。通过统计分析检验五个假设。

Result: 验证了不对称共情特征：压力下决策支持的回复共情水平显著高于任务导向交流（M=0.47 vs. 0.24, p<0.001），同时保持高工具性（0.83 vs. 情感倾诉的0.77, p<0.001）。行为共情占主导（36.6%），社区验证的回复质量与共情表达呈负相关（r=-0.075, p<0.001）。社区规范调节基线水平但保持结构顺序。

Conclusion: 研究确立了这一交互类型的人类共情基线，对校准情感 AI 系统中的共情表达具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: A central challenge in affective computing is determining appropriate empathy levels for different interaction contexts. Prior work has characterized two poles: task-focused interactions, where empathy demand is near zero, and emotional disclosure, where empathy demand is high. This paper identifies a distinct intermediate type, decision support under stress, in which a sender faces a consequential choice while experiencing emotional difficulty. We hypothesize that this type elicits an asymmetric empathy profile: empathy comparable to emotional disclosure but instrumentality comparable to task-focused exchange. We test five hypotheses using 28,239 post-reply dyads from three Reddit advice communities, classified into three interaction types and scored for empathy depth, empathy form, and instrumental proportion using LLM-based annotation with pattern-based robustness checks. Results confirm the predicted asymmetric profile: decision-support-under-stress replies show significantly higher empathy than task-focused replies (M = 0.47 vs. 0.24, p < 0.001) while maintaining high instrumentality (0.83 vs. 0.77 for emotional disclosure, p < 0.001). Behavioral empathy dominates (36.6%), and community-validated response quality is negatively associated with empathic expression (r = -0.075, p < 0.001). Community norms modulate baselines substantially but preserve the structural ordering. These findings establish a human empathy baseline for this interaction type and have direct implications for calibrating empathic expression in affective AI systems.

</details>


### [192] [Inform, Coach, Relate, Listen: Auditing LLM Caregiving Support Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29473)
*Drishti Goel,Agam Goyal,Veda Duddu,Olivia Pal,Jeongah Lee,Qiuyue Joy Zhong,Violeta J. Rodriguez,Daniel S. Brown,Dong Whi Yoo,Ravi Karkar,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大模型被部署于非正式照护场景时，其安全表现是否会随其所扮演的支持角色（如信息提供、教练、共情、倾听）而变化。通过对5000条真实阿尔茨海默病相关社区查询和多个模型进行实验，发现模型的支持角色系统性地影响了交互风险的种类与分布，并且人类评估显示，更具信息导向的角色虽被认为更有帮助，但伴随更高的交互风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估多在通用提示下测试模型行为，未考察模型的安全表现是否随其支持角色的变化而改变。在非正式照护这一高风险、关系复杂的场景中，这一问题尤为关键。

Method: 基于社会支持理论，将支持角色操作化为四种（Inform、Coach、Relate、Listen）及两个基线条件，在三个语言模型上对5000条真实ADRD社区查询进行评估，并开展人类评估研究。

Result: 模型的支持角色系统性地影响了交互风险的流行率与组成；更具信息导向的（directive, information-oriented）角色虽被认为更可信、更有帮助，却表现出更高的交互风险。

Conclusion: 存在感知质量与安全之间的张力：信息导向角色虽受用户信赖，但风险更高，提示在部署LLM支持系统时需要权衡帮助性与安全性。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly being deployed for conversational support in informal caregiving contexts, where interactions often extend beyond information-seeking: caregivers seek emotional reassurance, guidance, and help, while navigating uncertain, relationally complex care decisions. Yet most safety evaluations assess model behavior under generic prompts, leaving a critical question unexamined: does a model's safety profile change with its support role? We study this by operationalizing four expert-reviewed support roles grounded in social support theory: Inform, Coach, Relate, and Listen, and comparing them against two baseline controls: a basic prompting condition and a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) condition. We evaluate across three language models (GPT-4o-mini, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and MedGemma-1.5-4b-it) on 5,000 real-world queries from online Alzheimer's Disease and Related Dementias (ADRD) communities. We find that the LLM's support role systematically shapes both the prevalence and composition of interactional risks. Furthermore, a human evaluation study reveals a perceived quality--safety tension: more directive, information-oriented roles are rated as more helpful and trustworthy despite exhibiting elevated interactional risk profiles. We release ~90,000 support role-conditioned model responses with risk annotations as an ecologically grounded resource for research on safer LLM-mediated conversational support.

</details>


### [193] [Understanding the Rising Human-AI Affective Bonding: Conceptualization and HAABI Scale Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29484)
*Lu Chen,Xiaoran Xue,Rongqi Ding,Fenghua Tang,Anji Zhou,Chenxi Wang,Mengyu Miranda Gao,Zhuo Rachel Han*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发和验证了人机情感联结量表（HAABI），通过两项研究（52人访谈+673人问卷调查）确定了四个维度（情感真实性、分离焦虑、情感投入、浪漫亲密感），共20个项目，具有良好的信效度，为研究人与对话式AI之间的情感联结提供了中性、以用户为中心的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具多改编自人际依恋框架或聚焦于特定关系结果，缺乏专门评估人机情感联结的测量工具。本研究旨在开发一个从人机交互自身特性出发、中立的用户中心化量表。

Method: 研究1：对52名情感投入的对话式AI用户进行半结构化访谈，采用主题分析识别认知、情感和行为层面的联结特征。研究2：将主题分析结果转化为自评量表，对673名中文对话式AI用户进行验证，使用探索性和验证性因子分析确定因子结构。

Result: 最终确定20题、四因子结构：情感真实性、分离焦虑、情感投入、浪漫亲密感。量表具有良好的信度、结构效度和已知群体效度。

Conclusion: HAABI提供了一个有效、可靠的工具，可用于研究用户与对话式AI之间情感联结的形成机制、体验特征及其与用户心理结果的关系。

Abstract: As conversational AI becomes capable of sustained, affectively responsive interaction, users may form bonds beyond instrumental use. Existing measures often adapt interpersonal frameworks or focus on specific relational outcomes, leaving limited tools for assessing human-AI affective bonding on its own terms. Across two studies, we developed and validated the Human-AI Affective Bonding Inventory (HAABI). Study 1 used thematic analysis of semi-structured interviews with 52 emotionally engaged conversational AI users to identify cognitive, emotional, and behavioral features of bonding. Study 2 translated these insights into a self-report inventory and validated it among 673 Chinese conversational AI users. Exploratory and confirmatory factor analyses supported a 20-item, four-factor structure: emotional realism, separation anxiety, emotional investment, and romantic intimacy. The HAABI showed good reliability, construct validity, and known-groups validity. The scale therefore provides a neutral, user-centered tool for studying how affective bonds with conversational AI are formed, experienced, and related to users' psychological outcomes.

</details>


### [194] [From Prompts to Context: An Ontology-Driven Framework for Human-Generative AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29675)
*Ngoc Luyen Le,Marie-Hélène Abel,Bertrand Laforge*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于本体的框架"From Prompts to Context"，通过核心组件CCAI（上下文协作AI本体）将生成式AI与人类的协作过程（任务、角色、资源、约束）建模为机器可读的结构化轨迹，替代传统的不透明提示-响应交互，并通过软件开发案例验证其能够提升任务上下文透明度、贡献可追溯性以及人机协作的可问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI协作中，短提示开始、不透明输出结束，缺乏对参与者、任务、资源和约束的显式上下文建模，导致信任、可追溯性和问责性不足，尤其是在信息密集型工作流（搜索、查询、档案管理）中。

Method: 提出本体驱动框架，核心是Contextual Collaboration AI Ontology (CCAI)，对任务、智能体角色、资源、约束等协作关键要素进行建模，作为共享机器可解释词汇表；结合填充后的CCAI实例与基于SPARQL的上下文检索，将瞬时提示-响应交互转化为可查询的结构化协作轨迹。

Result: 通过软件开发团队构建能力本位教育功能的案例（覆盖需求分析、设计、实现、测试），表明显式协作建模能使任务上下文更明确，提升AI生成贡献的可追溯性，并支持更透明、可问责的人机协作实践。

Conclusion: 未来人机生成式AI系统的设计应不仅关注输出质量，还需显式表示产出所在的协作上下文，并据此提出设计原则。

Abstract: Collaborations with Generative AI often begin with a short prompt and end with an opaque output, leaving implicit who was involved, what task was being pursued, which resources were used, and which constraints should have shaped the process. This limited contextual explicitness hinders trust, traceability, and accountability, particularly when Generative AI is embedded in information-intensive workflows such as search, querying, and profile management. This paper introduces From Prompts to Context, an ontology-driven framework for representing Human-Generative AI collaboration. Its core component, the Contextual Collaboration AI Ontology (CCAI), models key elements of collaboration - including tasks, agent roles, resources, and constraints - as a shared machine-interpretable vocabulary. By combining populated CCAI instances with SPARQL-based context retrieval in operational workflows, the framework turns otherwise ephemeral prompt-response interactions into structured and queryable collaboration traces linking prompts, outputs, and their surrounding context. The approach is illustrated through a case study involving a software development team building a competency-based education feature for viewing and updating learner competency profiles. The case study shows how the framework can support the representation and documentation of collaboration episodes across requirements analysis, design, implementation, and testing. Within this setting, the results indicate that explicit collaboration modelling helps make task context more explicit, improves the traceability of AI-generated contributions, and supports more transparent and accountable Human-Generative AI practices. We conclude by outlining design principles for future Human-Generative AI systems that emphasise not only output quality, but also the explicit representation of the collaborative context in which outputs are produced.

</details>


### [195] [Embodied Virtual Reality Feedback Reshapes Neural Representations to Support Continuous Three-Dimensional Motor Imagery Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29677)
*Niall McShane,Attila Korik,Karl McCreadie,Naomi Du Bois,Darryl Charles,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了具身虚拟现实（VR）反馈对运动想象驱动的连续脑机接口（BCI）的影响。在10名参与者、10个纵向session中，CNN-LSTM解码器在VR条件下达到了r=0.762的想象运动相关性，显著优于屏幕反馈（r=0.672）。VR在所有评估策略和运动维度上均表现出8.9-13.0%的提升（p≤0.002, d=1.42-2.05），即使没有重新训练也保持优势。神经生理学分析显示VR增强了感觉运动-顶叶去同步化、运动-额叶功能连接以及前脑岛和前顶叶的参与，与真实运动执行的模式相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈模态和纵向训练如何影响神经表征和解码性能仍不清楚，特别是具身VR反馈在连续BCI中的系统性研究尚属空白。

Method: 采用10名参与者的纵向实验设计（10个session），通过三种策略评估性能：FDG（固定解码器泛化）、SAT（序列自适应训练）和WSR（session内上限估计）。使用CNN-LSTM解码器，比较VR与屏幕反馈条件下的3D虚拟手臂运动想象解码性能。

Result: VR反馈在所有评估策略和运动维度上均显著优于屏幕反馈（8.9-13.0%提升，p≤0.002, d=1.42-2.05），且优势在无重新训练时仍保持。神经生理上，VR增强了感觉运动-顶叶去同步化、运动-额叶功能连接、前脑岛参与及上顶叶耦合。

Conclusion: 具身空间反馈是下一代面向直觉运动控制和神经康复的连续BCI的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Continuous brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) that decode motion trajectories from imagined movement offer intuitive motor control, yet how feedback modality and longitudinal training shape neural representations and decoding performance remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic investigation of embodied virtual reality (VR) feedback during real-time 3D virtual limb control driven by motor imagery, across ten longitudinal sessions in ten participants. Performance was evaluated using three strategies: actual online performance (Fixed Decoder Generalisation, FDG), periodic retraining (Sequential Adaptive Training, SAT), and within-session upper-bound estimation (Within-Session Reconstruction, WSR). A CNN-LSTM decoder achieved within-session imagined movement correlations of r = 0.762 under VR and r = 0.672 under screen feedback. VR significantly outperformed screen feedback across all strategies and movement dimensions (improvements of 8.9-13.0%, all p <= 0.002, d = 1.42-2.05). This advantage persisted under fixed decoders without retraining, demonstrating that embodied VR feedback elicits inherently more decodable and generalisable neural representations. Linear mixed-effects modelling confirmed robust main effects of feedback modality and movement axis with no interaction. Neurophysiologically, VR produced stronger sensorimotor-parietal desynchronisation and enhanced motor-frontal functional connectivity, with pervasive anterior insula engagement across all frequency bands and increased superior parietal lobule coupling, paralleling patterns associated with real movement execution. These findings establish embodied spatial feedback as a key design principle for next-generation continuous BCIs targeting intuitive motor control and neurorehabilitation.

</details>


### [196] [Label Over Logic? How Source Cues Bias Human Fallacy Judgments More Than LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29928)
*Mahjabin Nahar,Nafis Irtiza Tripto,Aiping Xiong,Ting-Hao `Kenneth' Huang,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 人类评估者对内容来源标签（如AI或人类撰写）存在显著偏见，更信任标为人类或人类辅助AI的内容，即使其中包含逻辑谬误；而LLM的评估则相对不受来源标签影响，表明来源标签偏差主要存在于人类推理中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI生成内容泛滥背景下，来源标签对人类和LLM推理判断的偏见影响是否存在差异，以及LLM是否能提供更不受来源影响的评估。

Method: 在线实验（N=505），设置不同来源条件（人类、AI、人类辅助AI、AI辅助人类、不披露），让参与者评估包含逻辑谬误的评论；并将相同条件应用于多个LLM（GPT-5.2, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.5）进行比较。

Result: 人类评估者对标注为人类或人类辅助AI写的内容显著更宽容（更高信任度和评分），即使包含逻辑谬误；LLM评估在各来源条件下相对稳定，但模型间存在差异；人类和LLM的置信度在各条件下均较高。

Conclusion: 来源标签偏差在推理评估中主要是人类的脆弱性，LLM可提供更来源无关的评估，凸显了人-LLM协作在AI中介环境中的潜力。

Abstract: As AI-generated and AI-assisted content floods online spaces, source labels attached to such content can distort human reasoning judgments, with downstream consequences for moderation, evaluation, and decision-making. Whether LLMs share this vulnerability, or offer more source-agnostic evaluation, remains an open question with direct implications for human-AI collaboration. We examine this issue using logical fallacies as a controlled setting to isolate source-label effects on reasoning quality, independent of domain knowledge. We conduct an online study (N=505) where participants are assigned to a source condition (human, AI, human with AI assistance, AI with human assistance, or no disclosure) and evaluate comments containing logical fallacies, comparing their judgments with those of LLMs (GPT-5.2, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.5), who were evaluated across the same source conditions. Human evaluators were significantly more susceptible to fallacies labeled as written by human or human with AI assistance and assigned higher trust and evaluation ratings in these conditions. LLM evaluations remained comparatively stable across source labels, though performance varied across models. Confidence levels were similarly high across conditions for both humans and LLMs, regardless of fallacy presence. Our findings indicate that source-label bias in reasoning evaluation is primarily a human vulnerability and highlight the potential of human-LLM collaboration in increasingly AI-mediated environments.

</details>


### [197] [LLUMI: Improving LLM Writing Assistance for Mental Health Support with Online Community Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30273)
*Jiwon Kim,Maya Ajit,Sherry Gong,Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Dong Whi Yoo,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出LLUMI框架，利用Reddit社区偏好信号（点赞/踩）和人类评估，通过SFT和DPO训练开源小模型，在无需GPT等闭源模型的情况下，实现同等质量的隐私保护型心理健康支持回复生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在心理健康对话中提升有用性、共情力和安全性需要大量计算、专家输入和标注数据；同时，部署专有云模型处理敏感心理健康数据存在严重的隐私和数据治理风险。因此，需要一种能在受保护环境内部署、兼具高质量和隐私保护的心理健康支持方案。

Method: 构建LLUMI双组件框架：(1) 生成模型(GM)起草回复；(2) 改进模型(IM)修订人工草稿。利用Reddit心理健康社区的点赞/踩模式构建"选择-拒绝"回复对，进行SFT和DPO训练，并在可读性、共情、连接感、可操作性和安全性五个维度进行人类评估对齐。

Result: 尽管基于较小的开源模型而非GPT等专有云模型，LLUMI在语言分析和人类评估中均达到与闭源大模型相当的性能水平。

Conclusion: 通过社区偏好信号训练的开源模型，能够在保证高质量心理健康支持的同时，为敏感场景提供更具隐私保护性的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise in generating supportive responses for mental health queries, but improving their usefulness, empathy, and safety often requires substantial compute, expert input, and labeled data. At the same time, deploying proprietary, cloud-based models for mental health-related interactions raises important privacy and data-governance concerns, given the sensitivities. To address this challenge, we introduce LLUMI setup that can be hosted in-house within protected environments. LLUMI consists of two complementary components: a generation model (GM), which drafts supportive responses to mental health queries, and an improvement model (IM), which revises an initial human-crafted response. We leverage feedback signals from Reddit mental health communities, using community endorsement patterns such as upvotes and downvotes to construct chosen-rejected response pairs for Supervised Fine Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). We further align LLUMI using human evaluation across five dimensions: readability, empathy, connection, actionability, and safety. Our results show that, despite relying on smaller open-source models rather than proprietary cloud-based GPT models, LLUMI achieves comparable performance across linguistic analyses and human evaluations. These findings suggest that open-source models, when trained with community-derived preference signals, can support high-quality mental health support assistance while offering a more privacy-preserving alternative for sensitive support contexts.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [The incremental voter model: mean-field analysis and convergence to equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28984)
*Fei Cao,Xiaoqian Gong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出增量投票者模型（IVM），一个离散意见多智能体系统，其中智能体在随机选择的说服者影响下逐步改变意见。通过推导平均场极限下的非线性常微分方程组，建立了研究意见分布渐近行为的数学框架，有助于理解社会影响过程和意见极化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的投票者模型通常假设意见可以瞬间跳变，缺乏对意见逐步演化的建模。本文旨在引入一种更符合现实的渐进式意见更新机制，以更准确地模拟社会影响过程中的意见极化现象。

Method: 采用离散意见状态空间{-k,...,0,...,k}，智能体每次更新时意见最多改变1个单位。通过随机选择说服者并基于其意见进行逐步更新，推导出描述大群体极限行为的平均场非线性常微分方程组。

Result: 建立了严格的平均场极限数学框架，得到了控制意见分布渐近行为的非线性ODE系统，分析了群体规模趋于无穷时意见分布的极限行为。

Conclusion: 该模型为理解复杂系统中的社会影响过程提供了理论工具，特别有助于建模意见极化现象，并为未来更高级模型的构建提供了指导。

Abstract: We introduce the incremental voter model (IVM), a discrete-opinion multi-agent system where agents undergo step-wise transitions biased by the opinion of a randomly selected persuader. Our incremental voter model comprises a large population of interacting agents, each holding an opinion represented by an element of the discrete set $\{-k,\ldots,0,\ldots,k\}, k \in \mathbb{N}_{+}$. At each update step as time progresses, a pair of distinct agents are selected independently and uniformly at random from the population, and the first agent (viewed as the ``listener'') updates its opinion based on that of the second (viewed as the ``persuader''), adopting a new opinion that differs from its current one by at most one unit. By deriving the mean-field system of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) that governs the large-population limit of the agent-based model, we develop a rigorous mathematical framework to study the asymptotic behavior of the opinion distribution in the mean-field limit. These results contribute to a deeper understanding of social influence processes in complex systems, particularly in modeling opinion polarization, and may guide the formulation of more advanced models in future research.

</details>


### [199] [LLM-ALSO: LLM-Driven Adaptive Learning-Signal Optimization for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29293)
*Xiaoguang Wu,Zhi Zheng,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出LLM-ALSO框架，利用LLM进行迭代式自适应奖励塑形，通过诊断-提案-验证循环来优化稀疏奖励下的多智能体强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励场景下多智能体强化学习缺乏有效训练引导，现有方法需大量领域知识，而基于LLM的方法多为单智能体、一次性设计，缺乏对MARL动态训练过程的适应性验证。

Method: 设计Critic LLM诊断阶段性的学习与协调失败，Generator LLM基于诊断生成候选奖励塑形配置，并通过分支验证反馈在影响主训练轨迹前对候选方案进行细化筛选。

Result: 在稀疏奖励协作MARL任务上，LLM-ALSO提升了稀疏评估性能和学习效率。

Conclusion: 迭代式LLM驱动的自适应学习信号优化框架能有效降低不可靠LLM生成修改的风险，提升MARL在稀疏奖励下的训练效果。

Abstract: Effective training-time guidance is central to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), yet remains difficult in sparse-reward settings where weak supervision limits coordination and policy improvement, and existing methods often require substantial domain expertise or manual design effort. Large language models (LLMs) provide a promising alternative for flexible learning-signal design, yet existing LLM-based methods remain largely single-agent-oriented, one-shot, or weakly validated for the evolving training dynamics of cooperative MARL. To address these limitations, we propose LLM-ALSO, an iterative LLM-driven adaptive learning-signal optimization framework for MARL. Rather than directly deploying LLM-generated rewards, LLM-ALSO decomposes adaptation into iterative diagnosis, proposal, and validation: a Critic LLM diagnoses stage-specific learning and coordination failures from sparse-return metrics and compact behavior evidence, a Generator LLM proposes candidate reward-shaping configurations conditioned on the diagnosis, and branch-validation feedback refines candidates before they affect the main training trajectory. Through short-horizon validation and stage-aware adaptation, LLM-ALSO promotes only validated updates into training, reducing the risk of unreliable LLM-generated modifications. Experiments on sparse-reward cooperative MARL tasks show that LLM-ALSO improves sparse-evaluation performance and learning efficiency.

</details>


### [200] [DynaGraph: Lightweight Multi-Model Interaction Framework via Dynamic Topological Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29511)
*Yanxing Guo,Zihao Zheng,Fangzhou Wu,Ling Liang,Lin Bao,Zongwei Wang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DynaGraph提出了一种轻量级多模型框架，通过动态拓扑重构在单张消费级GPU上运行，将任务分解为可动态调整的子模块，采用分层自愈机制（细粒度补丁和子图重构）解决静态拓扑的级联错误和动态架构的轨迹发散问题。在StrategyQA、MATH和FinQA上，8B模型接近72B单体的推理能力，同时相比无约束动态架构延迟降低68.1%、token消耗减少68.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决复杂推理任务的方法存在严重计算冗余（大规模单体LLM）或关键困境（静态拓扑易级联错误、无约束动态架构轨迹发散与内存膨胀），亟需一种既能保持推理质量又能显著降低计算开销的轻量级方案。

Method: DynaGraph框架包含两层核心机制：(1) 执行层：在共享基础模型上复用时分PEFT适配器，实现单张消费级GPU上的全系统训练与推理部署；(2) 路由层：Evaluator持续监控执行置信度，触发分层自愈——细粒度补丁（Fine-grained Patching）修复局部数据缺口，子图重构（Subgraph Reconstruction）应对严重逻辑断裂。

Result: 在StrategyQA、MATH和FinQA上，8B模型性能接近72B单体模型（StrategyQA 87.6%、MATH 82.7%）；相比无约束动态架构，延迟降低68.1%，token消耗减少68.6%。

Conclusion: DynaGraph通过动态拓扑重构和分层自愈机制，成功在单GPU上实现了接近大模型推理能力的小模型方案，大幅降低了计算开销和延迟，为高效推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tackling complex reasoning tasks typically relies on massive monolithic LLMs, which suffer from severe computational redundancy. While task decomposition through structured pipelines or multi-agent collaborations offers an alternative, these approaches inevitably fall into a critical dilemma: predefined static topologies are highly vulnerable to cascading errors, whereas unconstrained dynamic agents suffer from trajectory divergence and unpredictable memory bloat. To address this, we present DynaGraph, a lightweight multi-model framework driven by dynamic topological reconfiguration. At the execution level, DynaGraph multiplexes time-division PEFT adapters over a shared base model, enabling both full system training and inference deployment on a single consumer-grade GPU. At the routing level, the Evaluator continuously monitors execution confidence to trigger hierarchical self-healing: Fine-grained Patching for localized data gaps and Subgraph Reconstruction for severe logical ruptures. Experiments on StrategyQA, MATH, and FinQA demonstrate our 8B model closely approximates the reasoning capabilities of a 72B monolithic model (e.g., 87.6% on StrategyQA, 82.7% on MATH). Furthermore, it reduces latency by up to 68.1% and token consumption by 68.6% compared to unconstrained dynamic architectures.

</details>


### [201] [Evolve as a Team: Collaborative Self-Evolution for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29790)
*Zhezheng Hao,Tianfu Wang,Huanshuo Dong,Ziyan Liu,Hong Wang,Xiankun Lin,Qiang Lin,Can Wang,Hande Dong,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Team，一种基于协作自我进化的经验驱动型多智能体系统进化框架，通过保留执行上下文和后任务通信实现多尺度自我进化，在六个长周期基准测试中优于单智能体系统、手工MAS和现有MAS进化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在真实任务中常出现执行失败，且这类失败难以在设计阶段消除，因此需要一种能够根据自身执行经验进行自我进化的机制，但MAS的执行经验复杂冗长，交织多个智能体的执行链和通信消息，难以识别改进点。

Method: Meta-Team框架保留每个智能体的执行上下文并协调任务后通信，使智能体能够交换分布式证据以指导进化；在此基础上进行多尺度自我进化，将执行经验转化为智能体行为、智能体间协作和团队级组织三个层面的可复用改进。

Result: 在六个长周期智能体基准测试中，Meta-Team一致优于单智能体系统、手工设计的MAS以及先前的MAS进化方法；进一步分析表明Meta-Team实现了更可靠和可扩展的MAS自我进化。

Conclusion: Meta-Team通过协作自我进化的方式有效解决了MAS经验驱动的进化难题，能够从复杂的执行经验中提取可复用的改进，显著提升了系统的可靠性和可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as an effective paradigm for complex and long-horizon tasks. However, in real-world tasks, MAS often exhibit various failures during execution and such failures are difficult to eliminate during design. This motivates experience-driven MAS evolution, where a system improves based on its own execution experience. Yet such evolution is challenging because MAS experience is prolonged and intricate, interleaving multiple agents' execution chains and communication messages, which makes it difficult to identify what should be improved. To address this challenge, we propose Meta-Team, an experience-driven MAS evolution framework based on collaborative self-evolution. Meta-Team preserves the execution context of each agent and coordinates post-task communication, enabling agents to exchange distributed evidence for evolution. Building on this design, Meta-Team conducts multi-scale self-evolution, transforming execution experience into reusable improvements to agent behaviors, inter-agent coordination, and team-level organization. Across six long-horizon agent benchmarks, Meta-Team consistently outperforms single-agent systems, hand-crafted MAS, and prior MAS evolution methods; further analyses demonstrate that Meta-Team enables more reliable and scalable MAS self-evolution.

</details>


### [202] [Evolutionary Dynamics of Cooperation in Next-Generation LLM Agent Systems: A Cross-Provider Empirical Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29874)
*Francisco León Zúñiga Bolívar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究将Willis等人建立的演化博弈论基准扩展到2025-2026年发布的四个前沿LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.4 Mini），发现合作偏差在多数模型-prompt组合中持续存在，但提供商差异是均衡结果的最强预测因子，而非模型代际，其中Gemini 2.5 Flash在偏态条件下可达77%的对抗均衡，而GPT-5.4 Mini在Self-Refine下可达70%的合作均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究新一代LLM智能体是否继承了前代模型的合作偏差，以及模型规模和提供商多样性如何在竞争性多智能体环境中改变均衡行为。

Method: 采用与Willis等人相同的实验协议，使用重复囚徒困境（IPD）作为博弈环境，对四个2025-2026年发布的模型在三种提示风格（Default、Prose、Self-Refine）和四种群体组成（平衡与偏态，有噪声与无噪声）下进行测试，每个条件进行500次Moran迭代。

Result: 合作偏差在多数模型-prompt组合中持续存在；提供商间差异显著（Gemini 2.5 Flash达77%对抗均衡，GPT-5.4 Mini达70%合作均衡）；Self-Refine提高了所有模型的内部合作差异（ICD），Claude Sonnet 4.6 Refine达最高0.913；噪声敏感性平均约6-13个百分点，但跨研究差异在统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 提供商身份而非模型代际是均衡结果的最强相关因素；噪声仍然是所有模型面临的普遍挑战，不论模型规模或发布时间。

Abstract: Do next-generation LLM agents inherit the cooperative biases documented in their predecessors, or does scale and provider diversity reshape equilibrium behaviour in competitive multi-agent settings? Willis et al. established a benchmark for this question using evolutionary game theory and the Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD), finding consistent cooperative biases in ChatGPT-4o and Claude 3.5 Sonnet. We extend this benchmark to four frontier models released in 2025-2026 - Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.4 Mini - applying the identical protocol across three prompting styles (Default, Prose, Self-Refine) and four population compositions (balanced and biased, with and without noise). Cooperative bias persists across providers (H1): nine of twelve model-prompt combinations favour cooperative equilibria in balanced noiseless conditions. Cross-provider divergence is substantial (H3): Gemini 2.5 Flash reaches up to 77% aggressive equilibria under biased conditions, while GPT-5.4 Mini reaches 70% cooperative equilibria under Self-Refine. Support for aggressive capability parity is partial (H2): Self-Refine raises ICD in all models and Claude Sonnet 4.6 Refine achieves the highest ICD in the dataset (0.913), but Default and Prose prompts show no systematic narrowing. Evidence on noise robustness is directionally positive but not robustly confirmed (H4): with n=500 Moran iterations per condition, average noise sensitivity is approximately 6 percentage points for Claude Sonnet 4.6 versus 13 pp for Claude 3.5 Sonnet, but this cross-study gap is not statistically significant once the predecessor's unreported sampling error is propagated. Provider identity, rather than model generation, is the strongest correlate of equilibrium outcomes; noise remains a universal challenge regardless of model size or vintage.

</details>


### [203] [Discovering Cooperative Pipelines: Autoresearch for Sequential Social Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30003)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双层自动研究框架：外层AI研究员（$\mathcal{R}$）自主重新设计内层LLM策略合成系统，用于多智能体序列社会困境（SSD）。在Cleanup和Gathering两个游戏中，该研究员在两种策略合成LLM和两种福利目标下，均超越手工设计基线，降低运行方差，且仅在最大化（Rawlsian）目标下自动注入公平性机制，体现了目标驱动的信息设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM策略合成系统在处理多智能体社会困境时，依赖于人工设计的管道（pipeline），缺乏自适应性和目标导向的优化能力。作者希望探索AI研究者能否自主重新设计这些管道，并根据不同的社会福利目标（功利主义效率vs.罗尔斯最大化）自动调整策略，从而超越手工基线和仅提示优化的方法。

Method: 采用双层自动研究框架：外层研究员$\mathcal{R}$以编码智能体运行，读取内层管道源代码，编辑系统提示、反馈函数、辅助库和迭代逻辑，运行评估并决定保留哪些更改。将$\mathcal{R}$应用于两个SSD游戏（Cleanup和Gathering），使用两种策略合成LLM，针对两种福利目标（功利主义效率和罗尔斯最大化）进行实验。

Result: 研究员$\mathcal{R}$在两种游戏、两种策略合成LLM和两种福利目标下，均可靠地超越了手工设计基线，显著降低了运行间方差，并优于仅提示优化方法。发现自动设计的管道具有目标依赖性：仅在最大化目标下，研究员才会向合成管道注入显式公平性机制，这类机制在目标无关的系统提示和所有效率优化管道中均不存在。

Conclusion: 该工作支持一种信息设计解读：研究员根据福利目标选择向有限理性的策略合成器透露什么信息。这展示了自动研究范式在多智能体合作问题中的有效性，揭示了目标函数对管道设计选择的因果影响。

Abstract: We study two-level autoresearch for cooperation: an outer-loop AI agent autonomously redesigns the inner-loop pipeline of an LLM policy-synthesis system for multi-agent Sequential Social Dilemmas (SSDs). A researcher agent $\mathcal{R}$ (run as a coding agent) reads the inner-loop source code, edits system prompts, feedback functions, helper libraries, and iteration logic, runs evaluations, and decides what to keep, following the autoresearch paradigm. Across two games (Cleanup and Gathering), two policy-synthesizer LLMs, and two welfare objectives (utilitarian efficiency and Rawlsian maximin), the researcher reliably exceeds hand-designed baselines, sharply tightens run-to-run variance, and outperforms prompt-only optimization. The discovered pipelines are objective-dependent: only under maximin does the researcher inject an explicit fairness mechanism into synthesizer pipelines, a class of mechanism that is absent from its own objective-agnostic system prompt and from every efficiency-optimized pipeline. This supports an information-design reading in which the researcher chooses what to reveal to the boundedly rational synthesizer as a function of the welfare objective. Code at https://github.com/vicgalle/autoresearch-social-dilemmas.

</details>


### [204] [When Cloud Agents Meet Device Agents: Lessons from Hybrid Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30102)
*Corrado Rainone,Davide Belli,Bence Major,Arash Behboodi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了混合多智能体系统（结合云端大模型和端侧小模型）的设计空间，揭示了任务准确性、货币成本和边缘能耗之间的复杂权衡。研究发现，虽然小模型能从大模型辅助中受益，但最优架构高度依赖于具体任务，且更大的前沿算力并不总能带来更好的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合多智能体系统的设计缺乏系统性原则，组件选择往往是针对特定领域的临时决策，缺乏对任务精度、成本和能耗三者耦合关系的深入理解。

Method: 适配两种代表性多智能体系统架构以支持混合推理，系统性地研究不同设计选择如何在功耗、成本和性能的帕累托前沿上移动工作点。

Result: 混合MAS的设计存在细微差别：小模型能有效利用大模型辅助，但最优架构高度任务依赖，且更大规模的前沿计算并不总带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 混合多智能体系统的设计不存在通用最优解，需根据具体任务在准确性、成本和能耗之间进行针对性权衡。

Abstract: The design space of agentic AI inference spans two extremes: frontier large language models (LLMs), typically hosted in the cloud and offering strong performance across a wide range of tasks at substantially high cost, and more cost-efficient small language models (SLMs), which are amenable to on-device inference. Hybrid multi-agent systems (MASs) combining on-device and cloud models offer a promising middle ground, but they also introduce a complex and poorly understood design space in which task accuracy, monetary cost, and edge energy consumption are tightly coupled; in the absence of general design principles, hybrid components, although not the most prevalent choice, are typically introduced through ad hoc decisions tailored to specific domains. In this work, we examine this design space more systematically. We adapt two representative MAS architectures to support hybrid inference and study how individual design choices shift the operating point along the Pareto frontier of power, cost, and performance. Our findings paint a nuanced picture of hybrid MAS design: while SLMs can effectively benefit from LLM assistance, the optimal architecture is highly task-dependent, and greater frontier-level compute does not consistently translate to better performance.

</details>


### [205] [EASE Configuration Facilitates A Reproducible Science of LLM Social Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30258)
*Sneheel Sarangi,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Aurélien Bück-Kaeffer,Zachary Yang,Jean-François Godbout,Reihaneh Rabbany*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了EASE（环境-智能体-模拟引擎-评估指标）模块化架构，并基于此开发了开源硅社会沙箱SiliSocS，通过三个案例研究展示了该框架在高配置性、可复现的LLM多智能体社会模拟中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社会模拟器多为临时性、单体式架构，缺乏标准化设计，导致研究不可复现且下游评估复杂，亟需建立系统化的多智能体模拟科学体系。

Method: 将核心组件模块化为环境(Environments)、智能体(Agents)、模拟引擎(Simulation engines)、评估指标(Evaluation metrics)四个模块（EASE架构），并将其封装在实验研究模式中，用于组织围绕特定研究问题的工作流；基于此实现开源系统SiliSocS。

Result: 通过三个案例研究分别展示了SiliSocS对现有问题的综合评估能力、对复杂问题的深入探究能力以及对已有研究的细化和扩展能力，揭示了当前建模方法的局限性并分离了设计选择对关键结果的影响。

Conclusion: EASE架构和SiliSocS系统为基于LLM的社会模拟提供了标准化、可配置、可复现的研究基础设施，有助于推动该领域的严谨科学发展。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed to simulate social interactions, yet many of the existing simulators remain ad hoc and monolithic. This lack of architectural standardization prevents reproducible research and complicates downstream evaluation. We advance a rigorous science of LLM-based multi-agent simulation by modularizing core components into Environments, Agents, Simulation engines, and Evaluation metrics (EASE). We demonstrate the utility of EASE configuration by wrapping it in an experimental study schema for orchestrating workflows centered around answering explicit research questions in generated scenarios. We contribute SiliSocS, an open-source, research-ready Silicon Society Sandbox implementing a study-structured EASE configuration to enable highly configurable and reproducible LLM-based social simulations. Using SiliSocS and EASE, we present three case studies, showcasing the system's comprehensive assessment of existing questions, ability to dive deeper into complex questions, and elaboration of existing studies, respectively. Together, these case studies highlight the limitations of current modeling approaches and isolate the impacts of design choices on key results.

</details>


### [206] [SpecBench: Evaluating Specification-Level Reasoning for Software Engineering LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30314)
*Grant Hamblin,Kevin Song,Zhanda Zhu,Anand Jayarajan,Sihang Liu,Nandita Vijaykumar,Gennady Pekhimenko*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpecBench，一个评估软件工程智能体在规范设计阶段推理能力的基准，填补了现有基准只关注代码生成的空白。SpecBench 基于 RFC 流程，要求智能体识别初始设计提案中的遗漏、歧义、不一致和错误假设，最佳模型 GPT-5.4 仅达到 44.4% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SWE 基准（如 SWE-Bench）聚焦于实现阶段，假设规格说明完整正确，忽略了真实世界中规格说明常存在缺陷、需经专家审查修订的关键阶段。为填补这一空白，本文提出评估智能体在规范层面推理能力的基准。

Method: 基于成熟开源项目的 RFC 流程构建 SpecBench：为每个任务提供初始设计提案、项目代码库和历史 RFC 讨论，要求智能体识别提案中的遗漏、歧义、不一致和错误假设，并与专家维护者在历史 RFC 审查中提出的批评进行对比评估。数据集涵盖 Kubernetes、React、Rust、TVM 和 vLLM 五个仓库。

Result: 评估了当前最先进的 SWE 智能体，最佳模型 GPT-5.4 在 SpecBench 上仅达到 44.4% 准确率，表明现有智能体在无需执行反馈的系统设计推理方面能力有限。

Conclusion: 现有 SWE 智能体在规范设计阶段的推理能力明显不足，SpecBench 填补了对这一关键能力评估的空白，为该方向的进一步研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents are transitioning from code generation to full software development lifecycle automation. A critical phase in this lifecycle is specification design: transforming initial proposals into carefully considered requirements through expert review. Existing benchmarks such as SWE-Bench are implementation-focused by measuring the agent's ability to generate code given fixed, precise design requirements. This formulation assumes specifications are correct and complete. In real-world complex and critical software systems, initial specifications are often incomplete and flawed, requiring extensive expert reviews and revisions before being accepted for implementation. To fill this gap, we introduce SpecBench to evaluate specification-level reasoning: the ability to generate complete, unambiguous, consistent, and correct system specifications. SpecBench tasks are derived from the Request for Comments (RFC) process used by mature open-source projects. For each task, an agent is given an initial design proposal, the project codebase, and all past project RFC discussions. The agent is tasked with identifying specification deficiencies: omissions, ambiguities, inconsistencies, or incorrect assumptions in the initial proposal. We evaluate predictions against critiques raised by expert maintainers during historical RFC reviews. SpecBench contains tasks from 5 diverse repositories: Kubernetes, React, Rust, TVM, and vLLM. We evaluate state-of-the-art SWE agents on SpecBench, analyzing their capacity to reason about system design without execution feedback. The best performing agent, GPT-5.4, achieves 44.4% accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的AC-MPC变体，在不牺牲控制性能的前提下显著降低了训练和推理延迟，在无人机竞速任务中实现了最先进的圈速和近极限动态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC-MPC虽然集成了MPC与强化学习以实现复杂动力系统的高性能控制，但其可微MPC层需要在前向和反向传播中反复求解优化问题，导致训练和推理延迟过高，成为实际部署的瓶颈。

Method: 提出CUDA加速的AC-MPC变体，通过GPU并行计算加速可微MPC层的前向和反向传播过程，从而减少端到端执行时间。

Result: 在敏捷无人机竞速任务中，该方法实现了最先进的圈速和近极限动态行为，同时训练和推理时间显著减少。

Conclusion: CUDA加速有效解决了AC-MPC的可微MPC层计算瓶颈问题，在保持控制性能的同时大幅降低了计算延迟，为复杂动态系统的实时控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [208] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“动态对称性”概念，通过测量机器人可达到的质心加速度均匀性（动态各向同性），在超过1000种模拟形态中发现高动态对称性持续提升轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效。开发了Argus系列球形机器人，其中20腿实体变体实现接近极端的动态各向同性，展示了与方向无关的运动、在杂乱和可变形地形上的敏捷移动、快速自稳定以及对部分执行器故障的恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人设计中的对称性主要局限于几何形态，而未被应用于动态驱动能力层面。本文旨在探索将对称性理念从形态扩展到动力学层面，为机器人提供统一的敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性设计原则。

Method: 提出动态对称性概念并构建动态各向同性度量；通过1000+模拟形态进行系统性评估；开发Argus系列球形机器人（采用径向线性致动器直接塑造质心动力学），构建物理20腿原型进行实验验证。

Result: 高动态对称性在模拟中一致改善轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效；20腿Argus原型实现接近极端的动态各向同性，展示了方向无关运动、杂乱/可变形地形敏捷移动、快速自稳定、部分执行器故障恢复能力，以及分布式感知支持的全向感知和连续运动中的物体交互。

Conclusion: 在机器人设计中不仅追求形态对称性，更追求可达动力学的对称性，为在不确定的陆地与地外环境中实现敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性提供了强大且通用的设计路径。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [209] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo提出利用单臂机器人示教与人类协作数据，通过数据增强生成合成双臂机器人示教数据，从而训练双臂操作策略，在零样本和少样本微调场景下均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协调操作对现实任务至关重要，但双臂机器人和数据集的稀缺限制了双臂策略的学习。单臂机器人在实验室中广泛可用，因此希望探索能否利用单臂机器人数据来训练双臂策略。

Method: MonoDuo通过遥操作单臂机器人执行双臂任务的一侧，同时人类协作执行另一侧，然后交换角色覆盖两侧；利用手部姿态估计、图像和点云分割及修补技术，将RGB-D观测数据增强为针对目标双臂机器人的合成示教数据，用于训练双臂策略。

Result: 在箱子搬运、背包打包、布料折叠、夹克拉链和盘子交接五个任务上，MonoDuo实现了对未见过的双臂机器人配置的零样本部署，成功率最高达70%；仅用25个目标机器人示教数据进行少样本微调，成功率达到从零训练的65-70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo证明了从单臂机器人数据高效迁移知识到双臂机器人策略的有效性，为利用广泛可用的单臂机器人克服双臂数据稀缺问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [210] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相位条件化、力感知的鲁棒可变形物体操作框架。通过FiLM调节的ACT编码器根据任务阶段调制特征提取，结合多模态相位预测器（视觉、力、位姿）实时估计相位并检测接触失败，配合混合阻抗控制器和触觉遥操作接口，在双机械臂挂/脱T恤任务中，自主错误恢复将成功率从56%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时依赖马尔可夫假设，导致视觉相似但需要矛盾动作的观测出现状态混叠，且无法自主从执行失败中恢复，限制了可变形物体操作的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出闭环分层架构：(1) FiLM调节的ACT编码器根据当前任务相位调制特征提取，实现单策略下的相位特定行为共享；(2) 融合视觉、力、位姿的多模态相位预测器实时估计相位，检测视觉不可见的接触失败并触发恢复轨迹；(3) 混合阻抗控制器实现柔顺执行；(4) 触觉遥操作接口用于力感知数据采集。

Result: 消融实验表明FiLM调制显著优于未调节和token级调节基线。t-SNE分析显示FiLM诱导了分离良好的相位特定特征表示。在双机械臂挂/脱T恤任务中，闭环系统通过自主错误恢复将挂T恤成功率从56%提高到87%。

Conclusion: 相位条件化和力感知信息的引入能有效解决变形体操作中的状态混叠和执行失败恢复问题，为可变形物体操作提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [211] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双无人机空中对接平台，采用主从编队和被动磁锁紧模块化框架，通过进度感知任务管理器实现四阶段对接（接近、对准、捕获、稳定），并在仿真和真实实验中验证了对接成功率、时间等指标，为模块化空中协作提供了可重复的基准测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机之间的可靠空中对接是实现模块化空中协作和操作的关键，但受到推力、载荷限制以及高精度相对位姿控制需求的挑战，现有平台缺乏可重复性和统计可比的评估手段。

Method: 采用主从编队的两架四旋翼无人机，搭载带有被动磁锁紧的轻量化模块化框架；设计进度感知的任务管理器管理接近、对准、捕获、稳定四个阶段；集成完整软硬件栈（ROS 2、Crazyflie/PX4接口）和同步日志记录。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，通过编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接时间及失败模式统计等指标对平台进行了定量评估。

Conclusion: 该平台支持对对接监督与同步策略进行统计可比的比较，为模块化空中协作和可重复的空中对接操作提供了实用的测试床。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [212] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA是一种即插即用的框架，通过多视角一致性约束、实例感知模块和自监督3D编码分支，在无需额外标注的情况下为预训练VLA模型注入3D场景理解能力，显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解能力，表现为：弱3D空间位置提取且缺乏多视角一致性约束、3D实例理解不足、遮挡下推理脆弱。直接集成成熟3D感知方法存在架构不兼容和依赖昂贵实例级标注的问题。

Method: (1) 通过空间条件几何聚合方法实现跨模态的多视角一致性3D特征编码；(2) 利用高级实例token的实例估计模块实现3D实例感知；(3) 掩码自监督3D编码分支通过保留预测器进行视觉token补全以处理遮挡。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0基准上，集成多个VLA基线后，操作性能取得一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 3DVLA作为即插即用框架，有效验证了在不丢弃VLM先验知识且无需额外人工标注的情况下，增强VLA模型3D推理能力的可行性和兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [213] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA是一种即插即用的相位自适应推理框架，通过动态计算调度加速VLA模型，在感知-语言推理阶段引入五级计算模式，在动作生成阶段引入三级去噪模式，实现最高3.77倍加速，并将控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率低，难以满足实时机器人操控需求；现有加速方法往往优化单一组件或采用固定加速规则，忽略了机器人连续控制中不同控制步骤的非均匀推理需求。

Method: 提出ElegantVLA框架，包含一个轻量级调度器，通过观察时序表示相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进度，联合分配视觉编码器、LLM和动作头的计算资源。感知-语言推理采用五级Vision-LLM计算模式（从完全重计算到多步时序复用），动作生成采用三级去噪模式（稳定运动时复用中间状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整优化）。

Result: 在GR00T和CogACT上分别实现2.55倍和3.77倍加速；在六项真实世界GR00T任务中，计算量降低2.18倍，控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA作为一种通用的加速框架，无需修改或重新训练基础模型，通过动态计算调度有效应对了VLA模型在实时机器人操控中的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [214] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种人在环路的强化学习框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏技术，让视觉教师将知识传递给仅依赖位姿、扭转和力感的学生策略，在真实世界NIST装配基准板上实现约50分钟训练后95%的成功率，并泛化到8个未见任务变体，无需领域随机化或数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉策略容易过拟合训练时的视觉条件，限制了鲁棒性和可迁移性；现有方法依赖领域随机化或数据增强，难以在真实世界中高效部署。

Method: 采用人在环路RL框架，训练一个基于视觉的教师策略，再通过蒸馏将知识传递给一个仅依赖位姿、扭转和力传感器信息的无视觉学生策略，全程在真实世界训练，无需领域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在NIST装配基准板的3个代表性任务上，约50分钟训练后总体成功率达95%；在8个未见任务变体上实现鲁棒泛化；通过蒸馏微调在最难任务上达到完全成功。

Conclusion: 所提方法在鲁棒性和适应性上优于基线策略，证明了视觉知识蒸馏到无视觉策略的有效性，兼顾了快速训练与强任务泛化能力。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [215] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解释的计算框架，利用多感官触觉数据建模人类材料感知与识别。该框架包含三个互联模型：将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性（模型1）、基于感知表征进行材料分类（模型2）、直接从触觉特征分类材料（模型3）。结果表明，结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互能提升预测精度，热感线索对感知建模和材料分类尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对材料的触觉感知依赖复杂的多感官触觉线索，但低层次触觉信号与感知表征之间的关系尚不清晰，这阻碍了触觉在数字环境中的集成以及具有类人触觉感知能力的机器人开发。

Method: 提出一个包含三个互联模型的可解释计算框架：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性；模型2基于这些感知表征进行材料分类；模型3直接从触觉特征分类材料。利用了按压、静态接触和滑动三种交互模式，并系统评估了热感、顺应性等多模态触觉线索的作用。

Result: 结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互信息可提升预测精度；热感线索对感知建模和材料分类均尤为重要；热感和顺应性线索在当前的机器人手指和触觉显示中代表性不足。

Conclusion: 强调热感和顺应性线索对类人触觉感知的关键作用，指出将这些线索融入人工系统可提升其近似人类材料感知的能力，并为设计更具感知基础的触觉界面提供指导。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [216] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-Seg是一个针对越野场景语义分割的鲁棒性框架，通过风格扩展和纹理正则化来应对分布偏移（包括域差异和传感器噪声），在多种分布偏移的目标域上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境中的语义分割面临源-目标域差异和传感器退化导致的分布偏移问题，这会使训练条件与部署条件不一致，导致语义标签预测不准确，进而引发导航任务失败。

Method: 提出ST-Seg框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 风格扩展（SE），通过生成多样化逼真风格来扩增源域有限的风格信息，拓展域覆盖范围；2) 纹理正则化（TR），通过深度纹理流形稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表征。

Result: 在多种分布偏移的目标域上的实验表明，ST-Seg显著优于现有方法，具有实质性的性能提升。

Conclusion: ST-Seg增强了语义分割在越野导航中的实际应用鲁棒性，验证了风格扩展和纹理正则化对于应对分布偏移的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [217] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLAConf框架，利用冻结的VLA模型内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头部单次前向传播直接估计逐步骤异常分数，实现高效且跨架构通用的置信度估计，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人实验中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型置信度估计方法（基于集成范式或动作令牌概率）面临计算效率低和跨架构泛化性差的问题，需要重复采样且仅适用于离散动作空间，难以推广到连续动作空间。

Method: 提出VLAConf框架：利用冻结的预训练VLA内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头部在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数；同时采用步骤条件化建模对操控轨迹中的 rollout 阶段信息进行编码。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上显著提升后验校准中置信度信号的质量，推理效率大幅超越现有基线；在真实机器人实验中进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf为VLA模型提供了一种高效、通用且可扩展的置信度估计方案，适用于风险敏感决策和故障预测。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [218] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhAIL（Physical AI Leaderboard）基准，用时间-成功累积分布函数（CDF）替代传统二元成功率评估VLA策略，引入人类相对吞吐量（HRT）和KS检验，在Franka FR3机器人上以更少试验次数实现更可靠的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略评估依赖N≤25次 rollout 的固定超时二元成功率，缺乏置信区间和统计比较，小样本量难以可靠区分性能相近的策略。

Method: 提出分布评估方法：以时间-成功CDF为评估原语，将任务分为评分（人类相对吞吐量HRT，带bootstrap置信区间）和显著性检验（Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验，按对象计算后宏平均）。

Result: 在四种公开VLA上，宏平均KS检验在N≤30 rollout时成功分辨GR00T vs. ACT和OpenPI vs. ACT两组接近比较，而二元阈值指标无法做到；OpenPI vs. GR00T未能分辨。最优VLA比人类参考慢约7倍（RMST比率）。

Conclusion: 基于CDF的分布评估方法比传统二元成功率更高效，能在较小样本量下实现更可靠的VLA策略比较，建议社区采用该评估标准。
